### [1] [Quantitative Gaussian limits of periodic O(N) models at and below the critical temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14570)
*Weiqi Jiang,Pengcheng Hou,Zhiyi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明了零磁场最近邻O(N)模型中局部自旋坐标的体积一致高斯逼近，适用于d≥3、周期盒子（边长M）上、在球面模型临界温度及其以下的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究O(N)模型局部坐标在热力学极限下的分布逼近问题，旨在给出关于体积M和自旋维数N的定量高斯误差估计，明确误差随p、N、M的精确渐近行为。

Method: 利用有限体积平均球面模型及其谱移s_M，结合1-Wasserstein距离度量，证明局部自旋坐标向目标的逼近。目标为各坐标场相互独立的、各向异性缩放的高斯自由场，并在低于临界温度时叠加独立的空间常数高斯项。通过考察最近邻星形窗口与一般固定窗口，并利用周期性无穷体积聚点态，得到不同维数（d≥3及d≥5）下的最优误差界。

Result: 在固定空间窗口上，前p≤N个坐标的未归一化1-Wasserstein误差至多为C√p(N^{-1/2}+s_M)。在含最近邻星的窗口上，误差当且仅当p=o(N)且√p s_M→0时趋于零。对任意周期无穷体积聚点态，任意非空固定窗口上误差当且仅当p=o(N)时趋于零。d≥5且温度超过临界值至少一固定正量时，界改进为C(p/N+√p M^{-d})。对固定p，含星窗口的精确阶为N^{-1}+M^{-d}，周期聚点态下任意非空窗口为N^{-1}。

Conclusion: 本文建立了O(N)模型局部坐标高斯逼近的定量体积一致误差界，并刻画了误差随p、N、M的精确渐近阶，揭示了临界及以上温度、不同维数下的最优逼近行为。

Abstract: We prove a volume-uniform Gaussian approximation for local spin coordinates in the zero-field nearest-neighbour $O(N)$ model on periodic boxes of side $M$ in $d\ge3$, at and below the critical temperature of the limiting spherical model. On a fixed spatial window, for sufficiently large $M,N$, the unnormalized $1$-Wasserstein error for the first $p\le N$ coordinates is at most $C\sqrt p (N^{-1/2}+s_M)$, where $s_M\to0$ is the spectral shift of the finite-volume mean spherical model. The estimate is uniform on compact ranges of positive directional couplings and inverse temperatures. The target has independent coordinate fields, each a scaled anisotropic Gaussian free field with an independent spatially constant Gaussian term below criticality.
  On a window containing a nearest-neighbour star (a site and its $d$ positive-direction neighbours), the error tends to zero as $M,N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$ and $\sqrt p s_M\to0$. For any choice of periodic infinite-volume accumulation states, the error on any nonempty fixed window tends to zero as $N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$. In $d\ge5$, when the inverse temperature exceeds its critical value by at least a fixed positive amount, the bound improves to $C(p/N+\sqrt p M^{-d})$. For fixed $p$, the sharp order is $N^{-1}+M^{-d}$ on star-containing windows and $N^{-1}$ on every nonempty fixed window in periodic accumulation states.

</details>


### [2] [Rational layers in the low temperature expansion of the three-dimensional Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14577)
*Ran Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在零场简单立方格点Ising模型中，按"超过规定两个横向跨度所需最小壁面积的多余量"对液滴分层，证明了每个固定多余层的生成函数是有理二元生成函数，分母因子仅为1-x、1-y和1-x-y；并完整拼装出多余量为0到3的全部层，其已知几何成分可归因于早前的多联骨牌计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的低温展开按壁面积给Ising液滴排序，但相同阶次下会出现不同横向跨度的液滴，导致计数混杂难以区分结构。作者希望按横向跨度与多余壁面积重新组织，以获得具有精确代数结构的分层信息。

Method: 将重复的坐标切割压缩为有界装饰（bounded decorations），把每个无界区间指派给一条单调链（monotone chain），再由一个有限控制器强制生成诱导接触与任意覆盖色的重用，从而证明每个固定多余层具有分母因子限于1-x、1-y、1-x-y的有理二元生成函数。随后显式拼装多余量为0到3的全部宽度层的完整结构，并将其已知几何成分对应到早先的多联骨牌（polyomino）枚举，最后用精确格点检验和经认证的低温比较验证公式与混合精度。

Result: 证明了每个固定多余层（含全部对数覆盖修正与Euler反演）都具有分母因子仅含1-x、1-y、1-x-y的有理二元生成函数；完整拼装出多余量为0到3的所有宽度层，其几何成分可归因于早前的多联骨牌枚举；精确格点检验证实了公式；经认证的低温比较显示在某采样点有适度的混合精度提升，但评估成本更高。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了精确的分层结构，为按横向跨度与多余壁面积组织的Ising液滴提供了严格的代数刻画；但对无界多余量求和仍是一个独立的问题，尚未解决。

Abstract: A low-temperature expansion orders Ising droplets by wall area, but droplets with different transverse spans can occur at the same order. We instead organize the zero-field simple-cubic model by the excess above the minimum wall area compatible with two prescribed spans. We prove that every fixed excess layer, including all logarithmic cover corrections and Euler inversion, has a rational bivariate generating function with denominator factors restricted to 1 - x, 1 - y and 1 - x - y. The proof compresses repeated coordinate cuts into bounded decorations and assigns each unbounded interval to one monotone chain. A finite controller then enforces induced contacts and arbitrary cover-color reuse. We explicitly assemble the complete layers of excess zero through three over all widths, attributing their known geometric constituents to earlier polyomino enumeration. Exact lattice checks corroborate the formulas. A certified low-temperature comparison shows a modest hybrid accuracy gain at one sampling point and higher evaluation cost. The result establishes an exact layer structure; summation over unbounded excess remains a separate problem.

</details>


### [3] [Hidden universality of rare fluctuations and a central-limit structure at criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14621)
*Nikolaos G. Fytas,Víctor Martín-Mayor,Attilio L. Stella,Gianluca Teza,Alexandros Vasilopoulos,David Yllanes*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文首次在二维伊辛普适类中提供大偏差渐近律的直接数值证据，表明临界涨落的远尾在归一化后坍缩到单一普适律，并指向临界涨落的广义中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心极限定理依赖弱相关假设，在临界点因长程关联而失效；临界涨落的稀有（大偏差）行为因概率极小、超出传统数值方法能力而长期未被探索。作者希望回答：临界点处是否存在一般的极限定理？并寻找最近理论预言的普适大偏差渐近律的直接数值证据。

Method: 采用大规模系绳蒙特卡洛（Tethered Monte Carlo）模拟，探测二维伊辛普适类中序参量涨落，概率低至 10^{-200}；将涨落按其特征宽度归一化后考察远端尾部的坍缩行为，并由共形场论推导出精确的普适振幅猜想结果进行对比。

Result: 归一化后的远尾坍缩到单一渐近律，且该律被同一普适类中不同微观模型共享；数值提取的普适振幅与由共形场论推导的猜想精确结果相容。

Conclusion: 临界涨落的稀有事件服从普适的渐近大偏差律，其幅度具有普适性，表明临界涨落存在一个广义的中心极限定理。

Abstract: The central limit theorem is among the most fundamental principles in the natural sciences, governing the fluctuations of sums of weakly correlated random variables through Gaussian statistics. At critical points, however, long-range correlations invalidate this paradigm, raising a fundamental question: does any general limit theorem survive at criticality? Although scale invariance has long been recognised as the defining feature of typical critical fluctuations, the behaviour of rare fluctuations has remained largely unexplored, as their extraordinarily small probabilities place them beyond the reach of conventional numerical methods. Recent theoretical advances suggest that universal asymptotic laws {may govern large deviations in systems at criticality}, yet direct numerical evidence has been lacking. Here we provide the first such evidence in the two-dimensional Ising universality class. Using large-scale Tethered Monte Carlo simulations, we probe order-parameter fluctuations with probabilities as small as $10^{-200}$ and show that, once normalised by their characteristic width, the far tails collapse onto a single asymptotic law shared by distinct microscopic models in the same universality class. This collapse is governed by a universal amplitude, whose numerical extraction is compatible with the conjectured exact result we derive from conformal field theory, and points towards a generalised central limit theorem for critical fluctuations.

</details>


### [4] [Force-Dependent Change of Structural Behavior Upon Pulling a Grafted Flexible Polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14720)
*J. Matthew Smith,Michael Bachmann*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过广义系综蒙特卡洛模拟，研究拉力对柔性聚合物粗粒化模型结构相变的影响，并用微正则拐点分析法识别和分类结构相。结果表明拉力增大使coil-globule相变由二级变一级，并与液固相变融合；发现中间相和Gough-Joule效应，拉力超过阈值后相变消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过实验工具（如光镊）操纵聚合物链有助于理解分子力对大分子结构的影响。本研究旨在从理论上模拟拉力对柔性聚合物结构相的作用，填补外界机械力对高分子相变行为影响这一方面的系统性认识。

Method: 采用柔性聚合物的粗粒化模型，利用广义系综蒙特卡洛方法进行模拟；运用新提出的微正则拐点分析法（microcanonical inflection-point analysis）识别和分类结构相；借助特定的序参量表征拉力作用下系统的相变行为。

Result: 随拉力强度增大，coil-globule（线圈-球状）相变由二级变为一级，随后与液-固相变融合；在特定拉力强度下识别出介于线圈相和球状相之间的中间相；发现明显的Gough-Joule效应；当拉力超过某一阈值时，相变不再发生。

Conclusion: 外界拉力显著改变柔性聚合物的结构相变行为，不仅能改变相变级次、诱发中间相并促使相变融合，还表现出Gough-Joule效应；拉力过大则完全抑制相变。

Abstract: Manipulating polymer chains by means of experimental instruments such as optical tweezers has helped understand better the effects of molecular forces in macromolecular structures. In this study, we simulate the influence of a pulling force on structural phases of a coarse-grained model for flexible polymers using generalized-ensemble Monte Carlo methodologies. The recently introduced microcanonical inflection-point analysis method is employed to identify and classify structural phases of the system. Specific order parameters help characterize the transition behavior in response to the present pulling force. We show that, upon increasing the pulling strength, the coil-globule transition turns from second to first order, then merges with the liquid-solid transition. An intermediate phase, separating the coil and globule phases, is identified for certain force strengths. Clear evidence of the Gough-Joule effect is found as well. Transitions do not occur at all if the pulling force exceeds a certain threshold value.

</details>


### [5] [Optimal Driving between Nonequilibrium Steady States under a Dissipation Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14891)
*Keisuke Sugie,Dimitri Loutchko,Tetsuya J. Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种同时优化协议与时长、且受固定房客熵产生（housekeeping EP）预算约束的过量熵产生（excess EP）最小化框架，将总熵产生最小化映射为在“摩擦度量”加权下的测地线优化问题，并在近平衡条件下给出总熵产生下界，通过三态钙泵模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动系统在非平衡稳态之间切换时，总熵产生包含两部分：由控制参数变化导致的过量熵产生，以及维持稳态电流所需的房客熵产生。传统固定时长优化只最小化前者而忽略后者，导致在给定总耗散预算下并非最优。因此需要同时优化协议与时长，并在固定房客熵产生预算约束下最小化总熵产生。

Method: 在慢驱动极限下，将累计的房客熵产生视为“熵时间”，把问题转化为在一个由稳态熵产生率加权的摩擦度量下的测地线优化；通过该资源型几何确定最优路径与调度，刻画房客熵产生与过量熵产生之间的内在权衡；近平衡时进一步导出总熵产生的几何下界，并在由修正热力学长度给出的最优预算处以能量均分达到该下界。

Result: 该框架揭示房客熵产生与过量熵产生之间存在内在权衡：稳态亦持续耗散的系统经由资源型几何确定最优路径与时间调度。与固定时长驱动相比，获得明显不同的最优协议。数值模拟采用 ATP 驱动的钙泵三态模型，验证了总熵产生下界。

Conclusion: 通过将房客熵产生预算纳入优化，本文建立了一个统一的几何框架，同时优化驱动协议及其时长，最小化总熵产生，并给出近平衡条件下的总熵产生下界。数值结果证实该界限成立，说明考虑稳态耗散对设计低耗散驱动协议至关重要。

Abstract: Driving between nonequilibrium steady states incurs two thermodynamic costs: excess entropy production (EP) resulting from changing control parameters, and housekeeping EP required to sustain steady currents. Conventional fixed-duration optimization minimizes the former while neglecting the latter. Here, we instead optimize excess EP over both the protocol and its duration subject to a fixed leading order of housekeeping-EP budget, thereby minimizing total EP for that budget constraint. In the slow-driving regime, accumulated housekeeping EP acts as an entropic time, mapping the problem to a geodesic optimization under a friction metric weighted by the steady-state EP rate. This resource-based geometry determines optimal paths and schedules for systems that continue to dissipate even in steady states, establishing an intrinsic trade-off between housekeeping and excess EPs. Near equilibrium, this framework further yields geometric lower bounds on total EP, attained with equipartition at the optimal budget given by a modified thermodynamic length. Numerical simulations of a three-state model for an ATP-driven calcium pump reveal distinct optimal protocols compared to fixed-duration driving, confirming the total-EP bounds.

</details>


### [6] [Going beyond the standard quasiparticle picture: Entanglement spreading after quenching from highly entangled states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15409)
*Elisa Sabadini,Vincenzo Alba*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了XXZ自旋链中从n-rainbow态（一类由长程Bell对构成的宏观均匀非可积初态）出发的量子猝灭。作者证明局部稳态在n→∞时趋于无穷温度态（修正为1/n阶），但在无相互作用的XX链中，猝灭会产生2n种准粒子的纠缠多重态，其种间关联虽以1/n衰减，却强烈抑制纠缠熵的增长速率（n→∞时趋于零），这一行为可由推广的准粒子图像描述；而在相互作用的XXZ链中，tDMRG模拟显示纠缠熵以热力学熵跨截面交换的速率增长，未表现出多重态抑制现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准粒子图像为可积系统中量子猝灭后的纠缠扩散提供了定量描述，但它依赖于"猝灭产生纠缠准粒子对"这一假设，该假设仅对可积初态成立。作者旨在超越这一限制，研究非可积初态（n-rainbow态）下的纠缠动力学，并探究可积性、初态性质与纠缠增长之间的关系。

Method: 作者以XXZ自旋链为模型，从n-rainbow态（n>1时为非可积初态）出发进行量子猝灭。分析方法上：(1) 解析地证明局部稳态在n→∞时趋于无穷温度态，且修正为1/n阶；(2) 在无相互作用的XX链中，解析推导猝灭产生2n种准粒子的纠缠多重态，并建立推广的准粒子图像刻画其纠缠熵增长被抑制的行为；(3) 在相互作用的XXZ链中，采用含时密度矩阵重整化群(tDMRG)数值模拟，分别对均匀猝灭及n-rainbow态与无穷温度态拼接两种情况研究纠缠熵增长。

Result: (1) 解析结果：猝灭后的局部稳态以1/n阶修正趋近无穷温度态。(2) XX链：猝灭产生2n种准粒子的纠缠多重态，种间关联以1/n衰减，但强烈抑制纠缠熵增长率，n→∞时增长率趋于零，推广的准粒子图像可定量描述这一行为。(3) XXZ链：tDMRG模拟表明纠缠熵以热力学熵跨截面交换的速率增长，在可访问时间尺度内未观察到多重态抑制现象，这在均匀猝灭和n-rainbow态与无穷温度态拼接两种情形下均成立；对后者作者还猜测了von Neumann熵的公式。

Conclusion: 非可积初态（n-rainbow态）在XX链中会导致纠缠多重态的生成并显著抑制纠缠熵增长，这种抑制可由推广的准粒子图像刻画；而在相互作用的XXZ链中，该抑制效应在tDMRG可达时间尺度内消失，纠缠熵按热力学熵交换速率增长。这表明准粒子图像对初态可积性的敏感依赖，以及相互作用在恢复常规纠缠增长中的关键作用。

Abstract: The quasiparticle picture provides a quantitative description of entanglement spreading after quantum quenches in integrable systems. In interacting models, however, it relies on the assumption that the quench creates entangled pairs of quasiparticles, which is justified only for the so-called integrable initial states. Here we go beyond this setting by studying quenches in the XXZ spin chain from the $n$-rainbow states, a family of macroscopically homogeneous states formed by increasingly long-range Bell pairs, which are not integrable for $n>1$. The local stationary state after the quench approaches the infinite-temperature state as $n\to\infty$, with corrections of order $1/n$, as we show analytically. Nevertheless, in the noninteracting XX chain the quench produces entangled multiplets of $2n$ species of quasiparticles. Although interspecies correlations vanish as $1/n$, they strongly suppress the growth of the entanglement entropy, whose rate vanishes as $n\to\infty$. We capture this behaviour within a generalized quasiparticle picture. In the interacting XXZ chain, instead, time-dependent density matrix renormalization group (tDMRG) simulations show that the entanglement entropy grows at the rate at which thermodynamic entropy is exchanged across the cut. Within the time scales accessible to tDMRG, the data show no trace of the multiplet suppression. This holds both after homogeneous quenches and after joining the $n$-rainbow state with an infinite-temperature state, for which we conjecture a formula for the von Neumann entropy.

</details>


### [7] [Cumulant dynamics of the ferromagnetic dipolar XY model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15485)
*Jinxin Yang,Alberto R. Cavallar,Lode Pollet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究正方晶格上偶极铁磁XY模型在制备动力学态时二阶截断累积量动力学的行为，发现即使在非绝热但足够慢的斜坡过程中也能形成有限尺度、有限磁化的相干区域，且这两个尺度由斜坡常数、初始熵和时间共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索偶极铁磁XY模型中动力学态制备的可行性，理解在更大系统尺度下实现偶极XY平衡物理所需的条件与限制。

Method: 采用二阶截断累积量动力学（second-order truncated cumulant dynamics）方法，在正方晶格上模拟偶极铁磁XY模型，系统线度最大达到 L = 60。

Result: 发现体系会形成有限空间尺度、有限磁化的相干区域，这一现象即使在非绝热（diabatic）但斜坡足够慢的情况下依然成立；相干区域的尺度和磁化强度是斜坡常数、系统初始熵和时间的函数。

Conclusion: 研究结果对未来在更大系统尺度上开展偶极XY平衡物理实验提出了严格的要求，暗示实现目标需要精细控制斜坡速率、初始熵等参数。

Abstract: We study the second-order truncated cumulant dynamics for the preparation of dynamical states in the dipolar ferromagnetic XY model on the square lattice for system sizes up to linear size L = 60. The observations lead to a transparent picture in which a coherent region of finite extent and finite magnetization forms, which is possible even for diabatic but sufficiently slow ramps. Abstract: We study the recovery of a rank-one signal embedded in a symmetric Euclidean random matrix, whose dense and geometrically correlated entries generally lack rotational invariance. As the signal strength increases, a crossing between competing eigenvalues can change their ordering, causing the leading eigenvector to switch abruptly from a noise-dominated to a signal-bearing direction. This mechanism gives rise to a discontinuous BBP-type transition, in which the overlap with the signal jumps from zero to a finite value. Using the replica method, we reduce the problem to a Fredholm eigenvalue equation and derive general criteria for the existence of a positive recovery threshold, expressed in terms of the unperturbed spectral edge, the spike mean and the spatial averages of the principal eigenfunctions. Abstract: When a system is driven by a prescribed protocol, the entropy production can be decomposed into the housekeeping and non-autonomous contributions, each satisfying a fluctuation theorem. For a solvable harmonic model, we map the dynamics onto an autonomous system in which the measurement and filtering process acts as an effective thermal bath. Abstract: The anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior may arise under noise, adding a noise-induced contribution to the defect density beyond the conventional Kibble-Zurek term. Abstract: Spatial heterogeneity in antibiotic environments drives the evolution of bacterial resistance. Abstract: Although information thermodynamics explains how the second law is modified due to the information acquired by Maxwell's demon, its formulation in many-body systems remains largely open. By additionally assuming that each local feedback term depends only on nearby measurement outcomes, we rigorously prove that the extractable work coincides in the thermodynamic limit with that extractable from the corresponding canonical ensemble. We further express this extractable work in terms of quantum-information quantities, including the quantum-classical mutual information and the Holevo information for the corresponding canonical ensemble. We also show that feedback based on distant measurement outcomes can exploit long-range correlations hidden in MITE, yielding an extensive increase in extracted work relative to the canonical ensemble.

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-time random walk process via a structured density by tracking the state of the particle as the system evolves. A wide range of governing integro-differential equations may be obtained from this formulation, some of which exhibit various types of anomalous behaviour. For certain choices of reset probabilities, waiting times and jump distributions, we explicitly derive the mode-dependent memory kernels and investigate their continuum limits. The framework can recover the time-and-space-fractional Fokker-Planck equation, which traditionally models the interplay between long wait times and large jumps and exhibits anomalous diffusion. Furthermore, we obtain a process which converges toward the equilibrium distribution as time approaches some finite value through a sequence of waiting times which decrease geometrically. Finally, we investigate a process that incorporates self-excitation and self-inhibition into the waiting times for each state to produce a mean square displacement proportional to $t^ν$ for any $ν> 0$. Through these examples, we illustrate the link between the microscopic mechanisms in these processes and their non-local macroscopic governing equations.

Abstract: We investigate the transient dynamics of a one-dimensional ballistic random walker under threshold resetting (TR). The walker starts at $x_0>0$, moves towards a threshold at the origin with a velocity drawn from a distribution $φ(v)$, and on reaching the threshold is reset to $x_0$ with a new sampled velocity. Whenever the mean first-passage time to the threshold is finite, the walker reaches a steady state that is uniform on $[0,x_0]$ and independent of $φ(v)$. The relaxation towards it, in contrast, is highly sensitive to the behavior of $φ(v)$. For a large class of $φ(v)$-s, we find, rather remarkably, that the relaxation rate depends explicitly on the spatial coordinate, in contrast to conventional relaxation phenomena, where the rate is typically position-independent. Furthermore, this space-dependent relaxation rate eventually freezes to a constant, position-independent value beyond a critical position, a phenomenon we identify as \textit{freezing transition}. Additionally, for a power-law velocity distribution, the system exhibits an anomalously slow, algebraic relaxation towards the steady state. We also showed that a sharp cutoff in $φ(v)$ generates a shock-like discontinuity in the particle propagator that travels towards the threshold, with a jump height that decays with time. These results demonstrate how velocity fluctuations, despite leaving the steady state unchanged, can profoundly influence the transient spatial structures and showcase rich non-equilibrium behavior. Our analytical results are corroborated by Monte Carlo simulations, showing an excellent agreement.

Abstract: Work dissipation into a thermal bath is standard, whereas the reverse process -- sole work extraction from a thermal bath without a net state change -- contradicts the Kelvin-Planck formulation of the second law of thermodynamics. By assigning this assembly a canonical temperature derived from its Shannon/information entropy, the framework naturally extends to molecular-scale machines. We demonstrate paradigmatic examples where the assembly's canonical temperature is cooler than the negative-temperature bath. Consequently, sole work extraction represents standard heat flow from a hot system (the negative-temperature bath) to a cooler system (the working assembly), rendering the process fully consistent with the second law. Method: 从Landau-Dean-Kawasaki方程（即Landau方程大偏差原理的随机对应物）出发，在$1/N$领头阶导出经验分布函数$n$点矩的无穷层级，并证明其可由准高斯封闭精确求解。由此得到由Landau方程、两点矩Lyapunov方程和三点矩动力学方程组成的最小Landau涨落层级；更高阶矩由这三点代数地递推出。该层级精确保持碰撞不变量及其涨落，并给出中心涨落区域观测量的完整概率分布。

Conclusion: 在大$N$下，三个确定性耦合偏微分方程可完全替代大量模拟系综来刻画有限$N$涨落的统计，大幅降低计算成本并提供完整的涨落统计预测。

Abstract: Finite-$N$ fluctuations govern the slow relaxation of long-range interacting systems, yet accessing their full statistics demands huge ensembles of costly simulations. We show that, in homogeneous systems without collective effects, this statistical problem collapses onto just three deterministic kinetic equations. Starting from the Landau-Dean-Kawasaki equation, i.e. the stochastic counterpart of the large deviation principle for the Landau equation, we derive the infinite hierarchy of $n$-point moments of the empirical distribution function at leading order in $1/N$. We prove that it is solved exactly by a quasi-Gaussian closure. The resulting minimal Landau fluctuation hierarchy is formed by the Landau equation, a Lyapunov equation for the two-point moment, and a kinetic equation for the three-point moment. From these three equations, all higher-order moments follow algebraically. The hierarchy exactly preserves the collisional invariants and their fluctuations, and predicts the full probability distribution of observables in the central fluctuation regime. Benchmarked against $N$-body realizations, it reproduces the mean, variance and higher-order moments with an accuracy that improves systematically with $N$. A single deterministic time-integration of three coupled partial differential equations thus replaces large ensembles of simulations, for any large $N$.

Abstract: Motivated by recent proposals of $p$-wave magnets, which host odd-parity spin splitting, we study electrons near Van Hove singularities with local repulsive interactions. We demonstrate how the symmetry of the $p$-wave spin splitting governs the competition among instabilities. Using a parquet renormalization group (pRG) analysis of a simple one-sublattice model of a $p$-wave magnet, we find that superconductivity is the leading instability over the entire phase diagram for purely repulsive interactions. The resulting superconducting state is generically a mixture of spin-singlet and spin-triplet pairing and approaches an equal-weight mixture when $T_c$ is small compared with the energy offset between each Van Hove electron and its inversion partner. Extending the pRG analysis to a more realistic two-sublattice model, we find that superconductivity remains the leading instability, while the sublattice degree of freedom leads to a competition among various singlet-triplet pairing channels and even a finite-momentum equal-spin pair-density wave. Our mechanism applies broadly to odd-parity magnets and opens a natural route to nodal topological superconductivity.

Abstract: Competition between spin and valley orders makes graphene a versatile platform for engineering correlated topological phases in the quantum Hall regime. We show that a tunable superlattice potential reorganizes this competition, and stabilizes the ferromagnetic quantum Hall topological insulator at charge neutrality without requiring a large in-plane magnetic field or strong dielectric suppression of Coulomb interactions. The interaction anisotropies responsible for choosing between different competing states are first computed by a generalized renormalization group procedure that accounts for the superlattice potential. We find that the potential suppresses anisotropies that compete with spin-polarization. At the same time, the potential introduces dispersion which prefers a partly spin-polarized compensated semi-metal. Therefore, we carry out a self-consistent momentum-space Hartree-Fock computation with renormalized aniostropies to establish the regime of stability of the insulating spin-polarized ferromagnet. Finally, we verify that this ferromagnet is adiabatically connected to the quantum Hall ferromagnet, thereby confirming its topological character. Our results establish superlattice potentials as a means of controlling the phase diagram of interacting graphene and accessing its helical quantum Hall regime.

Together, these observations provide spectroscopic evidence for termination-selective $f$-Dirac hybridization and the development of 4$f$ character in a topological surface state. Abstract: $O(N)$ models provide a versatile framework for describing continuous phase transitions in a wide variety of physical systems. We investigate an extension of these models arising from order parameters that transform under interlocked internal and spatial symmetries which describe transitions towards non-polar structural order. Such order occurs, for instance, in dipolar antiferromagnets, spin-orbit-coupled magnets, non-polar ferroics, and certain nematic systems. The resulting effective theory is an $O(d)$ model with an additional symmetry-allowed kinetic term $h(\partial_iφ_i)^2$, which modifies the relative stiffness of longitudinal and transverse fluctuations. We study both thermal and quantum phase transitions within this $O(d)$ model using the functional renormalization group (FRG). In contrast to conventional $O(N)$ models, the interlocking of internal and spatial symmetries distinguishes spatial and imaginary-time derivatives, such that the quantum model is not simply obtained from the thermal one by a dimensional extension $d\rightarrow d+1$. We derive and analyze the corresponding FRG flow equations, paying particular attention to the tensor structure of the two-point function, which is essential for consistently extracting the flow of the kinetic terms. Our analysis shows that the additional kinetic coupling is irrelevant at the $O(d)$ Wilson-Fisher fixed point in both thermal and quantum cases, where quantum-to-classical correspondence thus re-emerges. However, its small but sizable correction-to-scaling exponent gives rise to substantial corrections to scaling in the vicinity of the critical point.

We find that discrete translation symmetry, encoded in a nonuniform quantum geometry, affects fractionalized states at filling fractions $ν=1/3$ and $ν=2/3$ differently, and that band mixing can stabilize incommensurate crystalline ground states. Our results further reveal the delicate competition between topological and non-topological crystalline phases.

Abstract: Landau quantization can induce nematic order in a two-dimensional Fermi liquid that is isotropic at zero magnetic field. Using a mean-field theory near a quadrupolar Pomeranchuk instability, we show that varying the magnetic field produces alternating nematic and isotropic regions, with transitions that can be either continuous or first order. The resulting nematic state also leaves distinctive signatures in the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations. At fixed chemical potential, the field-dependent deformation of the Fermi surface can produce substantial changes in the oscillation frequency, even when the cyclotron mass varies only modestly. At fixed particle density, conservation of the Fermi-surface area preserves the leading oscillation frequency, while the cyclotron mass and harmonic amplitudes remain sensitive to nematic order. These contrasting behaviors provide experimentally relevant signatures of field-induced nematicity and reveal the importance of thermodynamic constraints in quantum-oscillation measurements.

Abstract: Quantum oscillations are widely used to probe the Fermi surface of metals, but their behavior becomes more complex when electronic interactions can spontaneously deform that surface. We investigate this interplay in a two-dimensional model of interacting electrons capable of developing nematic order, using a uniform mean-field approximation in a perpendicular magnetic field. We show that Landau quantization can favor or suppress nematic order as the field varies, producing alternating isotropic and nematic regions near a Pomeranchuk instability, with both continuous and first-order transitions. Conversely, the evolving nematic order modifies the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations in ways that depend on the thermodynamic conditions. At fixed chemical potential, the oscillation frequency can change significantly even when the cyclotron mass varies only modestly. At fixed particle density, conservation of the Fermi-surface area preserves the leading Onsager frequency, while the cyclotron mass and harmonic amplitudes remain sensitive to nematic order. The magnetization and magnetic susceptibility provide additional signatures of the nematic transitions. These results show how quantum oscillations can both reveal and influence electronic order, offering a framework for interpreting experiments in strongly correlated metals.

Abstract: We develop a framework to study subsystem symmetry-protected topological (SSPT) phases using lattice response theory. We begin with a systematic construction of spacetime lattice gauge fields from general microscopic $X$-type symmetry actions on qudit systems, including global symmetries, subsystem symmetries, and more general modulated symmetries. Given a gauge-invariant response action, we then construct a corresponding entangler and explicitly show that it implements stacking with the SSPT phase described by the response action. We show that a broad class of SSPT phases can be described by intersection pairings and realized by cluster entanglers. We also study SSPT phases beyond intersection pairings, including those described by subsystem analogues of type-II group cocycles involving the Bockstein homomorphism. We construct a new 3+1d SSPT phase protected by two 2-foliated planar subsystem symmetries and a global 0-form symmetry, whose response action is a subsystem analogue of a type-III cocycle. On its boundary, the 0-form symmetry acts by stacking with a 2+1d linear SSPT phase, signaling a mixed 't Hooft anomaly.

Abstract: We construct non-Fermi liquid (NFL) quantum impurity models for doublet impurities in a cubic metal, involving conduction electrons with half-integer total angular momentum, $J$. We highlight the only two crystal-symmetry-protected NFL quantum impurity models: the spin-half impurity, $J=3/2$ conduction electron Kondo model, and the two-channel Kondo model originally derived in the context of the quadrupolar Kondo model. We show explicitly that the quadrupolar Kondo model preserves time-reversal symmetry. We also construct two cubic quantum impurity models in which NFL behavior is not symmetry-protected. Each has $Γ_8$ and either $Γ_{6}$ or $Γ_{7}$ conduction electrons screening the impurity simultaneously, one model for a non-Kramers doublet and the other for a Kramers doublet impurity ground state. To our knowledge, the latter model has not been considered before; we expect it to realize a new NFL fixed point, but its solution is left for future work. Finally, we derive the relevant cubic perturbation that drives the non-symmetry-protected systems away from their NFL scaling regimes.

Abstract: Light can manipulate magnetic order and its excitations on ultrafast timescales, including through resonantly driven phonons. In antiferromagnets, light can also create correlated magnon pairs, or bimagnons. Unlike single magnons, whose damping has been measured and modeled in detail, little is known about the lifetime, interactions and decay of such correlated pairs: prior two-magnon measurements show broad linewidths or coherences that dephase within 200 fs. Here we pump the phonon-assisted two-magnon absorption of NiO with mid-infrared pulses, far below its bandgap, and probe the resulting magnetic dynamics at finite momentum with time-resolved resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS). Upon photoexcitation, the one-magnon linewidth and energy are essentially unchanged, indicating that the long-range magnetic order is preserved without the photocarrier-induced demagnetization characteristic of above-bandgap excitation. A pump-induced two-magnon signal appears on the energy-gain (anti-Stokes) side of the spectrum. Its spectral weight peaks 4 ps after the pump, well beyond our 0.5 ps time resolution, indicative of a delayed growth likely due to momentum redistribution of the light-induced magnon pairs across the Brillouin zone. This two-magnon signal decays with a time constant of $28\pm11$ ps, exceeding the previously reported two-magnon dephasing time by more than two orders of magnitude. These results establish long-lived light-driven bimagnon correlations beyond the one-magnon state, akin to the higher-order correlations measured in ultracold gases.

Result: 在弱、强耦合下相互作用边缘附近存在无能隙模式，中间耦合时边界进入完全有能隙的SMG相；在超过临界相互作用强度后出现模型Z2对称性自发破缺。三个边界相中均观测到量子化电荷泵浦，且每磁通量子泵浦电荷相同，但泵浦电荷的空间分布随相而异。SMG相中，能隙边缘端点处产生相互作用诱导的味混合电流（弱、强耦合极限下不存在），揭示了SMG相特有的电荷重分布机制。

Conclusion: 边界即使被对称地打开能隙（SMG），量子化电荷泵浦依然保持，且泵浦电荷拓扑上等价（每磁通量子相同），仅空间分布改变；SMG相的味混合电流揭示其独特的电荷重分布机制。此外，该模型的电荷泵浦可类比标准模型中的拓扑轻子生成：3-4-5-0模型的无反常手征U(1)对称性类似标准模型的重子数减轻子数（B-L）对称性，两者的电荷泵浦都关联于沿经典定义但量子反常方向上的连续形变，而离散子群保持精确量子对称性。

Abstract: Quantized charge pumping in quantum Hall systems is a bulk topological property, typically manifesting on gapless boundaries as spectral flow through chiral edge modes upon flux insertion. When the boundary is anomaly-free, it can be symmetrically gapped by symmetric mass generation (SMG) interactions. However, the effects on quantized charge pumping at an interacting gapped boundary are unclear. We address this using density-matrix renormalization group simulations of a four-leg ladder quasi-(2+1)d realization of the (1+1)d 3-4-5-0 lattice chiral fermion model with six-fermion interactions at one edge. We find gapless modes near the interacting edge for both weak and strong couplings, but the edge can be in a fully gapped SMG phase at intermediate coupling. We also identify spontaneous breaking of the model's $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry above a critical interaction strength. Across all three boundary phases, we find quantized charge pumping with the same pumped charge per flux quantum. The spatial distribution of the pumped charge varies between phases. Besides charge pumping, we study how a single injected fermion propagates under open boundary conditions. In the SMG phase, an interaction-induced flavor-mixing current develops at the endpoints of the gapped edge, which is absent in the weak- and strong-coupling limits. This current reveals a charge-redistribution mechanism unique to the SMG phase. Additionally, we propose an analogy between charge pumping in this model and a topological leptogenesis scenario in the (3+1)d Standard Model. The anomaly-free chiral U(1) symmetries of the 3-4-5-0 model play a role analogous to the baryon-minus-lepton symmetry of the Standard Model. In both settings, charge pumping can be associated with a continuous deformation along a classically defined but quantum-anomalous direction, while a discrete subgroup remains an exact quantum symmetry.

</details>

Abstract: The Poisson $σ$-model (PSM) over a closed worldsheet $Σ$ is reinterpreted as a theory of connections on a trivial principaloid bundle $P_0 = Σ\times G \to Σ$ with the symplectic structure groupoid $(G,ω)$ integrating the Poisson target $(M,Π)$, and that of sections of the shadow bundle $F_0 = Σ\times M \to Σ$ - as defined in 2503.09886 [math.DG]. The gauge anomaly of the ensuing field theory is derived and demonstrated to vanish for prequantisable $ω$. A general anomaly-cancellation mechanism is proposed, which couples the bare model - rewritten as the holonomy of a connection-dependent trivial gerbe on $F_0$ - to a background gerbe over $M$ endowed with a $G$-equivariant structure. The coupling takes the form of the tensor product of the two gerbes and the extended PSM is defined as the holonomy of the product gerbe. An essential generalisation of the theory is then proposed - to shadows $F$ of principaloid bundles $P$ of arbitrary topology, in keeping with the interpretation of the gauging as descent to the orbistack $M//G$. The generalisation uses local product gerbes depending on local connections over trivialisations of $F$. The $G$-equivariant structure is proven to ensure a 2-stacky descent of the local gerbes to a global one over $F$. Its holonomy is taken as the definition of the generalised PSM. A cohomological discussion of obstructions against the descent is provided. Finally, a reformulation of the generalised PSM as a field theory with a topological gauge-symmetry defect is given, in which the $G$-equivariant structure is employed - alongside the transition cocycle of $P$ - as the datum of the defect.

Abstract: The paper presents a brief overview of recent advances in the mathematical theory of evolution equations for quantum many-particle systems.

It has been shown that the concept of cumulants of operator semigroups forms the basis for non-perturbative representation of solutions to hierarchies of evolution equations that describe the state and observables.
  It is proven that the constructed dynamics of correlations underlie the description of the evolution of the state, both for finitely and infinitely many particles, governed by the BBGKY hierarchy for reduced density operators or by the hierarchy of nonlinear evolution equations for reduced correlation operators. In fact, it is demonstrated that the behavior of particle correlations governs the dynamics of states in quantum systems with an infinite number of particles.

Result: 主要结果有三点：(1) 对最低权重大于 1/2 的物种族，两个边界代数是相互交换子，且为非超有限 II∞ 型因子，其非零有限角为非超有限 II₁ 型因子；(2) 权重限制是尖锐的：当 0<d≤1/2 时，基本点场在正热正则化后仍保持无界；(3) 对单个无质量标量，特定有界观测量与正规态的高能极限由一个超有限连续核描述，而该极限过程不保持精确代数的非超有限性。

Abstract: We construct the canonical boundary algebras of Jackiw--Teitelboim gravity coupled to free bosonic matter. Their nonzero finite corners are nonhyperfinite type $\mathrm{II}_1$ factors. The construction uses bounded heat-regularized fields and a Hilbert algebra, which gives a faithful normal semifinite trace without a path-integral argument. The weight restriction is sharp: elementary point fields remain unbounded after positive heat regularization when $0<d\leq1/2$. For one massless scalar, specified bounded observables and normal states have a high energy limit described by a hyperfinite continuous core. We explain why this limit does not preserve the nonhyperfiniteness of the exact algebra.

Method: 构造metriplectic结构（Volterra能量函数、熵变量、log-canonical Poisson双矢），仅当CA+AᵀC≤0时成立；通过随机GENERIC的涨落-耗散关系引入乘性噪声，协方差由AC⁻¹的对称部分决定；证明平稳解满足Lyapunov型恒等式AK+KᵀAᵀ+Q=0（K=Cov(x,log x)）；计算零滞后对数丰度协方差导数、Poisson部分对应的稳态熵产生；并用捕食-被捕食模型仿真验证下界近似紧致。

Abstract: We show that a Lotka--Volterra system with an interior equilibrium admits a metriplectic structure, with a Volterra function $V_C$ as energy and an entropy variable for the environment, if and only if Volterra's dissipativity condition $CA+A^{\top}C\le0$ holds, and we construct it explicitly with a log-canonical Poisson bivector. Adding noise through the fluctuation--dissipation relation of stochastic GENERIC yields multiplicative environmental noise, with covariance fixed by the symmetric part of $AC^{-1}$, whose stationary law is an explicit Gibbs measure: a product of independent Gamma distributions. Unlike known Gibbs measures for stochastic Lotka--Volterra systems, this holds for nonsymmetric interactions, predator--prey included. The stationary law determines only the equilibrium and $C/k$: every interaction matrix in an open cone of dimension $n^2$, in particular any Poisson part, produces with its compatible noise the same law as uncoupled stochastic logistic equations. Gamma-distributed, uncorrelated abundance fluctuations, as observed in microbial communities, are therefore no evidence of absence of interactions. The interactions are, however, visible in time: the zero-lag derivative of the log-abundance covariance equals $A(C/k)^{-1}$, and its antisymmetric part is the Poisson part, which carries all the stationary entropy production, computed in closed form. Finally, for general noise we prove an exact Lyapunov-type identity $AK+K^\top A^\top+Q=0$ for $K=\mathrm{Cov}(x,\log x)$ at any stationary solution. It implies that the populations are independent if and only if the noise is metriplectically compatible, and gives an explicit lower bound on interspecies cross-covariances in terms of the distance of $Q$ to the compatible family. Simulations of a predator--prey model show that the bound is nearly sharp.

</details>

Abstract: We construct two-parameter Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators for the transfer matrices of noncompact open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC-type spin chains with general boundary interactions, treating both one-boundary and two-boundary models. Abstract: We give a probabilistic characterization of the ideals of resolvent algebras. Abstract: Data-driven constitutive model discovery is recast as causal discovery of mechanism parent sets using time-unrolled structural causal models. Conversely, observational data with a temperature--moisture correlation of $-0.998$ fail entirely because confounded scores are 250--380 times flatter. Abstract: We study various families of non-periodic configurations of symbols assigned to vertices of regular graphs. We restrict ourselves to such systems where considered configurations support a uniquely ergodic measure. Symbols can be interpreted as particles and we are concerned with configurations which minimize the interaction energy of particles, the so-called ground-state configurations. One of the main questions then is whether ground-state configurations are stable with respect to small perturbations of interactions. Such stability may be interpreted as a sort of rigidity of non-periodic structures such as tilings, substitution systems, Sturmian systems, and other uniquely ergodic non-periodic systems. We introduce a property of bounded fluctuations of finite patterns of non-periodic configurations, the so-called Strict Boundary Property. We discuss how relevant this property is for stability of non-periodic structures. Examples of Robinson's tilings, Thue-Morse, and Sturmian systems are presented. Some open problems are formulated.

Abstract: Norton's dome illustrates how a Newtonian initial-value problem can admit both rest and delayed departure from the same initial state. The one-particle Hamiltonian $Υ$ and the coupling map are determined by the Hamiltonian, and the field obeys a quantum Langevin equation. Spin-dressed implementations in Fock space, at the finest scale and for form factors in $D(Υ^{-1/2})$, are exactly the generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians, unique up to the spin Hamiltonian. At the roughest scale, the renormalized spin--boson Hamiltonian of a two-level system with ultraviolet-singular form factor and arbitrary tunnelling is a spin-dressed implementation, and the definition alone yields asymptotic Weyl operators on the test functions along which the coupling map is integrable, a dense set under standard assumptions.

This construction provides new families of Tremblay-Turbiner-Winternitz (TTW)- and Post-Winternitz (PW)-like Hamiltonians with integrals of arbitrarily high order and Painlevé VI in the potential. This framework also recovers models from the classification of third- and fourth-order integrals as particular cases. For a representative oscillator extension we compute the Casimir explicitly and recover the finite-dimensional energy spectrum from a deformed-oscillator realization. We then analyze the special-solution sector using the explicit families collected by Gromak, Laine and Shimomura. The resulting potentials are organized into a small number of spectral classes, and the distinction between formal zeros of the ladder norm and physical self-adjoint representations is made explicit. The regular nonclassical rational examples are rational Darboux deformations of the trigonometric Pöschl--Teller system and lead to strictly isospectral exceptional-Jacobi sectors or to state-adding representations with an isolated angular level. Abstract: The two-dimensional one-component plasma is a system of point charges that repel each other logarithmically, in a uniform neutralizing background. When the inverse temperature $β$ is at most $2$, we prove that its correlations decay exponentially, and that its Gibbs state is unique among point processes whose fluctuations are controlled. This constitutes part of the fluid phase predicted by physicists. As a consequence, we obtain the existence of an infinite-volume limit, extending the Ginibre point process, which we call $\mathrm{Gin}_β$. We show that its truncated two-point function, at the scale of the Debye length, is given to first order in $β$ by the Debye-Hückel approximation.

Abstract: For ferromagnetic Ising chains with $n^2J_n\to B>0$, the critical excess magnetization in a field inversely proportional to spin separation determines the connected correlation. Their slowly varying profile has finite logarithmic integral exactly when the ordered thermal slope is finite. For any finite nonnegative core, an exact tail gives inverse-logarithmic correlations and a square-root cusp. TL;DR: 碳质常负高斯曲率曲面在实验室中实现了 Lobachevsky（双曲）平面的一部分，本文围绕平衡 Gauss–Bonnet 定理这一统一原理，综述并拓展其构造，并将三类赝球面与 SL(2,R) 的单参数子群及 BTZ 黑洞的三个静态相联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常负曲率碳表面可在实验室中实现 Lobachevsky 平面片段，应用涵盖类比引力（椭圆、Beltrami、双曲赝球面分别对应 BTZ 黑洞的三个静态相）与海水淡化等。作者希望用一个统一原理来理解这些结构的构造，即由拓扑决定的曲率如何在向错缺陷、弹性变形六元环与边界之间分配，并探究缺陷排列与对称群之间的深层联系。

Method: 以平衡 Gauss–Bonnet 定理为核心原理：总曲率由拓扑固定，并在向错缺陷、弹性变形的六元环与边界之间共享。作者给出只有六元环的球面是不穿孔球面（punctured sphere）的显式多面体构造；利用 SL(2,R) 的椭圆、抛物、双曲单参数子群推导三类赝球面上缺陷的排列方式，其中对椭圆族做了显式构造，恢复了已知的 Beltrami 赝球面排列，并提出双曲赝球面的缺陷排列；进一步将理想排列与经设计及模拟纳米挤压获得的碳结构进行对比；最后提出无穷多个 Fuchsian 群可能对应无穷多个 Lobachevsky 曲面。

Result: 证明了无缺陷曲率不能改变拓扑：仅由六元环构成的球面实为不穿孔球面，并给出显式多面体构造；成功构造了椭圆族赝球面的缺陷排列，恢复了 Beltrami 赝球面已知的排列，并提出双曲赝球面的新缺陷排列方案；理想排列与设计/模拟纳米挤压制得的碳结构表现出对应关系；并提出 Fuchsian 群与 Lobachevsky 曲面之间可能的无穷对应。

Conclusion: 常负曲率碳表面可由平衡 Gauss–Bonnet 定理这一统一原则理解：曲率在缺陷、弹性变形六元环与边界间分配，而缺陷排列由 SL(2,R) 的椭圆、抛物、双曲单参数子群自然生成。三类赝球面的缺陷排列分别对应 BTZ 黑洞的三个静态相，凸显了几何、拓扑与对称群在碳纳米结构中的深刻联系，并暗示无穷多个 Fuchsian 群可能对应无穷多个 Lobachevsky 曲面。

Abstract: Carbon-made surfaces of constant negative Gaussian curvature realize portions of the Lobachevsky plane in the laboratory, with motivations ranging from analogue gravity, where the elliptic, Beltrami and hyperbolic pseudospheres correspond to the three static phases of the Bañados--Teitelboim--Zanelli black hole, to water desalination. We review and extend their construction around a single principle, the balanced Gauss--Bonnet theorem: the curvature fixed by the topology is shared among disclination defects, elastically deformed hexagons and boundaries. Curvature without defects cannot change the topology: a sphere made of hexagons only is a punctured sphere, as an explicit polyhedral construction shows. The arrangements of the defects on the three pseudospheres stem from the elliptic, parabolic and hyperbolic one-parameter subgroups of $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$: we construct them for the elliptic family, recover the known one of the Beltrami pseudosphere, and propose one for the hyperbolic pseudosphere. We compare these ideal arrangements with carbon structures obtained by design and by simulated nano-extrusion, and suggest that the infinitely many Fuchsian groups may correspond to the infinitely many Lobachevsky surfaces.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 仅凭链端的有限（甚至有噪声的）测量即可在不解析体拓扑的情况下，以严格失败概率认证局域 Majorana 算符的持续性；该方法在合成数据和真实超导量子器件上均有效，为在近期量子硬件上可验证地认证拓扑保护型局域算符提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Finite measurements at a chain end can certify local quantum dynamics without determining bulk topology. We combine Hamiltonian learning with commutator bounds to certify the persistence of localized Majorana operators in Kitaev chains. Time-resolved single-shot boundary measurements and local bond calibrations define joint confidence sets for local Hamiltonian coefficients. Under explicit device assumptions, these sets yield state-independent lower bounds on symmetrized autocorrelations with a prescribed failure probability, valid for all compatible continuations. With equal hopping and pairing on the first bond and no additional boundary terms, an exact identity enables inference from the end qubit alone, even with arbitrary time-independent interactions elsewhere. Synthetic benchmarks near a boundary sweet spot certify persistence for up to about 60 times the measurement window. Three- and six-qubit IBM records strengthen conditional guarantees for the programmed coherent Hamiltonian. Applications to quantum-dot models relate tuning precision to persistence times and detuning-induced splitting.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The low-frequency (LF) mode observed in the THz spectrum of liquid water near 10 meV has been known for decades, yet its microscopic origin remains debated. Leading interpretations assign it to a transverse acoustic-like excitation, a transverse optic-like mode, or a mode-mode coupling mechanism. We address this question by probing the collective dynamics of H$_2$O and D$_2$O at room temperature and pressures from 0.1 to 1 GPa using high-resolution (1.6 meV) inelastic X-ray scattering (IXS). The LF mode remains heavily damped, close to critical damping, throughout the explored range, while its frequency hardens markedly with pressure, much more than expected for a transverse acoustic mode. ### [81] [Little-Parks Oscillations and Parity Effects in Mesoscopic Superconducting Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14597)
*Iogann Tolbatov,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在介观准一维超导环中，利用带扭曲边界条件的Ginzburg-Landau展开，从弱耦合BCS极限到中等耦合区间解析研究Little-Parks效应，导出跨耦合强度的闭合Tc(Φ)表达式，并证明LP响应由离散拓扑宇称效应与连续质心动能项两部分组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清Little-Parks效应中离散拓扑宇称效应与连续质心动能项各自随耦合强度的演化，弥补以往从BCS弱耦合极限直接延伸到中等耦合区间时对有限尺寸拓扑干涉与超流驱动质心偏移处理不完整的问题。

Method: 采用带扭曲边界条件的Ginzburg-Landau展开，在介观准一维环几何中同时纳入离散有限尺寸拓扑宇称干涉和超流引起的连续质心（CM）动能偏移，推导出在GL框架内适用于不同耦合强度的Tc(Φ)闭合表达式，并分项分析其行为。

Result: LP响应由两部分构成：由关联长度比ξ/R控制的离散拓扑宇称效应，以及按1/R²标度的连续动能项。随耦合增强、对关联长度收缩（ξ≪R）时，组成对在几何上对全局边界约束"失明"，离散宇称调制被指数抑制；但凝聚体仍须获得依赖磁通的宏观CM动量，故Tc保持以超流宏观动能为主导的代数型LP调制。

Conclusion: 在中等耦合区间，离散拓扑宇称效应随ξ/R减小而指数衰减，而连续质心动能项以1/R²持续存在并维持代数型LP振荡；理论适用范围受限于强耦合极限附近相位涨落使平均场GL失效前。

Abstract: We analytically investigate the Little-Parks (LP) effect, the periodic oscillation of the superconducting transition temperature $T_c$ driven by an applied magnetic flux $Φ$, from the weak-coupling Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) limit to the intermediate-coupling regime in a mesoscopic, quasi-one-dimensional ring. Utilizing a Ginzburg-Landau (GL) expansion with twisted boundary conditions, we correctly incorporate both the discrete finite-size topological parity interference and the continuous center-of-mass (CM) kinetic energy shift induced by the supercurrent. We derive a closed-form expression for $T_c(Φ)$ valid within the GL framework across variable interaction strengths. We demonstrate that the LP response comprises two distinct physical contributions: a discrete topological parity effect governed by the correlation length ratio $ξ/R$, and a continuous kinetic energy term scaling as $1/R^2$. As the coupling increases and the spatial correlation length of the pairs contracts ($ξ\ll R$), the constituent pairs become geometrically blind to the global boundary constraint, leading to an exponential suppression of the discrete parity modulation. However, the requirement that the condensate acquires a flux-dependent macroscopic CM momentum persists. This ensures that the critical temperature $T_c$ maintains an algebraic LP modulation driven predominantly by the macroscopic kinetic energy of the supercurrent. We bound our theoretical approach by discussing the breakdown of mean-field GL validity before phase fluctuations dominate near the strong-coupling limit.

</details>


### [82] [Symmetry-Enabled Rolling of Composite Bosons: A Route to Superconductivity from Strong Repulsion with $T_c$ Proportional to the Electron Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14615)
*Zhiyu Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究强库仑排斥电子体系中的超导电性，发现满足特定对称性的晶格上，三电子-一空穴(3e1h)复合粒子可通过"滚动"运动避免高阶过程的沉重有效质量，从而在强束缚控制区间实现仅由电子带宽限制的超导转变温度Tc，并构造了14个最小格点模型并给出实验实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强库仑排斥体系中发现的超导体引发疑问：排斥能否产生仅受电子带宽限制的Tc？虽然库仑排斥通过束缚电子与空穴可形成玻色性3e1h复合粒子并导致超导，但在传统情况下，强束缚使复合粒子变得沉重、超流刚度受限，导致Tc远低于带宽。作者希望打破强束缚与高Tc之间的这一权衡。

Method: 借助对称性方法：在满足特定对称性要求的晶格中，复合粒子可通过单粒子跳跃实现移动（称为"滚动"），无需经过能量代价为束缚能量级的高阶中间态。作者推导滚动判据，构造全部14个支持滚动的最小格点模型，证明可滚动的3e1h复合粒子在基态中稳定，并提出双栅极双层结构实验方案，利用精确数值计算验证Tc。

Result: 在满足对称性条件的晶格上，复合粒子即使束缚能无穷强，其质量仍保持有限，从而可在受控区间实现正比于电子带宽的Tc。推导出滚动判据并构造了全部14个最小格点模型，证明可滚动3e1h复合粒子能在基态稳定；双栅极双层结构的精确数值显示Tc可达到电子跳跃能的百分之几。

Conclusion: "可滚动性"可作为寻找和设计强排斥电子体系超导体的指导原则，所构造的最小模型可作为原型，为在强库仑排斥系统中实现高Tc超导提供了新途径。

Abstract: Motivated by superconductors discovered in systems with a strong Coulomb repulsion, we ask whether such a repulsion can give rise to superconductivity with a transition temperature $T_c$ bounded only by the electron bandwidth. Coulomb repulsion can produce pairing as it binds electrons to holes. If each hole binds three electrons into a three-electron-one-hole (3e1h) composite, the condensation of these bosonic composites can give rise to superconductivity. However, the resulting $T_c$ falls far below the electron bandwidth in the controlled regime where the binding is much stronger than the carriers' kinetic energy. This is because, on a lattice, a tightly bound composite usually has to move through a higher-order process where the intermediate state costs an energy of order the binding energy, so that the strong binding makes the composite heavy, limiting the superfluid stiffness and hence $T_c$. Here, we show that symmetry offers a way around this tradeoff. In lattices satisfying certain symmetry requirements, a composite can move by single-particle hops without any costly intermediate state, a motion we call rolling. Such rolling keeps the mass of the composite finite even for an infinitely strong binding, so that a $T_c$ proportional to the electron bandwidth can be achieved in the controlled regime. We derive a criterion for rolling and apply it to construct all fourteen minimal lattice models that support such rolling. We show that rollable 3e1h composites can indeed be stabilized in the ground state. We then propose to realize such superconductivity in a dual-gate bilayer, and use exact numerics to show that $T_c$ can reach a few percent of the electron hopping. These results establish rollability as a guiding principle in the search for and design of superconductors in strongly repelling electron systems, with the minimal models constructed here as prototypes.

</details>

Wright,Archit Shah,Jimy Encomendero,Rishabh Singh,Chandrashekar Savant,Hangwang Yang,Anthony Hyatt,Jaehong Choi,Thomas Varghese,Kushagra Aggarwal,Paul B. Welander,Apurva Mehta,David Schuster,Valla Fatemi,Gregory D. Fuchs,Michael C. Hamilton,Hong X. Tang,David A. Muller,Huili Grace Xing,Debdeep Jena*

TL;DR: 本文首次通过分子束外延（MBE）实现了具有晶体隧道势垒的 NbN/AlN/NbN 超导-绝缘-超导（SIS）约瑟夫森结，打破了长期以来晶体势垒结无法用 MBE/MOCVD 等外延技术制备的瓶颈。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: MBE 制备晶体势垒约瑟夫森结的成功，为采用合金与超晶格电极/势垒的丰富材料体系打开了大门，并有望实现超导量子电路与 HEMT、激光器等有源电子/光电器件的单片集成。

Abstract: Superconductor-insulator-superconductor Josephson junctions with crystalline tunnel barriers have remained elusive by epitaxial techniques such as MOCVD or MBE that produce the junction materials and devices powering today's electronic and photonic systems like transistors and lasers. Here, we overcome the key materials hurdle to realize the first functional nitride crystalline barrier Josephson junctions by MBE. Despite an AlN barrier thickness of approximately 4.5 nm, the NbN/AlN/NbN trilayers exhibit coherent Josephson tunneling with critical current densities of approximately 17 A/cm2, an IcRn product of approximately 2.5 mV, and a superconducting gap voltage of approximately 3.7 mV with a BCS-like gap evolution with temperature. Hysteretic current-voltage characteristics and Fraunhofer current modulation with magnetic field establish underdamped SIS Josephson behavior. The realization of crystalline barrier Josephson junctions by MBE opens the doors to a richer materials palette with alloys and superlattice electrodes and tunnel barriers, as well as potential monolithic integration of superconducting quantum circuits with active electronic and photonic devices such as HEMTs and lasers.

</details>

*Ruiqing Fu,Xianxin Wu,Ziqiang Wang,Sen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用单轨道t-W-J模型，在p型van Hove填充附近进行自洽平均场计算（2×2超胞），并通过Ginzburg-Landau分析识别对称性允许的耦合项，从而理解均匀超导如何重组PDW的振幅与相位结构。

Result: 均匀超导分量重新组织了三个F_1 PDW分量的振幅和相位结构，产生了纯键配对极限中不存在的新超导态；Ginzburg-Landau分析表明对称性允许的耦合可以促成这种重组；所得态表现出独特的电荷调制与准粒子谱，手性PDW随在位吸引增强发生拓扑转变。

Conclusion: 均匀超导会重塑PDW态的序参量格局与准粒子拓扑，展示了均匀超导与PDW共存时对系统物理性质（电荷调制、准粒子谱及拓扑特性）的深刻影响。

Abstract: We study the interplay between pair density wave (PDW) and uniform superconductivity on the kagome lattice within a single-orbital $t$-$W$-$J$ model near the $p$-type van Hove filling, where $W$ and $J$ denote effective pairing attractions on lattice sites and nearest-neighbor bonds, respectively. While onsite attraction favors zero-momentum pairing through the conventional BCS instability, nearest-neighbor attraction naturally promotes finite-momentum pairing due to the kagome geometry and sublattice interference. The model therefore provides a framework for exploring the competition and coexistence between uniform superconductivity and PDW order. Self-consistent mean-field calculations in a $2\times2$ supercell reveal that the uniform component reorganizes both the amplitudes and phase structure of the three $F_1$ PDW components, giving rise to superconducting states absent in the pure bond-pairing limit. A Ginzburg--Landau analysis identifies symmetry-allowed couplings that can favor this reorganization. The resulting states exhibit distinct charge modulations and quasiparticle spectra, while the chiral PDW undergoes topological transitions as the onsite attraction increases. Our results demonstrate how uniform superconductivity reshapes the order-parameter landscape and quasiparticle topology of a PDW state.

</details>


### [85] [Machine-accelerated extraction of experimental superconducting parameters for physical insight and prediction across thousands of materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15627)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新超导体可推动核聚变、无损耗电子学等下一代技术，但机器学习预测超导体缺乏除临界温度以外经过验证的实验训练数据，制约了数据驱动的超导发现。

Method: 使用大语言模型从超过47000篇arXiv预印本中提取8个超导参数，构建AutoSuperCon数据库（6793种超导体、20354条记录），并与NIMS SuperCon等参考数据集交叉验证（R²=0.96）；在此基础上分析超导物理规律，并训练基于组成的临界温度与上临界场预测模型，采用迁移学习提升性能。

Result: AutoSuperCon与NIMS SuperCon一致性达R²=0.96；分析发现能隙比中位数从常规超导体的3.6升至铜氧化物的6.4，28%的上临界场超过Pauli极限，临界温度存在与其超流密度成正比的上限；训练的组成预测器临界温度达R²_log=0.83、上临界场达R²_log=0.69，且迁移学习提升了性能。

Abstract: New superconductors could unlock next-generation technologies from nuclear fusion to lossless electronics. Machine learning can predict candidate superconductors but lacks validated experimental training data beyond the critical temperature. Here we present AutoSuperCon, a database of eight superconducting parameters, containing 20354 entries for 6793 superconductors. The data were extracted from over 47000 arXiv preprints using a large language model and validated against reference datasets ($R^2 = 0.96$ with NIMS SuperCon). Using AutoSuperCon, we show that median gap ratios rise from 3.6 in conventional superconductors to 6.4 in cuprates, 28% of upper critical fields exceed the Pauli limit, and critical temperatures lie below a ceiling proportional to the superfluid density. Further, we train composition-based predictors for the critical temperature ($R^2_{\log}=0.83$) and the upper critical field ($R^2_{\log}=0.69$), boosted by transfer learning. Within known superconducting families, composition alone is sufficient to screen candidates for both parameters. AutoSuperCon thus enables physical insight and data-driven discovery across thousands of superconductors.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期二维材料中手性超导体发现的启发，研究无序（disorder）对具有非平庸能带几何材料的超导性的影响。

Conclusion: 非平庸能带几何显著改变了无序对超导的影响；扭曲MoTe2的BCS配对机制与实验电阻率数据存在矛盾；BCS超导体在弱无序下不存在涡旋玻璃相。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent discovery of chiral superconductors in 2d materials, we study the effects of disorder on superconductivity in materials with non-trivial band geometry. First, we show how Anderson's theorem and the conventional Abrikosov-Gorkov description of disorder-induced pair breaking are modified by the structure of the normal-state Bloch functions. We apply these results to twisted MoTe2 and show that a BCS pairing mechanism for the observed superconductor is in tension with the measured normal state residual resistivity. Second, we study whether random potential disorder can nucleate superconducting vortices in a chiral time- reversal-breaking superconductor. We show that, for a superconductor stabilized by the usual BCS mechanism, weak disorder does not induce a vortex glass phase, in contrast to superconductivity formed from an anyon fluid.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 幂律温度依赖的各向同性与残余损耗的各向异性提示UTe₂可能为拓扑超导体，需结合各向同性幂律与各向异性残余损耗来理解其配对与拓扑性质。

Abstract: We explore the pairing mechanism and possible topological properties of the unconventional superconductor UTe$_2$ through their influence on both the inductive and resistive channels using resonant cavity perturbation techniques. We measure a composite surface impedance for a variety of UTe$_2$ single crystals at microwave frequencies, where the inductive and resistive channels are comparable and can be treated on equal footing. By studying the composite electrodynamics for a large number of resonant modes, we are able to provide a comprehensive picture of the directional dependence of the excitations out of the ground state. We find similar power-law temperature dependencies at low-temperature for both the penetration depth and surface resistance, which in aggregate across modes, are quadratic and isotropic. On the other hand, we find a distinct and systematic anisotropy of the residual loss. The power-law results are inconsistent with a single pair of point nodes on the Fermi surface under weak scattering. We discuss the possibility of topological superconductivity as an interpretation of these isotropic power-laws in conjunction with the anisotropic residual loss.

### [88] [Quantum geometry drives orbital paramagnetism in superconducting fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16387)
*Zi-Ting Sun,Ying-Ming Xie,Chunli Huang,Naoto Nagaosa*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用高斯涨落理论研究发现，多分量超导体在临界温度以上时，除了常规的Cooper对质心运动导致的抗磁涨落外，还存在由序参量内部分量干涉引起的顺磁贡献，其源于Cooper对的量子几何。在保持时间反演对称的kagome超导模型中，顺磁贡献可压过抗磁贡献，产生净的涨落顺磁性；而破坏轨道时间反演对称后，单一低能模式主导涨落，抗磁性恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，超导体在转变温度以上由于短暂形成的Cooper对而表现出随趋近转变温度发散的轨道抗磁磁化率。作者希望探索多分量超导体中是否存在超越这一常规图像的磁响应行为，并揭示其物理起源。

Method: 采用高斯涨落理论研究转变温度以上的通用多分量超导体，将磁响应分解为两类竞争贡献：Cooper对质心运动的常规抗磁贡献，以及不同序参量分量之间干涉产生的顺磁贡献。后者与描述Cooper对内部分量随动量变化的量子几何相联系。进而将该理论应用于具有两个手性配对模式的kagome超导体模型，并考察时间反演对称性保持与破坏两种情形。

Result: 在父态保持时间反演对称时，顺磁贡献可超过传统抗磁贡献，产生净的涨落顺磁性。当轨道时间反演对称被破缺（例如形成弱环流序）时，两个手性模式的简并被解除；随冷却趋近超导转变，低能模式主导涨落，体系过渡到单分量物理，抗磁性重新占优。

Conclusion: 提出了一种超导体中涨落顺磁性的产生机制，并表明轨道磁响应可作为探测Cooper对量子几何的手段。

Abstract: Superconductors expel magnetic fields through the Meissner effect. Even slightly above the superconducting transition temperature, electrons can briefly form Cooper pairs, leading to an orbital diamagnetic susceptibility that diverges as the transition is approached. In this work, we use Gaussian fluctuation theory to study a generic multicomponent superconductor above its transition temperature and show that fluctuations can instead produce a paramagnetic response. We identify two competing contributions: the usual diamagnetic contribution from the center-of-mass motion of Cooper pairs and a paramagnetic contribution from interference between different components of the superconducting order parameter. The latter is related to pair quantum geometry, which describes how the internal components of the Cooper pair change with momentum. We then apply our theory to a model of a kagome superconductor with two chiral pairing modes. When the parent state preserves time-reversal symmetry, the paramagnetic contribution can outweigh the conventional diamagnetic contribution, producing net fluctuation paramagnetism. However, when the orbital time-reversal symmetry is broken, e.g., by forming a weak loop-current order, the degeneracy between the two chiral modes is then lifted. Upon cooling toward the superconducting transition, the lower-energy mode dominates the fluctuations, and diamagnetism wins as single-component physics takes over. Our results provide a mechanism for fluctuation paramagnetism in superconductors and show that the orbital magnetic response can probe the quantum geometry of Cooper pairs.

</details>


### [89] [Noise-Protected Superconducting Qudits from Higher-Harmonic Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16563)
*Hamed Vakili*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出利用电流-相位关系的二次谐波，在含三个约瑟夫森结的超导环中实现受强保护的 qutrit（三能级量子比特），并依据二次谐波的正负给出两种设计方案，同时指出交流磁约瑟夫森结是可行的候选平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qutrit 相比 qubit 每个电路元件能存储更多信息，但与 qubit 一样对噪声敏感，须在用于容错量子计算前克服退相干问题。作者旨在寻找能同时强抑制弛豫的 qutrit 设计方案，并以二次谐波符号作为保护的设计原理。

Method: 利用超导约瑟夫森结电流-相位关系的二次谐波来构建三结超导环中的 qutrit，并针对二次谐波的不同符号分别设计：二次谐波为正时，采用结间存在小幅不对称的简单环路，实现可调谐、对称保护的 qutrit；二次谐波为负时，引入对称破缺项（如弱约瑟夫森二极管效应）实现保护。通过微观计算验证栅极电压可调控二次谐波符号，并分析电荷噪声、准粒子误差等影响。

Result: 两种设计在保护机制上各有取舍：第一种具备精确的电荷选择定则；第二种几乎对电荷噪声不敏感且其准粒子误差可被纠错。两者都需要较大的二次谐波作为前提。微观计算表明，在交流磁约瑟夫森结中栅极电压可选择二次谐波的符号，但此时栅极电压噪声会成为主要的退相干（退相位）来源。

Conclusion: 二次谐波的符号可作为实现受保护超导 qudit 的设计原则：正号时用简单不对称环路即可得到对称保护的 qutrit，负号时则需附加对称破缺项。具体选择取决于所用材料，且交流磁约瑟夫森结是满足大二次谐波需求的候选平台。

Abstract: Qudits, which generalize qubits to $d$ levels, store more information per circuit element than qubits. As with qubits, however, their sensitivity to noise must be overcome before they can be used for fault-tolerant quantum computing. Here we show that, by exploiting the second harmonic of the current--phase relation, a superconducting loop of three Josephson junctions can host qutrits strongly protected against relaxation, while dephasing limits their coherence. When the second harmonic is positive, a simple loop with a small asymmetry between the junctions provides a tunable, symmetry-protected qutrit. When it is negative, a protected qutrit can be realized by adding a small symmetry-breaking term, such as a weak Josephson diode effect. The two designs involve different trade-offs: the first has an exact charge selection rule, whereas the second is nearly insensitive to charge noise and its quasiparticle errors can be corrected. The preferable choice therefore depends on the materials used. Both require a sizable second harmonic to begin with, and we identify altermagnetic Josephson junctions as a candidate platform. Finally, microscopic calculations show that a gate voltage selects the sign of the second harmonic in these junctions, although gate-voltage noise then becomes the leading source of dephasing. These findings establish the sign of the second harmonic as a design principle for protected superconducting qudits.

</details>


### [90] [Sign changing pairing in single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 revealed by nonmagnetic impurity bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16679)
*Huimin Zhang,Zhuozhi Ge,Michael Weinert,Lian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描隧道显微镜/谱学技术，研究了分子束外延生长的单层FeSe/SrTiO₃的超导配对对称性，发现非磁性Fe空位缺陷引起的隙内束缚态表现出电子-空穴不对称性及磁场下的无劈裂行为，支持符号反转的非常规配对序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层FeSe/SrTiO₃中发现的高温超导引发了关于其配对对称性究竟是常规符号保持的s波还是非常规符号反转的广泛争论，需要实验手段加以判别。

Method: 采用分子束外延（MBE）生长单层FeSe/SrTiO₃，并利用扫描隧道显微镜/谱学（STM/STS）探测Fe面内非磁性Fe空位缺陷所诱导的隙内束缚态，分析其空间分布对称性与外加磁场下的响应。

Result: 观察到由非磁性Fe空位缺陷引起的稳健隙内束缚态，其空间分布呈现强烈的电子-空穴不对称：空穴态具有二重对称性，电子态具有四重对称性。施加磁场后，束缚态不发生能量移动或劈裂，与符号反转序参量一致。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持单层FeSe/SrTiO₃的配对序参量具有符号反转特征，即其超导配对为非常规（sign-changing）对称性，而非常规符号保持的s波。

Abstract: The discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in single layer FeSe epitaxially grown on SrTiO3(001) substrates has instigated extensive debate over whether its pairing symmetry is conventional sign-preserving s-wave or unconventional sign-changing. Here we probe the pairing state for single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 grown by molecular beam epitaxy using scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy. We observe robust in-gap bound states induced by non-magnetic Fe-vacancy defects within the Fe-plane, which exhibit strong spatial electron-hole asymmetry with two-fold symmetry in hole states and four-fold in electron states. The bound states exhibit no energy shift or splitting under an applied magnetic field, consistent with a sign-changing order parameter.

</details>

### [91] [High-Fidelity In Silico Dataset of Ordered Binary and Ternary MXenes with Predictive Synthesis Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14687)
*Ahmed H. Biby,Charles B. Musgrave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用r²SCAN泛函的DFT计算，构建了1,040种有序二元与三元无剥离MXene的高保真数据集，系统评估其热力学稳定性，发现所有MXene在平衡条件下均不可及，但高温、高压及特定碳化学势下部分结构可稳定化，为MXene的可合成性与数据驱动发现提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXene的定向合成面临巨大挑战，原因在于其结构与组分空间庞大，且缺乏能够反映合成条件的可靠稳定性描述符，导致实验发现新MXene效率低下。

Method: 采用r²SCAN泛函的密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，构建包含1,040种有序二元与三元无剥离MXene的数据集，涵盖单过渡金属MXene、面内有序双过渡金属MXene、面内有序金属双空位MXene和面外有序双过渡金属MXene。对每个结构计算形成吉布斯自由能、分解吉布斯自由能、分解产物、层对称群与凸包上方能量（energy above hull），并在高温（500–2000 K）、高压（10 GPa）及碳化学势区间下评估其热力学稳定性。

Result: 所有MXene在平衡条件下均热力学不可及；但115种以碳化物为主的MXene在高温（500–2000 K）下可热力学稳定，23种在高压（10 GPa）下稳定，另有Mn–Fe–C化学空间中的3种MXene在实验可行的碳化学势范围内稳定。研究揭示了整个MXene空间的稳定性趋势。

Conclusion: 该工作明确了MXene的可合成候选结构及其所需的合成条件，填补了数据驱动的MXene发现中的关键空白，并为开发机器学习稳定性描述符以及未来自主实验工作流程奠定了基础。

Abstract: Despite their promise, the targeted synthesis of novel MXenes remains challenging due to their vast structural and compositional space and the lack of reliable stability descriptors that account for synthesis conditions. To address this gap, we utilized density functional theory (DFT) with the r$^2$SCAN functional to develop a high-fidelity dataset of 1,040 ordered binary and ternary bare delaminated MXenes, including mono-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered double-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered metal-divacancy MXenes, and out-of-plane ordered double-transition-metal MXenes. For each structure, we calculated formation and decomposition Gibbs energies, decomposition products, layer symmetry groups, and energies above the hull. We find that all MXenes are thermodynamically inaccessible under equilibrium conditions. However, 115 predominantly carbide MXenes become thermodynamically stabilized at elevated temperatures (500--2000 K), and 23 are stabilized under high pressure (10 GPa). Additionally, three MXenes in the Mn--Fe--C chemical space are stabilized within an experimentally feasible range of carbon chemical potentials. These findings reveal stability trends across the MXene space and identify synthesis strategies that can guide experimental efforts. Our dataset provides a robust foundation for developing machine-learned stability descriptors and for guiding experimental efforts toward novel MXene discovery. By identifying synthesizable candidates along with their synthesis conditions, this work bridges a critical gap in data-driven approaches to MXene discovery and lays the foundation for future autonomous experimental workflows.

</details>


### [92] [Improving Nonequilibrium Thermal Conduction in CaF2 by Breaking the Phonon Bottleneck Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14957)
*Mingde Qu,Minghuan Song,Yuxing Wang,Wudi Wang,Chenbo Zhang,Jiamin Quan,Jun Xu,Zhongwei Zhang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出通过选择性激发声子（特别是横向声学TA模式）来打破激光材料CaF2中由纵向光学（LO）声子输运受阻导致的非平衡声子瓶颈效应，从而主动提升热管理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光光学材料的散热不仅取决于热导率，还取决于吸收能量在各类声子模式间的再分配。在CaF2中，热电子能量快速传递给输运能力弱的LO声子，形成非平衡声子瓶颈，限制有效散热。因此需要找到打破该瓶颈的策略。

Method: 结合电子-声子散射计算、声子玻尔兹曼输运分析和非平衡分子动力学模拟，识别出LO声子为优先接收热载流子能量的模式；并基于三声子散射相空间分析，提出选择性激发横向声学（TA）模式，以加速LO弛豫并促进能量向载热的声学模式转移。

Result: 持续激发LO声子会使热导率降低约12%；而在LO声子瓶颈受限状态下额外激发TA模式，可使热导率相比瓶颈状态提升约21%，超过未激发时的数值。

Conclusion: 控制分支间能量再分配能够抵消声子瓶颈引起的热量积累，为激光光学材料提供了一条通过选择性调控声子布居实现主动热管理的可行路径。

Abstract: Heat dissipation in laser optical materials depends not only on their thermal conductivity but also on how absorbed energy is redistributed among various phonon modes. In calcium fluoride (CaF2), rapid energy transfer from hot electrons to weakly propagating longitudinal optical (LO) phonons can create a nonequilibrium phonon bottleneck effect that limits efficient heat removal. Here, we propose selective phonon excitation as an effective strategy to break this bottleneck. Combining electron-phonon scattering calculations, phonon Boltzmann transport analysis, and nonequilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, we identify LO phonons as the primary modes that preferentially receive energy from hot carriers, leading to energy accumulation due to their inefficient transport and relaxation. Guided by three-phonon scattering phase-space analysis, selective excitation of transverse acoustic (TA) modes can accelerate LO relaxation and promote energy transfer toward heat-carrying acoustic modes. While sustained LO-phonon excitation reduces the thermal conductivity by approximately 12%, additional TA-mode excitation improves heat conduction by approximately 21% compared to the state limited by the LO-phonon bottleneck, surpassing the unexcited value. These findings demonstrate that controlling interbranch energy redistribution can counteract phonon-bottleneck-induced heat accumulation and reveal a working route for active thermal management in laser optical materials through selective control of phonon populations.

</details>


### [93] [Reversal of the Anomalous Hall Effect by Directional In-Plane Electric Field in Strained Bilayer MoO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14982)
*Wenpeng Wang,hang Shi,Xiangru Kong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文研究了应变双层MoO中，面内电场方向可选择性切换单谷自旋劈裂并反转反常霍尔电导率，揭示应变与电场协同调控谷电子和自旋电子性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状磁性材料具有可调的谷与自旋自由度，有望用于多功能自旋电子器件；但如何在单一材料中实现谷选择性自旋劈裂的开关以及反常霍尔响应的可逆调控仍是挑战。

Method: 作者基于第一性原理计算，研究双层MoO的两种稳定堆叠构型（AC1和AC2）；在AC2构型中施加双轴应变诱导狄拉克型能带交叉，再沿晶格a、b方向施加面内电场，分析其电子结构、自旋劈裂、贝里曲率分布与反常霍尔电导率；同时考察AC1构型在应变下的金属-拓扑绝缘体转变、量子化自旋霍尔电导、谷极化及压磁效应。

Result: 在AC2构型中，沿a、b方向的电场分别选择性地解除X、Y谷处的自旋简并，同时保持另一谷的自旋简并；谷选择开关伴随符号相反的贝里曲率重分布，产生可逆的反常霍尔响应。AC1构型在应变下表现出金属到拓扑绝缘体的转变，具有量子化自旋霍尔电导，并呈现谷极化和压磁现象。两种构型均能量与动力学稳定。

Conclusion: 应变与电场的结合效应可实现谷选择性自旋劈裂和反常霍尔响应的可逆调控，双层MoO是用于多功能自旋电子与谷电子器件的理想平台。

Abstract: Layered magnetic materials with tunable valley and spin degrees of freedom hold promise for multifunctional spintronic devices. Here, we demonstrate that the orientation of an in-plane electric field enables selective switching of single-valley spin splitting and reversal of the anomalous Hall conductivity in strained bilayer MoO. In particular, electric fields along the a and b directions selectively lift the spin degeneracy at the X and Y valleys, respectively, while preserving it at the other valley. This valley-selective switching is accompanied by a redistribution of Berry curvature with opposite signs, giving rise to a reversible anomalous Hall response. These effects are realized in the energetically and dynamically stable AC2 stacking configuration, where biaxial strain induces Dirac-like band crossings that enable subsequent electric-field control. Meanwhile, the AC1 stacking configuration, which is also energetically and dynamically stable, exhibits rich strain-driven phenomena, including a transition from metal to topological insulator with quantized spin Hall conductivity, as well as valley polarization and piezomagnetism. Our findings reveal a combined effect of strain and electric fields for controlling valley-selective spin splitting and anomalous Hall responses, highlighting bilayer MoO as a promising platform for multifunctional spintronic and valleytronic devices.

</details>


### [94] [Depth determination from core-loss 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15037)
*Michael Deimetry*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于动量分辨芯损四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM）数据的掺杂原子深度测定方法，并通过贝叶斯框架在低剂量条件下实现亚纳米级深度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越样品投影显微图的限制，确定掺杂剂在三维空间中的分布对于材料表征与开发至关重要；现有方法难以从投影数据中获取掺杂原子的深度信息。

Method: 利用动量分辨芯损4D-STEM数据，在Born近似下对探针位置做傅里叶变换，产生条纹，其频率编码掺杂原子与焦平面的距离；针对动力学散射降低条纹对比度的问题，通过偏离主晶带轴采集来部分缓解；针对芯损信号剂量极低的问题，采用贝叶斯框架进行深度推断。

Result: 在模拟中，取决于信背比，仅用100至1000个芯损计数即可实现纳米到亚纳米量级的掺杂深度测量精度。

Conclusion: 该方法结合动量分辨芯损4D-STEM与贝叶斯推断，能够在抑制动力学散射影响并应对低芯损剂量的条件下，实现高精度的掺杂原子三维深度定位，为材料的三维掺杂分布表征提供了新途径。

Abstract: Moving beyond specimen-projected micrographs in scanning transmission electron microscopy to determine the three-dimensional distribution of dopants is of vital importance for materials characterization and development. We present an approach for dopant depth determination using momentum-resolved core-loss four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy data. Within the Born approximation, Fourier transformation over probe position reveals fringes whose frequency encodes the dopant's distance from the focal plane. Two principal challenges arise in extending this approach to realistic experimental conditions. First, dynamical scattering degrades fringe contrast which can be partially mitigated by acquiring off major zone axes. Second, because core-loss doses are punishing, we use a Bayesian framework which, in simulation, achieves nanometre to sub-nanometre depth precision from only 100 to 1000 core-loss counts, depending on the signal-to-background.

</details>


### [95] [Defective $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15060)
*Jorge Vega Bazantes,Timo Lebeda,Zhiqiang Mao,Jianwei Sun*

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论结合meta-GGA泛函，研究了体相α-In₂Se₃的电子结构及Se-rich/In-rich条件下中性点缺陷（空位与反位）的形成，发现中间层Se空位在两种化学环境下均具有异常低的形成能，并从结构弛豫角度解释了其稳定性。
