  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-In₂Se₃作为一种具有铁电应用前景的二维范德华材料，其本征缺陷会显著影响结构与电子性质。然而对缺陷形成机制、尤其是中间层Se空位异常稳定性的微观起源尚缺乏深入的理论理解，需要更高精度的交换关联泛函加以阐明。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT），并使用最先进的meta-GGA（meta-generalized gradient approximation）交换关联泛函，计算体相α-In₂Se₃的电子结构，以及在Se-rich和In-rich两种生长条件下中性点缺陷（包括空位和反位）的形成能，并对其进行结构弛豫与对称性分析。

Result: 计算结果准确再现了纯净α-In₂Se₃的实验结构参数；发现中间层Se空位在Se-rich和In-rich两种化学环境下均表现出异常低的形成能。结构分析表明，该空位邻近的四配位In原子沿三重轴发生显著的局域弛豫，使其配位数由4降至3的同时仍保持C₃ᵥ格点对称性，从微观上解释了该缺陷的高度稳定性。

Conclusion: 中间层Se空位因邻近In原子在保持C₃ᵥ对称性下的局域弛豫（四配位→三配位）而具有异常低的形成能，在两种生长条件下均易形成，是α-In₂Se₃中关键的稳定本征缺陷，对理解其实际材料性质具有重要意义。

Abstract: $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ is a two-dimensional van der Waals material with promising applications involving ferroelectricity. However, the presence of native defects can significantly influence its structural and electronic properties. In this work, we employ density functional theory with a state-of-the-art meta-generalized gradient approximation (meta-GGA) exchange-correlation functional to investigate the electronic structure of bulk $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ and the formation of neutral point defects, including vacancies and antisites, under Se-rich and In-rich growth conditions. Our calculations reproduce the experimental structural parameters of the pristine material and reveal that the Se vacancy in the middle layer exhibits an unexpectedly low formation energy under both chemical environments. Structural analysis shows that the neighboring tetrahedrally coordinated In atom undergoes a substantial local relaxation along the three-fold axis, which preserves its $C_{3v}$ site symmetry even as its coordination is reduced from four to three, providing a microscopic explanation for the remarkable stability of this defect.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往第一性原理研究多局限于高对称低指数晶面，因为建模低对称高指数表面计算成本高、复杂度大；但表面形成能本质上由面内原子密度和断裂键强度决定，理论上并不排除高 Miller 指数表面的能量竞争力，因此亟需系统探索这些实际可能出现的晶面。

Result: 十五个最低能量的非极性面中有九个是高指数面；每种化合物五个最佳候选面的表面能仅比最小值高出 2.5–5.3%。但能量接近并不转化为电子能带对齐相似：GaN 的两个最稳定表面形成能仅差 0.08 J/m²，真空参考带边位置却相差近 0.8 eV；LaN 的近简并 m 面和 a 面相差 0.16 eV。相近表面能的纤锌矿晶面可具有显著不同的电离势和电子亲和势。

Conclusion: 本文实现了对实际可能出现的纤锌矿晶面稳定性与能带对齐的联合系统评估，绘制了候选表面图谱，为相关纳米结构与异质界面的理性设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Surfaces and interfaces govern the performance of wurtzite semiconductors in electronics, sensing, and solar-fuel conversion. To date, first-principles studies have focused mostly on high-symmetry low-index facets, owing to the computational cost and complexity of modelling low-symmetry high-index surfaces. In practice, however, surface formation energy is determined by the planar atomic density and the strength of the bonds severed upon cleavage, neither of which inherently excludes high-Miller-index surfaces from being energetically competitive. Here, we combine automated surface generation, machine-learning interatomic potential screening, and density functional theory to explore 226 clean, stoichiometric surface models of wurtzite GaN, LaN, and ZnO, spanning all Miller orientations with $|h|,|k|,|l| \le 3$ and their inequivalent terminations. We find that low-symmetry high-index prismatic facets are energetically competitive with respect to the conventional $m$- and $a$-planes: nine of the fifteen lowest-energy nonpolar surfaces are high-index, and the five best candidates of each compound lie within only 2.5-5.3% of the minimum surface energy. Hybrid-functional calculations reveal, however, that this energetic proximity does not translate into similar electronic alignment. In GaN, the two most stable surfaces differ by just 0.08 J m$^{-2}$ in formation energy but by almost 0.8 eV in vacuum-referenced band-edge positions, whereas in LaN the nearly degenerate $m$- and $a$-planes are separated by 0.16 eV. Wurtzite facets with similar formation energies may therefore exhibit very different ionization potentials and electron affinities. Our results enable the joint, systematic assessment of stability and band alignment across the facets that can actually occur in wurtzites, and provide a map of candidate surfaces for the rational design of related nanostructure and heterointerfaces.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, the electronic band structure and the unknown Luttinger-like parameters associated with the p-type conduction bands (CBs) of boron arsenide (BAs) are calculated and analyzed using both 24-band $k.p$ and density functional (DFT) theories. For the DFT study, Gaussian and plane-wave basis sets implemented in the CP2K code were used. Comparison with DFT and available experimental results shows that the 24-band k.p model accurately reproduces the known electronic properties of BAs. These results demonstrate that the 24-band k.p model accurately reproduces the electronic band structure and key band parameters over the investigated energy range. In addition, the 24-band $k.p$ Hamiltonian parameters are adjusted and we show that the effective masses and the band parameters are validated against previously published theoretical studies. These findings suggest that the $k.p$ method is computationally more efficient than DFT, making it suitable for modeling complex nanostructures and quantum wells based on BAs semiconductor.

Abstract: Lead-free halide perovskites based on Ge and Sn avoid the toxicity of Pb, but the two cations favor different structural distortions. Here, we map how these two instabilities compete across the full composition range of CsGe$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$Br$_3$ using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations with a charge-aware machine-learned interatomic potential trained on hybrid-functional data. At low temperature the alloy exhibits a morphotropic phase boundary between $x=0.60$ and $0.65$ that separates a ferroelectric monoclinic $Cc$ phase from a monoclinic $P2_1$ phase with mixed antipolar and polar off-centering, with both the tilt pattern and the off-centering order changing discontinuously. The composition-temperature phase diagram reproduces the known transition sequences and temperatures of both end members and reveals an intermediate tetragonal $I4/mcm$ phase near the boundary that has no counterpart in either parent compound. Above the transition temperatures the $B$ cations remain locally off-centered but orientationally disordered. The structural transition at the boundary causes a jump in the band gap at low temperature, whereas at $350$ K the gap decreases smoothly with Sn content. These results establish $B$-site mixing as a means to tune polar and rotational order in lead-free halide perovskites.

TL;DR: 本文对39种二元主族金属氧化物多晶型的氧空位形成能进行了系统性r²SCAN密度泛函理论计算，并建立了一个仅需体相性质和能隙即可高精度预测空位形成能的可解释晶体特征模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧空位对氧化物材料（如固体氧化物燃料电池、太阳能热化学燃料生产）的功能至关重要。三元钙钛矿的空位形成能已有系统映射，但作为其概念与热化学构筑单元的二元氧化物，其空位形成能数据零散分布于使用不同计算方法的研究中，缺乏统一基准。

Conclusion: 该数据集为机器学习原子间势提供了基准，模型可仅凭体相性质估算主族氧化物的空位形成能，建立了二元氧化物氧空位形成能的统一参考体系。

Abstract: The function of oxide materials, from solid-oxide fuel cells to solar thermochemical fuel production, depends on their oxygen vacancies. Their formation energetics have been mapped systematically for ternary perovskites, but reported values for the binary oxides, their conceptual and thermochemical building blocks, remain scattered across studies with differing computational methods. Here we report a systematic r$^2$SCAN density functional theory survey of neutral oxygen vacancy formation energies across 39 binary main-group metal oxide polymorphs spanning groups 1, 2, 13, 14, and 15, using the ShakeNBreak method to locate reconstructed defect ground states. The formation energies span 8 eV and decrease down each group and, for most multivalent cations, with increasing oxidation state. Both trends follow the reducibility of the cation. An interpretable crystal feature model is fit to these energies; its inputs are properties of the pristine bulk, with the reduction potential alternatively taken from tabulated thermochemistry. Retaining only its two dominant descriptors, the crystal reduction potential and the band gap, the model reproduces the formation energies with a held-out mean absolute error of 0.31 eV, lower than that of its four-descriptor parent model for transition-metal perovskites. The bond-breaking and stability terms of the full Born-Haber fit prove statistically null for the binary oxides, and the largest residuals identify the hosts where electron accommodation departs from the cation-reduction picture that the descriptors encode. The dataset provides a benchmark for machine-learning interatomic potentials, and the model estimates vacancy formation energies of main-group oxides from bulk properties alone.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用新近发展的"归一化斜率平均法"（ANS）进行临界行为分析，在最高 7 T 的不同磁场窗口内提取临界指数（β、γ、δ），并分别沿易轴（B∥c）和面内难轴（B⊥c）方向施加磁场进行对比研究。

Result: B∥c 时体系表现为由双交换与巡游 RKKY 机制竞争主导的长程相互作用，随磁场增强被系统性抑制，并收敛至长程 3D Ising 普适类（β≈0.330，γ≈1.00）；B⊥c 时在整个测量磁场范围内呈现亚普适（γ<1）临界行为。

Conclusion: 非平庸范德华磁体的临界行为从根本上受外加磁场方向与磁晶各向异性强度的调制，这证明了相较于传统框架，开展磁场依赖的临界行为研究是必要的。

Abstract: We report a detailed investigation into the field-dependent critical behavior of the van der Waals ferromagnet $\mathrm{Fe_3GeTe_2}$ to elucidate how applied magnetic fields ($B$), strong magnetocrystalline anisotropy, and emerging other energy scales modulate its critical behavior. Utilizing the recently developed method, viz., the average of normalized slope (ANS), for analysis of critical behavior, we evaluate the critical exponents ($β$, $γ$, and $δ$) across distinct field windows up to $\rm{7\,T}$. For $B$ applied along the crystal's easy axis ($B\parallel c$), the system exhibits long-range interactions dominated by competing double-exchange and itinerant Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) mechanisms, which is systematically suppressed with increasing $B$ to converge toward the long-range 3D Ising universality class ($β\sim0.330$, $γ\sim1.00$). Conversely, under an in-plane hard-axis configuration ($B\perp c$), the critical behavior study exhibits a sub-unity regime ($γ<1$) across the measured range of $B$. This behavior is attributed to the intense competitive interplay between the external Zeeman energy and strong out-of-plane magnetocrystalline anisotropy. Our findings demonstrate that critical behavior in nontrivial van der Waals magnets is fundamentally modulated by the orientation of $B$ and the strength of magnetocrystalline anisotropy, demonstrating the necessity of $B$-dependent critical behavior investigation over conventional frameworks.

</details>


### [101] [Materials research with a general-purpose agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15451)
*Paulo R. Pires,Théo Cavignac,Tiago F. T. Cerqueira,Hai-Chen Wang,Silvana Botti,Miguel A. L. Marques*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于约1500万个晶体密度泛函计算的开源数据库Alexandria，结合海量机器学习势结构、生成模型、通用原子间势和图神经网络，构建了含174亿反应的化学反应图，并将这一切交给一个通用语言模型智能体，使其能独立完成从自然语言提问到可执行实验方案的全流程材料发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现通常需要大量专业数据、模型与人工分析协同，单一工具难以完成从抽象需求到可验证实验的闭环。作者希望将大规模计算数据、机器学习模型和反应图整合，赋予通用语言模型智能体自主完成材料筛选、合成设计与实验规划的能力，从而验证"数据+模型+反应图"协同能否超越各单一部分的局限。

Method: 构建包含约1500万晶体DFT计算（PBE、PBEsol、SCAN、TB-mBJ）的Alexandria数据库，涵盖高压（最高150 GPa）弛豫结构、介电张量、约14.5万电子-声子计算及4亿多个机器学习势弛豫结构。在此数据上训练晶体结构生成模型、通用原子间势和十多种性质的图神经网络（从带隙到超导临界温度）。基于凸包计算193,416个稳定化合物间的174亿反应，形成化学反应图。然后将数据库、模型和反应图全部提供给一个通用语言模型智能体，让其无人值守地执行筛选、候选设计、合成方案拟定与实验验证等任务。

Result: 智能体独立完成了多项任务：筛选太阳能吸收材料并剔除合成能量释放太低的候选，写出含前驱体、坩埚和气氛的合成方案；在熟知的Ba-Zr-S体系中找到一个未被报道的硫化物，并设计可证实或排除它的实验；针对空气稳定性需求设计替代材料，并用生成模型产生的新结构进行测试。作为对照，仅靠网络搜索的同一智能体只能提出文献中已有的方案。结果表明，数据、模型与反应图的整合使通用智能体实现了从自然语言提问到实验室可执行实验的闭环。

Conclusion: 将大规模计算数据库、机器学习模型和化学反应图整合给通用语言模型智能体，能够实现任一单一工具无法完成的端到端材料发现——从简单的自然语言提问直接产出可运行的实验方案，标志着自主材料设计迈出重要一步。

Abstract: Over the last decade we have built Alexandria, an open database of about 15 million density-functional calculations of crystals. It includes PBE, PBEsol, SCAN and TB-mBJ results, structures relaxed at pressures up to 150 GPa, dielectric tensors and about 145,000 electron-phonon calculations, together with more than 400 million structures relaxed with machine-learned potentials. On these data we trained a generative model for crystal structures, a universal interatomic potential and graph neural networks for more than ten properties, from band gaps to superconducting critical temperatures. From the convex hull we computed a reaction graph of the 17.4 billion reactions between its 193,416 stable compounds. Here we give all of this to a general-purpose language-model agent. Working unattended, the agent screens the database for a solar absorber, drops a candidate whose synthesis releases too little energy, and writes a synthesis plan with precursors, crucible and atmosphere. Asked about the well-studied Ba-Zr-S system, it finds an unreported sulfide and designs the experiment that would confirm or rule it out. Asked for an air-stable alternative, it designs one and tests it against new structures from the generative model. With web search alone, the same agent proposes what the literature already discusses. Put together, the data, the models and the reaction graph let a general agent do what none of them does alone: go from a question in plain words to an experiment that a laboratory can run and test.

</details>


### [102] [Defect Topology Governs the Optical Response of Doped Amorphous Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15478)
*Ridwan Hussein,Chinonso Ugwumadu,Louise A. M. Rosset,Roxanne M. Tutchton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过杂化泛函密度泛函理论与线性响应计算，研究缺陷拓扑和化学掺杂对非晶硅光学响应的影响，发现掺杂敏感性不仅取决于缺陷浓度，还取决于缺陷特性与空间组织；具有全伸展4配位的网络对掺杂介电扰动最强，含3/5配位缺陷（缺陷分数7.4%）的网络会反转通常的硼主导响应而变得对磷更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷拓扑与化学掺杂对非晶硅光学响应的影响机制尚不明确，需要厘清结构因素（缺陷的配位特征与空间分布）如何与掺杂共同决定光学性质。

Method: 采用杂化泛函密度泛函理论结合线性响应计算，构建四个结构不同的连续随机网络模型，并引入替位式硼和磷掺杂，计算光学性质；结合Tauc分析和光学求和规则分析光谱权重的再分布。

Result: 计算再现了关键实验趋势；具有全伸展4配位的构型对掺杂的介电扰动最强，而含原生缺陷态的网络中掺杂效应更加多样；多数网络中硼对介电响应的扰动强于磷；但兼具3配位和5配位缺陷（总缺陷分数7.4%）的网络反转了硼主导的响应，变得对磷更敏感；Tauc与求和规则分析表明网络拓扑决定整体光学景观，掺杂主要影响带隙附近态并重新分配光谱权重。

Conclusion: 缺陷拓扑是连接非晶结构、掺杂活性与光学响应的微观调控参数，掺杂敏感度由缺陷浓度、缺陷特性及其空间组织共同决定。

Abstract: The influence of defect topology and chemical doping on the optical response of amorphous silicon (a-Si) remains unresolved. In this study, hybrid-functional density functional theory and linear-response calculations are employed to examine four structurally distinct continuous random networks with substitutional boron and phosphorus doping. The calculated optical properties reproduce key experimental trends and demonstrate that dopant sensitivity is determined not only by defect concentration, but also by the character and spatial organization of defects. A configuration with fully extended 4-fold coordination shows the strongest dielectric perturbation to doping, while dopant effects in networks with pre-existing defect states are more varied. For most networks, the dielectric response is more strongly perturbed by boron than phosphorus. Notably, a network having both 3- and 5-fold defects with a total defect fraction of 7.4% reverses the typical boron-dominant response of other cases, becoming more sensitive to phosphorus. Tauc and optical sum-rule analyses further indicate that network topology defines the broader optical landscape, whereas doping primarily affects states near the gap and redistributes spectral weight. These findings establish defect topology as a microscopic control parameter that links amorphous structure, dopant activity, and optical response in amorphous silicon.

</details>


### [103] [Brute-force molecular dynamics simulation of aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15664)
*Elisa Petroni,Dario Baratella,Omar Abou El Kheir,Andrea Redaelli,Marco Bernasconi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用机器学习原子间势，作者对非晶Ge2Sb2Te5在玻璃转变温度以下进行约100 ns的分子动力学模拟，发现老化过程中轴向键的Peierls畸变增强、原子迁移率下降、键寿命增加，但同极键未减少，并将Peierls畸变增强与DFT计算中观察到的带隙变宽联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变电子存储器中使用的Ge2Sb2Te5非晶相会随老化发生电阻增大，影响器件运行，而老化背后的结构弛豫机制尚不清楚。

Method: 使用机器学习原子间势进行分子动力学模拟：先在400 K平衡24 ns，随后在略低于玻璃转变温度下老化约100 ns；并利用密度泛函理论（DFT）在较小的老化非晶模型中计算电子带隙。

Result: 老化过程中轴向键的Peierls畸变增强，这与DFT计算中电子带隙变宽相关；同时原子迁移率明显降低、键寿命增加；但未观察到同极键的减少。

Conclusion: 老化导致的结构弛豫主要表现为Peierls畸变增强（伴随带隙变宽）和动力学冻结（迁移率降低、键寿命增加），而同极键的消除应发生在更长的时间尺度上，理想老化玻璃中应不存在同极键。

Abstract: Chalcogenide alloys such as the prototypical Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ compound are used in phase change electronic memories due to their ability to undergo a fast and reversible transition between the amorphous and crystalline phases upon heating. The amorphous phase is subject to aging that leads to an increase in the electrical resistance, which is detrimental to the operation of the devices. Using a machine learning interatomic potential, we have investigated the structural relaxations responsible for aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ in molecular dynamics simulations at temperatures slightly below the glass transition. The aging simulation, lasting about 100 ns, follows an equilibration of 24 ns at 400 K. During aging we observed an enhancement of the Peierls distortion in axial bonds which is a typical feature of these alloys that we relate to the widening of the electronic band gap computed within Density Functional Theory in smaller aged amorphous models. We also observed a marked reduction of the atomic mobility with an increase of the bond lifetimes during aging. However, we do not see a reduction in homopolar bonds, which are expected to be absent in the ideal aged glass and which should be removed on a much longer time scale.

</details>


### [104] [Competing lattice instabilities in epitaxially strained BaSnO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15550)
*Benyao Sun,Anna Grünebohm*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论系统研究了BaSnO3在双轴压应变下的晶格动力学，揭示其结构不稳定性由极模软化与八面体旋转不稳定性之间的竞争及非谐耦合共同决定；大应变可诱导一阶相变至超四方铁电极态，且电场或电子掺杂可降低临界应变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清BaSnO3在双轴压应变下的相稳定机制——传统上相稳定性常仅由极性声子模的软化推断，但BaSnO3中同时存在旋转不稳定性，需考察各不稳定性之间的层级关系与竞争，以正确预测应变诱发的结构相变。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）绘制双轴压应变下BaSnO3的晶格动力学图景；通过双模（two-mode）能量面分析考察极模与旋转序参量间的耦合性质；比较A点、M点八面体旋转不稳定性与Γ点极性模的软化顺序及能量收益；并评估电场和电子掺杂对模竞争临界应变的影响。

Result: 极模与旋转序参量以竞争方式相互作用；A点和M点八面体旋转在极性模之前失稳，并在中等应变下主导谐波响应，但对应连续相变、无非谐晶格耦合且能量增益有限；大应变可诱导一阶相变进入超四方（supertetragonal）极态，其极化大小和铁电能量阱深度可与压应变下的BaTiO3相当；电场或电子掺杂能扭转不同模之间的平衡，在更低的临界应变下有利于超四方结构而非反极性和旋转结构。

Conclusion: BaSnO3的相稳定性取决于多种晶格不稳定性之间的竞争及其与结构弛豫的非谐耦合，而非仅由极模软化决定；大应变下的超四方极性态具有与BaTiO3相当的铁电性能，且可通过电场或电子掺杂在较低应变下实现，为该体系应变工程调控提供了机理依据。

Abstract: We use density functional theory to map the lattice-dynamical landscape of BaSnO3 under biaxial compressive strain and uncover the hierarchy of structural instabilities. Our results demonstrate that the phase stability of strained BaSnO3 cannot be inferred from the softening of the polar mode alone. Instead it is governed by the competition among lattice instabilities and their anharmonic coupling to structural relaxation. Two-mode energy-surface analyses show that the polar and rotational order parameters interact predominantly competitively. While A- and M-point octahedral rotations become unstable before the zone-center polar mode and dominate the harmonic response for moderate values of strain, they are related to a continuous transition without anharmonic lattice coupling and a moderate gain of energy with strain. Large strain may induce a first order transition to a supertetragonal polar state. Once this state is stabilized, the magnitude of polarization and ferroelectric energy well depth are comparable to BaTiO3 under compressive strain. Furthermore, we discuss how an electric field or electron doping may tip the balance between the different modes and favor of the supertetragonal structure over antipolar and rotational alternatives at a reduced critical strain.

</details>


### [105] [Cation-intermixing driven evolution from a narrow avoided crossing to an enlarged inverted gap in Ge$_2$Bi$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15631)
*Alexander S. Frolov,Alexander V. Fedorov,Nadezhda V. Vladimirova,Sergey M. Aksenov,Mark A. Naumov,Savely V. Domenko,Artem A. Minaev,Vasily S. Stolyarov,Denis V. Vyalikh,Lada V. Yashina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过结合拓扑绝缘体Bi2Te3与铁电Rashba半导体GeTe的结构单元，合成Ge2Bi2Te5化合物，发现阳离子混排可显著扩大其倒置能隙，为拓扑电子结构的调控提供了新的自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过结合Bi2Te3（拓扑绝缘体）与GeTe（铁电Rashba半导体）的结构基元来合成新材料，并理解阳离子混排如何影响其拓扑电子结构，从而为设计功能量子材料提供新的调控手段。

Method: 合成了Ge2Bi2Te5化合物并进行全面表征；利用动量分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）和从头计算（ab initio）研究其电子结构，揭示阳离子混排（Ge、Bi原子在阳离子亚晶格上的重排）对能带演化的微观机制。

Result: Ge2Bi2Te5表现出显著的阳离子混排，电子结构从窄的avoided crossing演化为大幅增大的倒置能隙；其微观起源在于阳离子亚晶格中Ge与Bi的重分布，增加了价带顶附近Bi衍生态的贡献，增强了自旋-轨道耦合的有效作用，从而稳定了拓扑电子结构。

Conclusion: 阳离子无序（可在晶体生长过程中控制）为工程化拓扑电子结构提供了额外的自由度，为功能量子材料的开发开辟了新机遇。

Abstract: Combining the building blocks and structural motifs of the topological insulator Bi$_2$Te$_3$ and the ferroelectric Rashba semiconductor GeTe, we synthesized and comprehensively characterized the resulting Ge$_2$Bi$_2$Te$_5$ compound, which exhibits pronounced cation intermixing. Using momentum-resolved photoemission spectroscopy and ab initio calculations, we demonstrate how cation intermixing drives the evolution of its electronic structure from a narrow avoided crossing to a substantially enlarged inverted band gap. We establish the microscopic origin of this behavior in the redistribution of Ge and Bi atoms within the cation sublattice, which increases the contribution of Bi-derived states near the valence-band maximum and enhances the effective contribution of spin-orbit coupling, thereby stabilizing the topological electronic structure. Our findings demonstrate that cation disorder, which can be controlled during crystal growth, provides an additional degree of freedom for engineering topological electronic structures, opening new opportunities for the development of functional quantum materials.

</details>


### [106] [Metric random tilings: exact entropy and the diffraction of counted disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16730)
*Xinyan Wu,Qingqing Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出“度量随机镶嵌”（metric random tilings）概念，研究拓扑锁定、但边长在精确面闭合约束下涨落的周期性网络，并用专用计数引擎枚举开普勒的11种均匀网络，区分出零熵与正熵的度量载体，重点刻画蜂窝网络的度量熵及其对衍射的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机镶嵌（如晶体、准晶体）计数的是固定形状的排列方式，而非几何本身；但实际材料的化学设计设定的是度规、衍射测量的是度规。作者希望把“可数性”从排列扩展到几何本身，即让边长涨落成为可计数的热力学变量，并将度量无序纳入精确计数范式。

Method: 引入度量随机镶嵌：周期性网络保持拓扑锁定，边长在精确面闭合条件下涨落，使几何本身可计数。构建专用计数引擎枚举开普勒的11种均匀网络，将其划分为7种零熵网络和4种具有正度量熵的载体，每个正熵载体都通过显式构造认证。重点在最致密的蜂窝网络上对所有边长取值域作熵的精确计数与拟合，并分析衍射中布拉格强度相干分数随取值域增大的变化及振幅流的德拜—瓦勒定律。

Result: 在蜂窝网络（最致密载体）上，所有边长取值域均具有广延熵：算术类达到每瓦片 s=0.544 kB（拟合带0.53–0.58），其余两类约0.2。平移增大取值域时熵严格不变，但在固定斑块尺寸下布拉格强度的相干分数从9×10⁻⁴变化到0.58，遵循振幅流的德拜—瓦勒定律。

Conclusion: 度量无序由此进入精确计数范式，并可通过真实材料中“被度量晕圈包裹的布拉格斑点”加以识别。

Abstract: Tiling mathematics has twice become the language of a state of matter: crystals, then quasicrystals, whose thermodynamics are described by the random tiling of fixed shapes. There the counted variable is the arrangement rather than the geometry, although the metric is what chemical design sets and diffraction measures. Here we introduce metric random tilings, periodic networks of locked topology whose edge lengths fluctuate under exact face closure, so that the geometry itself is countable. Purpose-built counting engines enumerate Kepler's eleven uniform networks and divide them into seven zero-entropy ones and four carriers with positive metric entropy, each certified by an explicit construction. On the honeycomb, the densest carrier, all edge-length value domains considered carry extensive entropy, with the arithmetic class reaching $s = 0.544\,k_B$ per tile (fit band 0.53--0.58), the other two $\approx 0.2$. In diffraction, translational increase of the value domain leaves the entropy exactly invariant while the coherent fraction of the Bragg intensity at fixed patch size varies from $9\times10^{-4}$ to 0.58, following the Debye--Waller law of the amplitude flow. Metric disorder thus enters the exact-counting paradigm, and is identifiable in real materials by Bragg spots wrapped in metric halos.

</details>


### [107] [Zero-Field Optical Readout of Quintet States in Through-Space Coupled Tetracene Oligomers at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15713)
*Jeannine Grüne,Maciej Majdecki,William K. Myers,Przemysław Gaweł,Hannah L. Stern*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在化学可模块化、通过空间耦合的并四苯低聚物中，实现室温、零磁场下的五重态光学读出，并结合EPR证实室温相位记忆时间达446 ns，说明构象动力学与自旋相干可兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学可寻址的电子自旋是量子技术的理想平台，分子体系可对其自旋与光学性质进行化学调控。实现室温、无外加磁场下分子自旋态的光学读出是实际应用的关键一步。单线态裂分产生的五重态是有前景的光学可寻址多能级qudit，但其在零场与弱场下的本征自旋跃迁尚缺乏研究，且显著的室温五重态相干此前主要在结构受限的分子骨架中实现。

Method: 作者将零场光学检测磁共振（zero-field ODMR）与瞬态及脉冲电子顺磁共振（EPR）相结合，研究构象柔性、通过空间耦合的并四苯低聚物中五重态的磁场依赖性与相干自旋性质。在并四苯二聚体与六聚体中实现室温零场光学读出，并测绘光检测跃迁对弱外加磁场的响应；利用EPR支持五重态通过随机交换涨落形成，并测定室温相位记忆时间。

Result: 在并四苯二聚体和六聚体中均实现了室温零场下的五重态光学读出，并测绘了光检测跃迁对弱磁场的响应。EPR测量表明五重态通过随机交换涨落形成，并测得室温相位记忆时间为446 ns，证明促成高自旋形成的构象动力学仍与可观的室温自旋相干兼容。

Conclusion: 该工作在一个化学可模块化、通过空间耦合的分子骨架中整合了零场光学读出、弱场响应与室温自旋相干，拓展了光学可寻址分子qudit和量子传感器的设计空间。

Abstract: Optically addressable electron spins are promising platforms for quantum technologies, with molecular systems offering chemical control over their spin and optical properties. Achieving optical readout of molecular spin states at room temperature and without an applied magnetic field is an important step towards their practical implementation. Quintet states generated via singlet fission are promising optically addressable multilevel qudits, but their intrinsic spin transitions at zero and weak magnetic fields remain largely unexplored, while substantial room-temperature quintet coherence has predominantly been demonstrated in structurally constrained molecular architectures. Here, we combine zero-field optically detected magnetic resonance (zero-field ODMR) with transient and pulsed electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) to investigate the magnetic-field dependence and coherent spin properties of quintet states in conformationally flexible, through-space coupled tetracene oligomers. We demonstrate zero-field optical readout of quintet states at room temperature in both tetracene dimers and hexamers, and map the response of optically detected transitions to weak applied magnetic fields. Complementary EPR measurements support quintet formation through stochastic exchange fluctuations and demonstrate a room-temperature phase-memory time of 446 ns, showing that the conformational dynamics enabling high-spin formation remain compatible with substantial spin coherence. Together, these results integrate field-free optical readout, weak-field response and room-temperature spin coherence within a chemically modular, through-space coupled molecular architecture, expanding the design space for optically addressable molecular qudits and quantum sensors.

</details>


### [108] [Chiral-phonon-induced anomalous Hall effect in bilayer MoSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15761)
*Omer Yaniv,Swati Chaudhary,Carolina Paiva,Dominik M. Juraschek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 文章证明：在非磁性、中心对称的双层MoSe₂中，通过圆偏振光驱动手性声子，可产生动态Rashba自旋轨道耦合，使Floquet能带在两个谷上携带同号Berry曲率，从而诱发反常霍尔响应；霍尔电导率约为10⁻²–0.21 e²/2πħ，符号由声子手性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在缺乏磁性和破缺反演对称性的材料中，如何利用光驱动的手性声子这一非磁性、超快手段来控制电子Berry曲率与输运性质，从而为超快调控反常霍尔效应开辟新途径。

Method: 作者以双层MoSe₂为研究对象，先用第一性原理计算参数化一个有效的k·p哈密顿量；随后考虑圆偏振光激发E_u声子，产生圆动态（circular dynamical）Rashba自旋轨道耦合；再基于Floquet理论分析周期驱动下的能带结构，计算两谷上的Berry曲率及其对输运的贡献，并评估实验可行光脉冲下霍尔电导率的大小。

Result: 圆激发E_u声子生成了圆动态Rashba自旋轨道耦合，所得Floquet能带在两个谷上携带同号Berry曲率，从而产生符号随声子手性变化的霍尔响应；在实验可及的光脉冲下，σ_xy约为10⁻²到0.21 e²/2πħ。

Conclusion: 手性声子为在超快时间尺度上调控电子Berry曲率与输运（特别是反常霍尔响应）提供了一条无需磁性的新路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that optically driven chiral phonons induce an anomalous Hall response in a nonmagnetic, centrosymmetric material. Using first-principles calculations to parameterize an effective $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ Hamiltonian for bilayer MoSe$_2$, we show that circular excitation of an $E_u$ phonon generates a circular dynamical Rashba spin-orbit coupling. The resulting Floquet bands carry same-sign Berry curvature at the two valleys, producing a Hall response whose sign follows the phonon handedness. We obtain a range of $σ_{xy}\sim10^{-2}-0.21$ $\frac{e^2}{2π\hbar}$ for experimentally accessible light pulses. Our results show that chiral phonons provide a route to controlling electronic Berry curvature and transport on ultrafast time scales.

</details>


### [109] [Dislocation Arrays Hosting Topological Bound States in Epitaxial Tetragonal FeSe Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16681)
*Huimin Zhang,Basu Oli,Zhengfei Wang,Michael Weinert,Lian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究晶体材料中拓扑缺陷对量子态的稳定局域化作用，并探讨如何实现对其形成的确定性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缺陷能够承载鲁棒的局域量子态，可用于量子信息处理和传感，但在晶体材料中实现对缺陷形成的确定性控制仍然十分困难。

Method: 原文摘要仅提供了研究背景陈述，尚未给出具体的研究方法。

Result: 原文摘要仅提供了研究背景陈述，尚未给出具体的研究结果。

Conclusion: 原文摘要仅提供了研究背景陈述，尚未给出具体结论。

Abstract: Topological defects in real space can host robust, localized quantum states for quantum information processing and sensing, yet deterministic control over their formation in crystalline materials remain challenging.

</details>


### [110] [A Hierarchical Harmonic-to-Anharmonic Framework for ab initio Screening and Mechanistic Analysis of Negative Thermal Expansion in ZrV2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15767)
*J. Bustamante,A. Miliute,N. Jaykhedkar,A. A. Naik,J. Grandel,F. Emmerling,T. M. Stawski,J. George*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出分层计算策略（QHA + TDEP + ML-MD）研究 ZrV2O7 的负热膨胀（NTE），并用 XRD 验证，揭示其准刚性单元模（QRUM）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 框架材料中的负热膨胀源于复杂的晶格动力学，传统理论方法难以捕捉，需要更有效的计算策略来筛选和阐明 NTE 材料及其微观机制。

Method: 建立分层计算策略：准谐近似（QHA）识别负 Grüneisen 参数的低声频声子模作为低成本筛选描述符；温度相关有效势（TDEP）纳入有限温度非谐性；机器学习加速的从头算分子动力学（ML-MD）揭示温度依赖的结构畸变，并辅以 X 射线衍射（XRD）实验测量验证。

Result: QHA 成功识别出负 Grüneisen 参数的低声频模；TDEP 和 ML-MD 揭示了驱动晶格收缩的温度依赖结构畸变；横向位移（uT）随温度同步增大，为高温下 NTE 的准刚性单元模（QRUM）机制提供了微观证据。

Conclusion: 低成本晶格动力学描述符可用于筛选潜在 NTE 材料，而更高层次的非谐模拟能在原子尺度解析其结构机制；该机制与此前 ZrW2O7 等框架材料中集体多面体旋转和横向原子运动导致 NTE 的报道一致。

Abstract: Negative thermal expansion (NTE) in framework materials arises from complex lattice dynamics that are challenging to capture using conventional theoretical approaches. Here, we establish a hierarchical computational strategy for investigating NTE in ZrV2O7, integrating the quasiharmonic approximation (QHA), temperature-dependent effective potentials (TDEP), and machine-learning-accelerated ab initio molecular dynamics (ML-MD). Quasi-harmonic lattice-dynamical calculations identify low-frequency phonon modes with negative Grüneisen parameters as inexpensive screening descriptors, while TDEP incorporates finite-temperature anharmonicity and ML-MD further reveals temperature-dependent structural distortions that drive the lattice contraction. The observations are further corroborated with X-ray diffraction (XRD) measurements. The concurrent increase in transverse displacement (uT) provides microscopic evidence for a quasi-rigid-unit-mode (QRUM) mechanism underlying the NTE behaviour at elevated temperatures. This is consistent with previous reports on other framework materials, including ZrW2O7, where collective polyhedral rotations and transverse atomic motions have been linked to NTE. Overall, our results demonstrate how relatively inexpensive lattice-dynamical descriptors can identify potential NTE materials, while higher-level anharmonic simulations can then resolve the underlying structural mechanisms at the atomic scale.

</details>


### [111] [Evidential Deep Regression for Universal Density Functional Theory Hamiltonian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15786)
*Jiaxuan Liu,Yan Zhang,Caiyuan Ye,Tiannian Zhu,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Quansheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 EviDTB，一种基于证据深度回归的方法，用于在单次前向传播中为紧束缚 DFT 哈密顿量同时给出快速预测与不确定性估计，并通过不确定性引导筛选加速材料电子性质搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 DFT 哈密顿量模型能高效地从原子结构预测电子性质，但如何在快速预测哈密顿量的同时可靠地估计预测不确定性仍是未解决的难题。

Method: 提出 EviDTB（evidential deep regression，证据深度回归）方法，在单次前向传播中同时输出预测值及不确定性估计；并与深度集成（deep ensembles）、蒙特卡洛 dropout、均值-方差估计（MVE）在计算成本与不确定性量化性能之间进行对比。

Result: 在 5,478 个留出测试结构上，EviDTB 的全自旋-轨道耦合哈密顿量矩阵元平均绝对误差为 0.622 meV；其单原子平均不确定性与平均局部平方预测误差的 Spearman 相关系数 ρ=0.812。剔除 15% 最不确定结构后，该误差的第 99 百分位从 16.63 eV² 降至 3.72 eV²，降幅达 78%。

Conclusion: EviDTB 在计算成本与不确定性量化性能之间取得更好平衡，其不确定性评分可有效用于优先筛选需要第一性原理验证的候选结构，有望加速对具有理想电子性质材料的搜索。

Abstract: Universal density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonian models trained across broad chemical and structural spaces offer an efficient route from atomic structure to electronic properties. However, estimating prediction uncertainty alongside fast Hamiltonian predictions remains a challenge. In this work, we present EviDTB, an evidential deep regression approach for tight-binding DFT Hamiltonians that effectively estimates prediction uncertainty in a single forward pass. Compared with deep ensembles, Monte Carlo dropout, and mean-variance estimation (MVE), EviDTB achieves a better balance between computational cost and uncertainty-quantification performance. On 5,478 held-out test structures, EviDTB achieves a full spin--orbit-coupled (SOC) Hamiltonian matrix-element mean absolute error of 0.622meV. Its mean uncertainty per atom correlates with the mean local squared prediction error (Spearman $ρ=0.812$); removing the 15% most uncertain structures reduces the 99th percentile of this error by 78%, from 16.63 to 3.72eV$^2$. This score offers a practical way to prioritize candidates for first-principles verification. We hope that combining fast Hamiltonian prediction with uncertainty-guided screening will accelerate the search for materials with desirable electronic properties.

</details>


### [112] [Lithium redistribution and electrode degradation in commercial NMC/Graphite and LCO/Graphite cells under high-rate cycling conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15818)
*Naisargi Kanabar,Nicholas Fusco,Seiichiro Higashiya,Devendra Sadana,Stephen Bedell,Haralabos Efstathiadis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过对比NMC811/石墨与LCO/石墨两种商业锂离子电池在不同倍率（0.3C–3C）下的循环性能，结合SEM、XRD和NRA表征，揭示了阴极化学与充电协议对快速充电诱导衰减的耦合影响，发现两种体系均在1C–2C之间出现循环稳定性转折，且2C时石墨阳极锂累积最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速充电会加速锂离子电池衰减，但阴极化学与充电协议的影响难以解耦，亟需在同一实验框架下系统比较不同阴极体系在高倍率循环中的退化行为。

Method: 将商业NMC811/石墨（200 mAh）和LCO/石墨（45 mAh）纽扣电池在0.3C、1C、2C和3C下各循环100次，随后采用扫描电子显微镜（SEM）、X射线衍射（XRD）和核反应分析（NRA）对循环后电极进行表征，以关联电化学性能与结构和锂分布变化。

Result: 两种电池均在1C与2C之间出现循环稳定性转折。NMC811/石墨在0.3C和1C下容量保持率约99.7%，2C降至72.3%，3C为63.5%；LCO/石墨在2C仅保持11.1%。NRA显示两种化学体系均在2C时石墨阳极锂累积最大，SEM观测到颗粒状表面沉积物支持该结果。XRD显示阴极(003)和石墨(002)反射随倍率发生偏移，表明循环中发生结构变化；3C时锂累积较低与电荷通过量减少有关，而LCO/石墨的3C条件受其充电协议限制。

Conclusion: 该研究表明阳极侧退化与充电协议是解释商业锂离子电池高倍率循环性能的关键因素，提示在评价快速充电性能时应综合考虑阴极化学与充电策略的相互作用。

Abstract: Fast charging accelerates degradation in lithium-ion batteries, but the effects of cathode chemistry and charging protocol remain difficult to separate. Here, commercial NMC811/graphite (200 mAh) and LCO/graphite (45 mAh) coin cells were cycled for 100 cycles at 0.3C, 1C, 2C, and 3C, followed by scanning electron microscopy (SEM), X-ray diffraction (XRD), and nuclear reaction analysis (NRA). Both cell types exhibited a transition in cycling stability between 1C and 2C. NMC811/graphite retained approximately 99.7% of its initial discharge capacity at 0.3C and 1C, decreasing to 72.3% at 2C and 63.5% at 3C, while LCO/graphite retained only 11.1% at 2C. NRA revealed maximum lithium accumulation in graphite anodes at 2C for both chemistries, supported by SEM observations of granular surface deposits. XRD showed rate-dependent shifts in cathode (003) and graphite (002) reflections, indicating structural changes during cycling. The lower lithium accumulation at 3C was associated with reduced charge throughput, while the LCO/graphite 3C condition was limited by its charging protocol. These findings highlight the importance of anode-side degradation and charging protocols in interpreting high-rate cycling performance of commercial lithium-ion batteries.

</details>


### [113] [A New High $γ$-$γ'$ Fraction Polycrystalline Co/Ni Superalloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15969)
*Cameron Crabb,Mark C. Hardy,Michelle S. Conroy,David Dye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一种可铸锭加工（ingot-processable）的Co/Ni基γ/γ′高温合金，用于航空发动机变形部件，兼具优异的高温强度、蠕变抗力和抗氧化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于航空发动机变形部件的镍基高温合金虽性能优良，但需要开发兼具可加工性、高温强度、蠕变性能和抗氧化性的新型合金体系；Co/Ni基γ/γ′合金有望填补这一需求，但需验证其铸锭加工可行性与长期组织稳定性。

Method: 采用8.5 kg真空感应熔炼（VIM）铸锭，经均匀化处理后热轧至真应变1；开发热处理工艺以获得50% γ′相和142 μm晶粒。随后进行700–850 °C高温拉伸、750 °C/600 MPa及800 °C/300 MPa蠕变、800 °C/100 h氧化，以及800 °C/8000 h长期时效等测试，并表征显微组织。

Result: 高温拉伸屈服强度为：700 °C时820 MPa、800 °C时785 MPa、850 °C时783 MPa；蠕变试验显示最低蠕变速率降低、断裂寿命延长（优于现有镍基变形合金）；800 °C氧化100 h形成约1 μm的双层Cr₂O₃/Al₂O₃氧化膜，亚表层退化极小；8000 h长期时效后γ′相粗化有限，且无有害金属间化合物或晶界相析出。

Conclusion: 该Co/Ni基γ/γ′合金在可加工性、强度与抗氧化性之间实现了良好平衡，适用于航空发动机高温静止与旋转部件。

Abstract: An ingot-processable Co/Ni-based $γ/γ'$ superalloy has been developed for wrought parts in aircraft engines. An 8.5 kg VIM ingot was homogenised and hot rolled to a true strain of 1. A heat treatment was then developed to produce 50% $γ'$ and a grain size of 142 $μ$m. High-temperature tensile testing showed yield strengths of 820 MPa at 700 $^\circ$C, 785 MPa at 800 $^\circ$C and 783 MPa at 850 $^\circ$C. Creep tests at 750 $^\circ$C/600 MPa and 800 $^\circ$C/300 MPa demonstrated reduced minimum creep rates and extended rupture lives relative to current wrought Ni-base alloys. Oxidation at 800 $^\circ$C for 100 h formed a 1 $μ$m dual chromia/alumina scale with minimal subsurface degradation. Long-term exposure at 800 $^\circ$C for 8000 h showed limited $γ'$ coarsening and no deleterious intermetallic or grain boundary phases. The alloy therefore offers a promising balance of processability, strength and oxidation resistance for high-temperature static and rotating components.or grain boundary phases. The alloy therefore offers a promising balance of processability, strength and oxidation resistance for high-temperature static and rotating components.

</details>


### [114] [Quantifying sample heating by ablation-plasma x-rays in laser-driven shock experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16054)
*R. F. Smith,I. K. OCampo,S. Singh,T. M. Hutchinson,T. E. Lockard,J. H. Eggert,C. P. McGuire,A. L. Coleman,J. M. McNaney,S. Pandolfi,B. Nagler,D. McGonegle,H. J. Lee,D. Khaghani,N. Czapla,A. Krygier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过独特的实验设计，在压力波到达前对样品进行X射线预热，结合原位X射线衍射定量测量了不同激光强度与烧蚀层配置下金、钛、铁样品的温升，揭示X射线预热的影响规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光驱动动态压缩实验中，烧蚀等离子体发射的X射线会导致样品预热，这一未知加热长期以来使实验解读复杂化，尤其是压力驱动与样品加热同时发生、难以区分，给实现明确热力学状态（如主雨贡纽）带来不确定性。

Method: 采用独特的实验设计，使样品在压力波到达之前先被X射线加热；利用原位X射线衍射将晶格膨胀与温度关联，定量测量1微米厚Au样品在使用常用的25微米和75微米厚Kapton-B（聚酰亚胺）烧蚀层、不同激光辐照强度下的温升；并考察了0.2微米Al闪镀层的增强效应，以及75微米厚无Al涂层Kapton-B对Ti、Fe样品的加热抑制效果。

Result: 25微米厚聚酰亚胺烧蚀层下观测到足以熔化Au的加热（ΔT约1050 K），且添加0.2微米Al闪镀层会显著增强加热；相比之下，75微米厚无Al涂层Kapton-B可强烈抑制加热，在高达1.2×10^13 W/cm^2的激光辐照下，2.5微米厚Ti和1微米厚Fe样品未观测到可测量的加热。

Conclusion: X射线预热程度强烈依赖于烧蚀层厚度与Al涂层配置：较薄烧蚀层和Al涂层会显著增强预热，而较厚无Al涂层的Kapton-B能有效抑制加热，为优化激光驱动动态压缩实验设计、降低预热不确定性提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Sample preheat due to absorption of x-rays emitted by an ablation plasma has long been a source of uncertainty in laser-driven dynamic compression experiments. This unknown heating complicates interpretation of experiments designed to reach well-defined thermodynamic states, such as the principal Hugoniot, particularly because the pressure drive and sample heating occur simultaneously and are difficult to disentangle. Here, we quantify sample heating from x-rays using a unique experimental design in which the sample is heated prior to the arrival of the pressure wave. Using in situ x-ray diffraction, we relate lattice expansion to temperature within a 1-um-thick Au sample for commonly used 25-um and 75-um-thick Kapton-B (polyimide) ablators over a range of laser irradiances. Heating, sufficient to melt Au (Delta T ~ 1050 K), is observed for 25-um-thick polyimide ablators, which is significantly enhanced by the addition of a 0.2-um Al flash coating. In contrast, heating is strongly suppressed for 75-um-thick, non-Al-coated Kapton-B, with no measurable heating observed in 2.5-um-thick Ti or 1-um-thick Fe samples at laser irradiances up to 1.2 x 10^13 W/cm^2. .

</details>


### [115] [Tunable Long-Range Magnetic Exchange in Adatom-Decorated Transition Metal Dichalcogenide Monolayers: An ab initio Embedding Model Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16085)
*Xiaohang Jia,Ryan Weiss,Yusong Bai,Brenda Rubenstein*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究金属性单层过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）上磁吸附原子间的间接交换作用，以 TaS₂ 上的 Fe 吸附原子为例，发现应变可调控交换耦合，且其可调性主要源于 RKKY 介导耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程二维材料中的磁相互作用为实现可编程磁相和可重构自旋电子学功能提供了前景，而金属性单层 TMD 上磁吸附原子间的可调间接交换是实现这一目标的关键机制。

Method: 采用 DFT+U 计算研究金属性单层 TaS₂ 上两个 Fe 吸附原子在中距离区间内的交换耦合，并通过双轴应变调控；进一步建立第一性原理嵌入（embedding）模型，复现 DFT 计算的成对交换耦合，量化 RKKY 贡献，并通过受控对比确认应变可调性的来源。

Result: DFT+U 显示交换耦合随原子间距产生原子尺度振荡，且双轴应变能调控耦合强度；嵌入模型确认交换中 RKKY 贡献显著，并表明 RKKY 介导耦合是交换作用应变可调性的主要来源。

Conclusion: 结合吸附原子排布与应变可作为在金属性单层 TMD 中工程可重构磁相互作用的手段。

Abstract: Engineering magnetic interactions in two-dimensional materials offers a promising route toward programmable magnetic phases and reconfigurable spintronic functionalities. Here, we investigate magnetic adatoms on metallic monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) as a platform for tunable indirect exchange, focusing on Fe adatoms on monolayer TaS$_2$. In the intermediate-distance regime, DFT+U calculations reveal atomic-scale oscillations in the exchange coupling between two Fe adatoms and show that biaxial strain tunes the coupling. We develop a first-principles embedding model that reproduces the DFT-calculated pair exchange and establishes a substantial RKKY contribution to the exchange; controlled comparisons further identify RKKY-mediated coupling as the primary origin of the exchange's strain tunability. These results point to combining adatom arrangement with strain as a means of engineering reconfigurable magnetic interactions in metallic monolayer TMDs.

</details>


### [116] [Uncertainty Aware AFM-IR Hyperspectral Cube Reconstruction via Convolutional-Attentive Conditional Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16118)
*William F. Kent,Roberto does Reis,Gajendra Shekhawat,Emma Alexander,Vinayak P. Dravid*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种卷积-注意力条件神经过程（CACNP），用于从稀疏采样的光谱和少量单波数结构图像中快速重建AFM-IR高光谱立方体，并附带预测不确定性，从而支持自主、高通量的AFM-IR工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFM-IR能以纳米级空间分辨率绘制化学组成图，但逐点采集光谱耗时过长，限制了高光谱成像的通量。需要一种能从少量稀疏测量中快速、可靠地重建完整高光谱立方体的方法，并量化预测不确定性以指导自适应采样。

Method: 提出卷积-注意力条件神经过程（CACNP）。该模型以稀疏采样的光谱和少量在不同选定波数下获取的结构图像为条件输入，重建完整的高光谱立方体，同时输出光谱强度及其不确定性。模型完全使用由实验红外光谱合成的立方体进行训练。

Result: 在嵌入环氧树脂的聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯（PMMA）和聚苯乙烯颗粒实验数据集上验证，仅用5%的可用光谱即可实现精确重建，性能与其他方法相当。在128×128高光谱立方体上推理时间小于0.1秒，预测不确定性可用于对局部化学特征进行自适应采样。

Conclusion: CACNP推进了自主、高通量的AFM-IR工作流程，并为加速其他高光谱成像方法提供了一种通用架构。

Abstract: Atomic force microscopy-infrared spectroscopy (AFM-IR) maps chemical composition at nanoscale spatial resolution, but the time required to acquire individual spectra limits hyperspectral imaging. Here, we introduce a convolutional-attentive conditional neural process (CACNP), a neural process architecture for reconstructing AFM-IR hyperspectral cubes from sparsely sampled spectra and a small set of structure images, each acquired at a selected wavenumber. The model is trained entirely on synthetic cubes generated from experimental infrared spectra and predicts both spectral intensities and their associated uncertainty. Validation on experimental datasets of poly(methyl methacrylate) and polystyrene particles embedded in epoxy demonstrates accurate reconstruction using only 5% of the available spectra, with competitive performance when compared to other methods. Inference takes less than 0.1 s on 128 X 128 hyperspectral cubes, supporting rapid reconstruction updates, while predictive uncertainty provides a basis for adaptive sampling of localized chemical features. This approach advances autonomous, high-throughput AFM-IR workflows and offers an architecture for accelerating other hyperspectral imaging methods.

</details>


### [117] [Direct imaging of plasmonic coupling between gold nanoparticles and one-dimensional van der Waals materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16132)
*Ibrahim Tanriover,Arindom Nag,Jong-Ho Lee,Huy Hoang,Haihua Liu,Thang Pham*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了金纳米颗粒与一维范德华材料NbS3之间的等离激元耦合，通过光子诱导近场电子显微镜直接测量Au-NbS3接触处的近场，并用FDTD模拟和SERS实验加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往等离子体金属与范德华材料杂化结构的研究局限于二维层状材料，且金属/vdW界面处的近场通常只能通过远场测量间接推断，缺乏对一维vdW晶体界面近场的直接表征。

Method: 采用盐辅助气-液-固生长法在石墨烯覆盖的TEM网格上生长NbS3纳米线和纳米带；利用原子分辨率电子显微镜表征Au/NbS3界面结构；使用1030 nm光子诱导近场电子显微镜（PINEM）直接测量接触处近场；结合时域有限差分（FDTD）模拟量化耦合对颗粒-NbS3间隙、NbS3厚度和结构尺寸比的依赖；并以罗丹明6G为探针进行表面增强拉曼散射（SERS）实验。

Result: 与NbS3接触的金颗粒产生的近场强度更高、范围更广，且热点仅在入射偏振平行于颗粒-NbS3轴时被激发；FDTD模拟重现了这些现象；特定尺寸的金纳米颗粒在NbS3纳米线上使罗丹明6G的拉曼散射增强1.8-4.2倍，检测限达10^-7 M。

Conclusion: 该工作将等离子体金属/vdW杂化结构拓展到一维vdW晶体，并建立了一种将金属/vdW界面原子结构与其纳米尺度等离子体响应关联起来的多模态方法。

Abstract: Hybrids of plasmonic metals and van der Waals (vdW) materials combine electromagnetic field enhancement with the electronic properties of the vdW component, but the studies reported so far concern two-dimensional layers, and the near field at the metal/vdW interface is generally inferred from far-field measurements. Here we report the plasmonic coupling between Au nanoparticles and a one-dimensional (1D) vdW material, NbS3, and measure the near field at the Au-NbS3 contact directly. NbS3 nanowires and nanoribbons were grown by salt-assisted vapor-liquid-solid growth on graphene-covered TEM grids, and atomic-resolution electron microscopy shows atomically sharp Au/NbS3 interfaces. Photon-induced near-field electron microscopy at 1030 nm shows that an Au particle in contact with NbS3 produces a near field of higher intensity and larger extent than an isolated particle of similar size, and that the hot spot at a contact is excited only when the incident polarization is parallel to the particle-NbS3 axis. Finite-difference time-domain simulations reproduce these observations and quantify the dependence of the coupling on the particle-NbS3 gap, the NbS3 thickness, and the dimension ratio of the NbS3 structure. Size-specific Au nanoparticles on NbS3 nanowires enhance the Raman scattering of rhodamine 6G by a factor of 1.8-4.2 relative to Au nanoparticles alone, with a detection limit of 10^-7 M. These results extend plasmonic metal/vdW hybrids to 1D vdW crystals and establish a multimodal approach that correlates the atomic structure of a metal/vdW interface with its nanoscale plasmonic response.

</details>


### [118] [Humidity-modulated Ostwald Step Rule in $\rm FAPbI_3$ Martensitic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16147)
*Ping Tuo,Ju Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过基于机器学习原子间势的大规模原子模拟，阐明了水分对 FAPbI₃ 钙钛矿相变双重作用（高温制备时促进 α 相，低温服役时加速 δ 相降解）的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验已观察到水分在 FAPbI₃ 相变中的双重作用：高温制备时湿度提升钙钛矿 α 相薄膜质量，而低温服役时湿度加速 α 相降解为光惰性的六方 δ 相。但水分调控相稳定性与相变路径的微观机制一直未知，亟需从原子尺度揭示其机理。

Method: 采用基于从头算机器学习原子间势（ab initio machine-learned potential）驱动的大规模原子模拟，复现不同湿度下 δ→α 相变温度的实验现象，并沿 Burgers 相变路径分析中间态的结构与水分亲和性。

Result: 模拟成功复现了随湿度增大 δ→α 相变温度升高的实验趋势；发现沿 Burgers 相变路径存在一种层状亚稳中间态，该中间态对水具有强亲和性。中间态与 δ 相之间竞争性吸水行为决定了相变动力学对湿度的依赖性。

Conclusion: 阐明了水分在钙钛矿相变中的双重作用机理，证明环境物种可通过稳定亚稳中间态来重塑相变路径，为 Ostwald 分步规则提供了环境依赖的新视角。

Abstract: Experimental observations reveal a dual role of moisture in $\rm FAPbI_3$ phase transitions. During fabrication at high temperatures, humidity improves the quality of the perovskite ($α$ phase) films, whereas at low service temperatures, humidity accelerates degradation of the photoactive $α$ phase into the photo-inactive hexagonal $δ$ phase. Despite extensive experimental evidence, the microscopic mechanisms by which moisture modulates phase stability and transition pathways remain unknown. Here, using large-scale atomistic simulations powered by an ab initio machine-learned potential, we reproduce the experimentally observed increase in the $δ$-to-$α$ transition temperature under increased humidity. Our simulations reveal a layered metastable intermediate along the Burgers transition pathway that exhibits a strong affinity for water. The competing water uptake of this intermediate and the $δ$ phase governs the humidity dependence of transition kinetics. These findings elucidate the dual role of moisture in perovskite phase transitions and demonstrate how environmental species can stabilize metastable intermediates and reshape transition pathways, providing an environment-dependent perspective on Ostwald's step rule.

</details>


### [119] [Studying temperature-induced decrease of spontaneous polarization in PbTiO$_3$ from vibrational amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16266)
*Zhikai Gao,Yuanji Xu,Fuyang Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理分子动力学模拟，提出一种投影振动振幅的定量表征方法，揭示PbTiO₃中TiO₆八面体转动振动模驱动其铁电极化减小、负热膨胀及四方-立方相变的内在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PbTiO₃是一种典型的铁电极化诱导负热膨胀材料，其TiO₆八面体常用以研究极化与负热膨胀，但此前缺乏对八面体随温度各向异性畸变的定量表征。

Method: 基于第一性原理分子动力学模拟，提出一种简洁的投影振动振幅方法，定量描述原子随温度的各向异性畸变；结合势能面随振幅的变化及单个原子的受力分析，计算振动模与模式Grüneisen系数。

Result: 发现巨大的负模式Grüneisen系数主要来源于四方相沿c方向两个边界对称点上TiO₆八面体转动的不同振动模；势能面随振幅的变化表明c轴压缩促进TiO₆八面体转动；绕负膨胀轴转动的TiO₆八面体振动模可随温度升高促进自发极化减小，从而诱发PbTiO₃从四方相到立方相的相变。

Conclusion: TiO₆八面体绕负膨胀轴的转动振动模通过促进自发极化降低驱动PbTiO₃的负热膨胀与四方-立方相变，为定量理解极化诱导负热膨胀机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: PbTiO$_3$ is a typical ferroelectric polarization induced negative thermal expansion (NTE) material at a wide range of temperature. The elegant TiO$_6$ octahedron is used to study the polarization and NTE, whereas lacking its quantitative characterization. In this work, we propose a concise projection of vibrational amplitude to describe anisotropic distortion of atoms with temperature based on $ab$ $initio$ molecular dynamics simulations. We find that the huge negative mode Grüneisen coefficient is mainly from the different vibrational modes of the TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation at two boundary symmetry points in tetragonal phase along $c$ direction. The potential energy surface as a function of amplitude suggests the $c$-axis compression facilitates the TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation. We show that the vibrational mode of TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation around negative-expansion axis can promote spontaneous polarization reduction with increasing temperature, making transformation of PbTiO$_3$ from tetragonal to cubic phase occur, via the projected amplitude, potential energy surface, and forces of individual atoms.

</details>


### [120] [Optically-detected room-temperature spin-coherence from the GaV$^-$ in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16281)
*Adam D. Dodson,Tiamike Dudley,Luca Basso,Leopold Diaz,Jesse J. Lutz,Raj Patel,Matthew Markham,Jacob Henshaw,Andrew M. Mounce*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种新发现的钻石中的带负电镓空位缺陷 GaV⁻，它兼具 NV⁻ 与 IV 族空位缺陷的优点，在室温下表现出可观的自旋相干性与优良光学性能，有望成为量子传感、网络与计算的关键资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钻石中的固态量子缺陷（NV⁻ 与带负电 IV 族空位 G4V）虽前景广阔，但各有短板：NV⁻ 易受电荷噪声影响且零声子线发射效率低，G4V 则需要低温工作。因此需要寻找兼具两者优点、克服各自缺陷的新型量子缺陷。

Method: 通过聚焦离子束（FIB）注入与高温高压退火在钻石中制备 GaV⁻ 缺陷，并对其物理性质进行表征，包括室温自旋相干性、Debye-Waller 因子、辐射寿命、零场劈裂的温度依赖性及缺陷对称性等测量。

Result: GaV⁻ 实现了 NV⁻ 与 G4V 优点的结合：室温下可实现光学可检测的自旋相干（T₂(293K) = 1.06 μs），Debye-Waller 因子约 0.4，辐射寿命约 3.83 ns，零场劈裂的温度依赖性是 NV⁻ 的 16 倍，并具有受电荷噪声保护的 D₃d 对称性，且低温下性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 新发现的 GaV⁻ 凭借其室温自旋相干、高 Debye-Waller 因子、短辐射寿命、强温度敏感性及电荷噪声保护对称性，成为量子传感、量子网络和量子计算领域极具潜力的关键资源。

Abstract: Solid-state quantum defects (point crystal imperfections with optically addressable spins) are among the most promising platforms for quantum information science due to their long-lived electron and nuclear spin lifetimes. The negatively-charged nitrogen-vacancy (NV$^-$) and negatively-charged group IV vacancies (G4V's) in diamond are some the most promising quantum defects but, the NV$^-$ is highly susceptible to charge noise and has low emission from its zero-phonon line while the G4V's require low operating temperatures. Here we report the physical properties of the newly discovered negatively-charged gallium-vacancy in diamond, GaV$^-$. We find that the GaV$^-$, created through FIB implantation and high-pressure high-temperature annealing, exhibits a combination of the best properties of both the NV$^-$ and G4V's: optically-detectable spin coherence at room temperature ($T_2(293K) = 1.06 μ$s), a large Debye-Waller factor $\sim$0.4, a short radiative lifetime ($\sim$3.83 ns), strong temperature dependence of the zero-field splitting (16x that of the NV$^-$), and charge-noise-protected D$_{3d}$ symmetry. These features (along with improved performance at low temperature) position the newly discovered GaV$^-$ as a key resource for quantum sensing, networking, and computation.

</details>


### [121] [ORCHID-SAGE: Fully Reproducible CALPHAD Assessment from Evidence to Phase Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16308)
*Raymundo Arróyave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了ORCHID-SAGE标准，将CALPHAD数据库评估过程中的人类专家判断编码为正式、可复现、可自动化的协议，使得数据库不仅交付参数，还交付其构建过程的完整记录，并可通过AI智能体持续更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CALPHAD数据库的构建需要大量人类专家判断，但这些决策很少被记录，导致他人只能使用数据库而无法复现或更新它；机器虽已能自动收集数据、用机器学习势构建初版模型并依据实验优化，但将各部分整合为可靠数据库的评估过程仍依赖人类专家。作者旨在使这一评估环节也能被可复现地自动化。

Method: 作者提出ORCHID-SAGE标准，将评估背后的专家知识编码为正式协议，使评估过程交付其构建记录而非仅输出参数。在Cr-Ni体系的首次应用中，通过三种方式验证了可复现性：(1) 在第二台机器上用脚本依据记录重新生成数据库；(2) 由仅通过模型上下文协议(MCP)工作的AI智能体依据记录重新生成；(3) 仅用公开数据重新拟合，三者均得到相同预测。

Result: Cr-Ni数据库能由其记录被脚本、AI智能体以及仅基于公开数据源的重新拟合三种途径完全复现，且预测结果一致，证明该标准可使CALPHAD评估过程实现可复现与可自动化。

Conclusion: 按ORCHID-SAGE标准记录后，数据库不再需要在发表时固定不变，而可由AI智能体随着自驱动实验室(SDL)及其他来源提供的新证据不断更新，同时判断权仍保留在人类手中并留存在记录中。

Abstract: Knowledge of phase stability is the first requirement for materials design, and predicting the conditions under which new materials can be synthesized has become a key capability of self-driving laboratories (SDLs). For inorganic materials, notably alloys, these predictions come from thermodynamic databases built with the CALPHAD method. Building a database, however, requires expert judgment at many steps, and because these decisions are rarely recorded, others can use a database but cannot regenerate or update it. Machines can already collect the data, build a first model with machine-learned interatomic potentials and refine it against experiments. The assessment itself, the sequence of judgments that turns these parts into a reliable database, is still carried out by human experts. We argue that it can be automated reproducibly only if the expert knowledge behind an assessment is encoded in formal protocols, so that an assessment delivers a record of its construction and not only its parameters. We introduce ORCHID-SAGE, a standard that organizes this record into reproducible and automatable protocols. In this first attempt, applied to Cr-Ni, the database was regenerated from its record by a script on a second machine, by an AI agent working only through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and by a refit from public sources only, with the same predictions. Recorded in this way, a database need no longer be fixed at publication but can be updated by AI agents as SDLs and other sources provide new evidence, while the judgment remains with people and on the record.

</details>


### [122] [Dynamic Tunneling a Prior Phenomenon to Intermediate Resistive States in Defect Engineered Mn-SrTiO3 RRAM Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16380)
*Gowtham Velpandi,Ishaan Tushir,Durairaj Murugan,Thanumalayaperumal Thirunavukarasu,Ranjith Ramadurai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了2%Mn掺杂SrTiO3（Mn-STO）电阻式随机存取存储器（RRAM）器件。通过脉冲激光沉积（PLD）和缺陷工程，在器件中同时引入Mn3+负电荷缺陷与正电荷氧空位，使导电细丝从两电极相向生长，实现了免电形成（forming-free）的双极阻变开关，开关比超过10²、保持时间超过10³秒（读电压0.1V）。XPS证实了偏压电极附近缺陷细丝的形成，并观察到SET/RESET过程中作为中间过程的动态隧穿行为，该行为产生的电流尖峰位于中间阻态，显示出多值存储潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统RRAM需要高电压电形成（forming）工序、开关参数不稳定以及难以实现多值存储等问题。通过缺陷工程调控两种带相反电荷的缺陷（Mn3+/Ti负缺陷与正氧空位），使导电细丝从两电极自发相向形成，以实现免电形成、高开关比和良好保持特性，并探索中间阻态下的多值存储能力。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积（PLD）制备不同厚度的2%Mn掺杂SrTiO3薄膜器件；通过缺陷工程设计，使器件同时含有Mn3+（及Mn、Ti负电荷缺陷）和单/双正电荷氧空位两种相反电荷缺陷。在偏压下两电极分别生长缺陷细丝并相向延伸。利用XPS对比开关前后器件，验证偏压电极附近缺陷细丝的形成；通过电学测试分析SET/RESET过程中导电细丝形成前后出现的动态隧穿中间过程及电流尖峰现象。

Result: 该双细丝生长机制显著增强了阻变特性，实现了免电形成的稳定双极阻变开关：开关比超过10²，在0.1V低读电压下保持时间超过10³秒。XPS明确观察到开关前后偏压电极附近缺陷细丝的形成。SET区在细丝形成前、RESET区在细丝断裂后均存在动态隧穿中间过程，表现为中间阻态处微小的电流尖峰，表明器件具有多值存储潜力。不同厚度Mn-STO器件均展现出良好的保持特性，缺陷工程可控制单值/多值存储。

Conclusion: 通过Mn掺杂SrTiO3的缺陷工程，利用氧空位与不同价态Mn负缺陷的协同效应，实现了从两电极相向生长的导电细丝，获得免电形成、高开关比、长保持时间的稳定双极阻变特性。动态隧穿中间过程及其在中间阻态的电流尖峰为多值存储提供了可能，证明缺陷工程能够有效调控单值及多值存储器件的性能。

Abstract: Resistive random-access memory devices work under the principle of resistive switching from high resistive state to low resistive state under applied electric field. In this work, 2%Mn doped SrTiO3, devices were fabricated using PLD in which, the defects are engineered such that the conducting filament formation is expected to have two oppositely charged defects: a) presence of Mn3+ dopants, giving rise to a negatively charged Mn and Ti and b) single and double positive oxygen vacancies. This process of two filaments emanating from both the electrodes has shown enhancement in the resistive switching characteristics aiding in forming-free switching that exhibits over 102 as ON/OFF ratio with a memory retention beyond 103 seconds at a low read voltage of 0.1V. All devices demonstrated a stable bipolar resistive switching dominated by conduction filament formation resulting from a synergic effect of oxygen vacancies and the varying oxidation states of Mn based negative defects that propagates toward each other under bias. The formation of defect-based filaments around the bias electrode was evidently observed using XPS of the devices before and after switching. A dynamic tunnelling process is observed as an intermediate process before the conduction filament forms in the SET region and following the snapping in the RESET region. Conduction filaments being heavily doped localizes conduction pathways with a small finite tunnelling barrier gap that are instantaneously formed and ruptures during switching cycles. The dynamic tunnelling behavior gives rise to a tiny spike in current and is observed at the intermediate resistive states which are potential for multi-state usage of these devices. Overall, Mn-STO devices of varying thicknesses show promising retention features and defect engineering offers control over single and/or multi-states memory.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-reference variational states resolve stripe selection and local $d$-wave pairing in the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16430)
*Honghui Shang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用多参考神经网络变分辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（MR-nVAFQMC）研究二维Hubbard模型在U/t=8下、1/8空穴掺杂时，条纹与d波超导的竞争关系。发现半填充反相畴壁能够产生局域d波配对，但仅抑制静态条纹序不足以在条纹间建立长程相干超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维Hubbard模型中，条纹（stripe）究竟是与d波超导竞争，还是通过涨落促进配对，这一问题长期悬而未决。此前多数研究使用对配对的、无自旋对称破缺的Slater行列式，缺乏条纹自旋序的描述能力，难以分辨局域配对是条纹涨落驱动还是条纹本身固有属性。

Method: 采用多参考神经网络变分辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（MR-nVAFQMC）方法，在U/t=8下将非配对的Slater行列式按不同条纹周期和畴壁位置进行叠加，在高达32×32的周期性晶格上获得媲美领先基准的变分能量。通过在次近邻跳跃（t'/t=-0.2）下调控对角跳跃，并系统考察1/8空穴掺杂时畴壁的相干离域、参数迁移到更大晶格及深度基准检查点，分析配对与静态电荷调制的演化。

Result: 对角跳跃保持电子掺杂的反铁磁性，同时对空穴运动产生动力学阻挫，形成Yamada条纹并伴随短程连通的d波配对穹顶。在1/8空穴掺杂下，空穴在反相自旋边界上的离域超过在位库仑排斥，使纯模型的填充条纹分裂为具有强局域配对的半填充畴壁。相干离域移动的畴壁强烈抑制静态电荷调制但短程配对不变，表明局域配对是半填充畴壁的固有属性而非条纹涨落驱动。然而在相邻反铁磁畴之间，配对关联在一个条纹周期及更远处无法分辨。参数迁移与深度检查点均保持条纹自旋序但未分辨出长程配对（在允许弱超导的不确定度范围内）。

Conclusion: 半填充反相畴壁产生局域d波库珀对，而单纯抑制静态条纹序并不在所研究态中产生跨条纹的相干配对。这意味着局域配对源于畴壁本身，长程超导相干性需要条纹间配对关联的建立，而后者在本研究的状态中未被分辨。

Abstract: Whether stripes compete with $d$-wave superconductivity or promote pairing through fluctuations remains unsettled in the two-dimensional Hubbard model. Using multi-reference neural variational auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (MR-nVAFQMC) at $U/t=8$, we superpose unpaired Slater determinants across stripe periods and domain-wall positions, reaching variational energies competitive with leading benchmarks on periodic lattices up to $32\times32$ sites. For cuprate next-nearest-neighbor hopping ($t'/t=-0.2$), diagonal hopping preserves electron-doped antiferromagnetism while kinetically frustrating hole motion into Yamada stripes with a dome of short-range connected $d$-wave pairing. At $1/8$ hole doping, hole delocalization across antiphase spin boundaries outweighs on-site repulsion, splitting the filled stripes of the pure model into half-filled walls with strong local pairing. Coherently delocalizing shifted walls strongly suppresses static charge modulation yet leaves short-range pairing unchanged, showing that local pairing is intrinsic to half-filled walls rather than driven by stripe fluctuations. Across intervening antiferromagnetic domains, however, pairing correlations become unresolved at one stripe period and beyond. Both parameter transfer to larger lattices without reoptimization and deep benchmark checkpoints retain stripe spin order without resolving long-range pairing, within uncertainties allowing weak superconductivity. Half-filled antiphase walls thus generate local $d$-wave pairs, whereas suppressing static stripe order alone yields no resolved coherence across stripes in the states studied.

</details>


### [124] [Edge-scaling softening of a universal machine-learned potential: point-defect formation energies in a coronene graphene quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16528)
*Andrii Tuzhykov,Vladimir Soloviev,Arnold Kiv,Andrii Bielinskyi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以花烯（coronene）石墨烯量子点为基准，系统比较了通用机器学习原子间势 SevenNet 与自旋极化 PBE DFT 在单空位、双空位和 Stone-Wales 缺陷能量预测上的差异，发现 SevenNet 系统低估空位形成能，低估幅度随欠配位程度增加而增大，但能保持缺陷排序与对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）在高通量纳米材料筛选中被广泛用作 DFT 的高效替代，但多项报道指出这类势在高能、远离平衡构型训练数据不足时会出现系统性的“软化”现象。作者希望揭示这种软化在石墨烯量子点欠配位边缘位点（如空位具有显著分子自由基特征时）如何表现，从而为 MLIP 在缺陷筛选中的适用范围提供量化依据。

Method: 以花烯（C24H12，具 D6h 对称性、三种碳类）为基准体系，对单空位、双空位和 Stone-Wales 缺陷进行基准测试：将 SevenNet（7net-omni-i12）与自旋极化 PBE DFT（GPAW）在完整位点及键分辨的能量面上进行对比；并进一步采用受控的“匹配重建”比较方法，利用自洽化学势储库对 28 个空位与双空位构型进行双重引擎评分，统计形成能差异及其随欠配位程度和断裂边缘 C-H 键数的变化。

Result: 在该数据集中，SevenNet 低估了全部 28 个空位和双空位的形成能；平均低估幅度随各缺陷族内欠配位程度增大而上升：单空位为 0.30–0.74 eV，双空位为 0.65–1.11 eV（随断裂边缘 C-H 键数增加）。相比之下，Stone-Wales 缺陷的平均能量差仅为 0.013 eV。SevenNet 能够保持三种氢依赖重建势阱的排序，并将 D6h 等价位点的能量复现到几 meV 以内。

Conclusion: 在本文花烯基准范围内，结果支持将 MLIP 用于结构筛选和初步排序，但绝对空位能量及多势阱边缘构型仍需 DFT 验证。类内对称性扩展（within-class symmetry spread）可作为一种实用的诊断指标，用于识别对势阱敏感的弛豫行为。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are increasingly used as computationally efficient surrogates for density functional theory (DFT) in high-throughput nanomaterials screening. Systematic softening has been reported for several universal MLIPs when high-energy and far-from-equilibrium configurations are underrepresented in training data. We examine how this behavior appears at under-coordinated edge sites in graphene quantum dots (GQDs), where a vacancy can have pronounced molecular-radical character. Using coronene (C$_{24}$H$_{12}$), a hexagonal GQD with $D_{6h}$ symmetry and three carbon classes, we benchmark SevenNet (7net-omni-i12) against spin-polarized PBE DFT (GPAW) for monovacancy, divacancy, and Stone-Wales defects. Beyond the full site- and bond-resolved landscapes, we perform a controlled matched-reconstruction comparison in which 28 vacancy and divacancy configurations are scored by both engines using self-consistent chemical-potential reservoirs. In this dataset, SevenNet underestimates all 28 vacancy and divacancy formation energies, and the mean deficit increases with under-coordination within each defect family: 0.30-0.74 eV for monovacancies and 0.65-1.11 eV for divacancies as the number of broken edge C-H bonds increases. By contrast, the mean Stone-Wales energies differ by only 0.013 eV. SevenNet also preserves the ordering of three hydrogen-dependent reconstruction basins and reproduces $D_{6h}$-equivalent sites to within a few meV. The within-class symmetry spread therefore provides a practical diagnostic of basin-sensitive relaxations. Within the present coronene benchmark, these results support MLIP-based structure screening and preliminary ranking, while absolute vacancy energies and multi-basin edge configurations still require DFT validation.

</details>


### [125] [Bulk CMSX-4 Single-Crystal Builds via Laser-Directed Energy Deposition: Epitaxial Stability and Microstructure Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16535)
*Divya Nalajala,Swapnil Bhure,R. Ajithkannan,Shubhang Galagali,Abhik Choudhury*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对激光定向能量沉积（L-DED）制备镍基单晶高温合金的难题，提出将基于原位红外热反馈的动态激光功率调控与仿真引导的外部冷却系统相结合，以维持长圆柱棒状件的连续单晶外延生长，并通过热处理工艺优化实现更快的组织均匀化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L-DED 加工镍基高温合金时，单晶生长受众多加工参数耦合影响，极难维持。尤其是在长圆柱棒状几何体中，随着构建高度增加，热积累与散热条件恶化，难以保持固液界面的准稳态散热，限制了连续外延生长的高度。

Method: 本文开发了一种协同策略：一方面基于原位红外热反馈动态调节激光功率；另一方面结合仿真引导的外部冷却系统。该组合方法在增材制造过程中优化热抽取，使凝固界面维持近稳态条件，从而延长连续外延生长。同时通过电子背散射衍射（EBSD）验证单晶连续性，并减少固溶处理时间优化热处理工艺。

Result: 该策略将连续外延生长高度扩展到仅靠工艺参数控制所能达到的高度的近两倍；EBSD 证实实现了连续单晶生长；近恒定的初生枝晶间距揭示了凝固过程的稳态特性；固溶时间缩短 47%，实现更快的共晶溶解；时效后组织与经典 Bridgman 法和 L-DED 法样品呈现相似的析出相分布。

Conclusion: 通过动态激光功率调控与外部冷却系统的结合，可在长圆柱棒状件中实现近乎两倍构建高度的连续单晶生长，并保持近稳态凝固；优化的热处理工艺显著缩短固溶时间且不损害时效组织质量，验证了 L-DED 制备镍基单晶高温合金的可行性。

Abstract: Single-crystal processing of Ni-based superalloys using laser-directed energy deposition (L-DED) is extremely challenging, given the interplay of a number of processing parameters. In this work, we develop strategies to maintain single-crystal growth in long cylindrical rod geometries by combining dynamic adjustment of laser power, informed by in-situ infrared thermal feedback, with a simulation-guided external cooling system. This combined approach ensures optimal heat extraction during additive manufacturing, maintaining near-steady-state conditions at the solidification interface and extending continuous epitaxial growth to nearly twice the build height achievable through process control alone. The developed strategy results in continuous single-crystal growth, as validated by electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD). The steady-state nature of solidification is revealed through the near-constant primary dendritic arm spacing. Further, the heat-treatment is optimized by reducing the solutionizing time (by 47\%), resulting in faster eutectic dissolution. Comparison of the aged microstructures reveals a similar distribution of precipitates between the classical Bridgman- and L-DED-processed samples.

</details>


### [126] [Tailored magnetic domain-wall ratchet effects through density gradients in triangular-hole arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16735)
*Julia Herrero-Albillos,María Ángeles Laguna-Marco,María Vélez,Xabier Zubizarreta-Iriarte,Daniel Arechavaleta,Aurelio Hierro-Rodriguez,Carlos Quirós,Michael Foerster,Cristina Bran,Arantxa Fraile-Rodriguez,José Ignacio Martín*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了非晶Co₀.₈₆Zr₀.₁₄薄膜中由密度梯度三角孔阵列调控的磁畴壁（DW）动力学与形貌，发现密度梯度可与几何棘轮效应协同或竞争，从而实现正向乃至反向磁畴壁传播，并大幅降低去钉扎场的随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁畴壁传播在自旋电子学与神经形态器件中具有重要应用，但传统周期阵列中磁畴壁去钉扎存在显著的随机不可控性，且缺乏对磁畴壁传播方向实现确定性反向控制的手段。作者希望通过引入密度梯度三角孔阵列，探究几何棘轮效应与密度梯度如何协同或竞争，以实现对磁畴壁传播的确定性控制并抑制随机波动。

Method: 作者在非晶Co₀.₈₆Zr₀.₁₄薄膜上刻蚀工程化、密度梯度的三角孔阵列，利用克尔显微镜（Kerr microscopy）和高分辨XMCD-PEEM成像观测磁畴壁动力学与形貌；并将孔取向、密度梯度与局部静磁相互作用共同纳入分析，用刚性磁畴壁模型和经验标度律（同时考虑孔间距与阵列周期）来描述去钉扎场。

Result: 研究发现：在协同构型中，密度梯度增强了几何棘轮效应；在竞争构型中，两种机制相互对抗，导致反向棘轮效应，从而实验上实现了反向磁畴壁传播。此外，密度梯度阵列显著降低了周期阵列中观察到的去钉扎场随机变异性，使磁畴壁运动高度可重复。去钉扎场可由刚性磁畴壁模型及包含孔间距和阵列周期的经验标度律较好描述。

Conclusion: 工程化密度梯度是磁畴壁传播确定性控制的有效途径，为自旋电子学与神经形态器件中磁畴壁的可编程、可重复操控提供了新思路。

Abstract: We investigate domain wall (DW) dynamics and morphology in amorphous Co$_{0.86}$Zr$_{0.14}$ thin films patterned with engineered, density-graded triangular-hole arrays. Using Kerr microscopy and high-resolution XMCD-PEEM imaging, we demonstrate that DW propagation is governed by the interplay between hole orientation, density gradients, and local magnetostatic interactions. In cooperative configurations, the density gradient reinforces the geometric ratchet effect, whereas in competing configurations, the two mechanisms oppose each other, leading to an inverse ratchet and enabling experimental access to backward DW propagation. Remarkably, density-graded arrays substantially reduce the stochastic variability of depinning fields observed in periodic arrays, resulting in highly reproducible DW motion. The depinning fields are described by a rigid DW model and an empirical scaling law incorporating both inter-hole spacing and array periodicity. These results demonstrate that engineered density gradients provide an effective route to deterministic control of DW propagation, with potential applications in spintronic and neuromorphic devices.

</details>


### [127] [Large Pockels Effect in Multiaxial Ferroelectrics Induced by Polarization Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16773)
*Zhijun Jiang,Jinyang Ni,Hong Jian Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在多轴铁电体中通过极化重取向产生结构柔软性、从而获得大线性电光（Pockels）效应的新策略，并以唯象理论和第一性原理计算验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前获得大Pockels效应主要依赖相界、畴壁或应变诱导相变等与结构柔软性相关的途径，缺乏更多样、可控的实现方式。作者希望建立一种新的物理机制，以拓展大电光效应材料的工程设计空间。

Method: 发展唯象理论，分析多轴铁电体中极化与电场方位角之间的滞回关系，并指出线性电光系数在特定极化方位角处可能发散；随后对单层SnSe和块体KNbO3等多轴铁电体进行第一性原理模拟，预测极化重取向对其电光系数的显著增强作用。

Result: 理论表明多轴铁电体中极化方位角与电场呈滞回关系，且在适当极化方位角处线性电光系数可发生发散；第一性原理计算预测极化重取向可显著增强单层SnSe和块体KNbO3的电光系数，验证了该策略的合理性。

Conclusion: 极化重取向为在多轴铁电体中工程化实现大Pockels效应提供了全新途径，多轴铁电体是获得大线性电光效应的有前景平台。

Abstract: Materials with large linear electro-optic (Pockels) effect have significant potential for fabricating energy-efficient photonic devices. Previous strategies have mostly relied on structural softness---associated with phase boundary, domain wall, or strain induced phase transition---to create large Pockels effect in ferroelectrics. In this Letter, we show that polarization reorientation in multiaxial ferroelectrics may create structural softness and thus induce large Pockels effect. By developing a phenomenological theory, we find that multiaxial ferroelectrics exhibit a hysteretic relationship between the azimuth angles of the polarization and the electric field, and a possible divergence of the linear electro-optic coefficient may occur at an appropriate polarization azimuth angle. This suggests a brand-new strategy for engineering large Pockels effect in multiaxial ferroelectrics. We perform first-principles simulations on several multiaxial ferroelectrics and predict that polarization reorientation can significantly enhance the electro-optic coefficients in monolayer SnSe and bulk KNbO$_3$. This validates the soundness of our strategy and emphasizes multiaxial ferroelectrics as promising platforms toward large Pockels effect.

</details>


### [128] [Systematic Evaluation of Training Data Generation Strategies for Machine Learning-Based Molecular and Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16792)
*Minh Nghia Nguyen,Beate Paulus,Akhil S. Nair*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了用于分子与材料性质预测的多种训练数据获取策略（从随机采样到各类主动学习方法），发现策略有效性高度依赖于模型、数据集与任务的组合，但基于不确定性的采样通常表现最佳，并据此提出标注预算约束下的策略选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用于分子和材料性质预测的机器学习模型常在通过任意或通用采样策略构建的大型数据集上训练，这会导致对预测任务最相关的化学与材料空间区域被过度或不足表示。因此，如何在最小化标注样本数量的同时高效地筛选训练数据以最大化预测性能，是一个重要挑战。

Method: 作者系统比较了从随机采样到多种主动学习方法的一系列训练数据获取策略，并在不同模型类别、分子/材料表示、数据集和目标性质上评估其性能表现。

Result: 结果表明，数据获取策略的有效性强依赖于模型、数据集与预测任务之间的相互作用，但基于不确定性的采样策略总体上优于其他替代策略。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，作者为在给定标注预算条件下如何选择数据获取策略提出了实用指南，指出应依据模型、数据集与任务的组合来选择合适策略，且不确定性采样是较为稳健的默认选择。

Abstract: Machine-learning (ML) models for molecular and materials property prediction are often trained on large datasets curated through arbitrary or generic sampling strategies. Such approaches can lead to the over- or under-representation of regions of chemical and materials space that are most relevant to the prediction task. An important challenge is therefore how to efficiently curate training data to maximize predictive performance while minimising the number of labelled examples required. Here, we systematically compare training data acquisition strategies for molecular and materials property prediction, ranging from random sampling to various active learning approaches. We evaluate their performance across different model classes, representations, datasets, and target properties. Our results show that the effectiveness of data acquisition strategies depend strongly on the interplay between the model, dataset, and prediction task, although uncertainty-based sampling generally outperforms alternative strategies. Based on these findings, we derive guidelines for selecting data acquisition strategies under a given labelling budget.

</details>


### [129] [Circular phonons generate negligible magnetization in insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17222)
*C-C. Wu,M. Buzzi,A. Iudica,R. Romero,G. De Vecchi,R. Merlin,A. Cavalleri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过同时测量被驱动材料内部的偏振旋转和外部的杂散磁场，厘清了圆声子驱动下是否产生“真实”磁化的争议。结果表明，虽然SiC内部出现巨大的偏振旋转（可能对应0.3 T或4 μT的磁化，取决于假设），但外部杂散磁场的测量确定其真实磁化可忽略不计（μ₀M < 0.001 T）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆声子的光激发开启了如合成磁场、拓扑序调控、铁轴向态操控等非平衡现象，但学界对于圆声子是否伴随可观的“真实”磁化存在重大争议：探测驱动材料中偏振旋转的多项实验解释受到非平衡条件下法拉第效应解读、以及泵浦-探测实验中虚假非线性光学贡献等混淆因素干扰。

Method: 作者通过同时测量两个量来消除歧义：(1) 测量被驱动材料内部的偏振旋转；(2) 利用邻近的法拉第探测器测量材料外部的杂散磁场。实验在5 MV/cm的中红外驱动下对SiC晶体进行。

Result: 5 MV/cm中红外驱动下，SiC晶体内部观察到与先前工作类似的巨大偏振旋转；依据不同假设，这可对应高达μ₀M=0.3 T或低至4 μT的磁化。但驱动样品外部杂散磁场的测量明确确定SiC中的磁化可忽略不计（μ₀M < 0.001 T）。

Conclusion: 内部巨大偏振旋转并非源于真实磁化，其解释受到测量假设和非线性光学伪影的影响；通过外部杂散磁场测量可无歧义地判定圆声子未伴随显著的真实磁化，从而解决了该领域的争议。

Abstract: The optical excitation of circular phonons has opened up non-equilibrium phenomena with no equilibrium counterparts, ranging from the generation of synthetic magnetic fields to the control of topological order or the manipulation of ferroaxial states. In this area, significant controversy has arisen on whether sizeable "true" magnetizations accompany circular phonons. Several confounding factors cloud the interpretation of the experiments, which are based on probing polarization rotation in the driven material. These range from the interpretation of the Faraday effect in non-equilibrium conditions to spurious nonlinear optical contributions in pump probe experiments. Here we resolve these ambiguities by measuring both polarization rotation inside the driven material and the stray magnetic field generated outside it with a proximate Faraday detector. For a 5 MV/cm mid-infrared drive, we observe a colossal polarization rotation inside a driven SiC crystal, similar to what was reported in previous work. Depending on the assumptions made, this could correspond to magnetizations as high as $μ_0 M = 0.3 T$ or as low as 4 $μT$. However, when measuring the magnetic stray field outside the driven sample we unambiguously determine that the magnetization in SiC is negligibly small ($μ_0 M \lt 0.001 T$).

</details>


### [130] [SiC-BN composites with extreme thermal and environmental stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17283)
*Abhijit Biswas,Eugenio Garcia-Granados,Saman Zare,Mozhdeh Mirakhory,JungHun Park,Shuvodeep De,Jessica Reyes,Hunter B. Schonfeld,Noah A. Burke,William Riffe,Michael S. Lafferty,Sudaice Kazibwe,Maryam Amiri,Shreyasi Chattopadhyay,Mingrui Xu,Gelu Costin,Tanguy Terlier,Jianhua Li,Ching-Wu Chu,Liangzi Deng,Bharat K. Jasthi,Manoj Tripathi,Venkataramana Gadhamshetty,Gi-Dong Sim,Kathy Lu,Adri C. T. van Duin,Patrick E. Hopkins,Sanjay Sampath,Pulickel M. Ajayan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种由等质量比6H-SiC与h-BN组成的放电等离子烧结陶瓷复合材料，在极端高温氧化环境下表现出优异的稳定性与宽温域近似不变的导热性，可作为轻质、易加工的极端环境结构材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亟需在极端条件下既保持结构完整性又具备抗热震性能的陶瓷材料，以满足航天、能源等多类高温氧化应用需求。

Method: 采用放电等离子烧结制备等质量比6H-SiC/h-BN复合材料；通过高速氧燃料火焰（HVOF）在空气中进行表面温度>1500 °C的长时间加热与热循环测试；结合微观组织表征、有限元热通量分析以及ReaxFF反应力场分子动力学模拟，研究氧化、热应力与界面演化机制。

Result: 该复合材料在>1500 °C高温氧化火焰下经受长时间加热和热循环仍保持稳定，而纯6H-SiC与h-BN迅速断裂或侵蚀；表面形成多层二氧化硅保护层抑制进一步氧化；固有孔隙可缓解热应力；高温下发生界面重构并形成富含Si-N/C-B的键合网络，增强界面结合力；同时具备宽温域几乎不随温度变化的导热性。

Conclusion: 这种轻质、易加工的6H-SiC/h-BN陶瓷复合材料凭借优异的抗高温氧化、抗热震性能和稳定的导热特性，是极端环境下极具前景的结构材料。

Abstract: Ceramic materials that maintain structural integrity and resist thermal-shock under extreme conditions are critical for many applications. Here we show that spark plasma sintered composites containing equal weight fractions of hexagonal silicon carbide (6H-SiC) and boron nitride (h-BN) exhibit exceptional stability under extreme high-temperature oxidizing conditions and unusually stable temperature-independent thermal conductivity across a broad temperature range. Under high-velocity oxygen-fuel torch exposure in air, it withstands prolonged heating and thermal-cycling at surface temperatures reaching >1500 °C, whereas pure 6H-SiC and h-BN rapidly fractures or erodes. Microstructural evaluation shows the formation of a multi-phase surface silica layer that further protects the material from oxidation. Finite-element analysis heat-flux map shows that intrinsic porosity mitigates thermal stress, while ReaxFF molecular dynamics simulations reveal thermally driven interfacial reconstruction and the formation of a Si-N/C-B-rich bonding network, enhancing the interfacial cohesion at elevated temperatures. This lightweight, easily machinable ceramic composite will be a promising structural material in extreme environment applications.

</details>
