The comparison is independent of the auxiliary compatible generalized connection. Within the intrinsic geometry we derive Killing--Yano (KY) and conformal Killing--Yano (CKY) integrability identities, construct Killing-spinor-preserving operators from odd special KY and even special closed CKY forms, and obtain these forms from Hermitian bilinears of Killing spinors with imaginary Killing number. We illustrate the framework on a global Dirac structure over $S^2\times S^1$ with nontrivial $H$-flux class and rank-two anchor. Its spin connection and symmetry operators are explicit, and its Killing-spinor space is a transverse family $C^\infty(S^1,\mathbb C^2)$. Finally, intrinsic Killing sections act on Killing spinors by a Lie algebra representation. For a graph Dirac structure over $S^3$, the odd Jacobi identity of the Hermitian-current bracket fails by a phase, and adjoining a single even phase generator yields a Lie superalgebra. These results connect generalized isometries, hidden spinorial symmetries, and flux-sensitive Lie-algebroid geometry.

</details>


### [169] [Swing Surfaces in AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15854)
*Jacqueline Caminiti,Sabrina Pasterski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 AdS₃/CFT₂ 平直极限下的全息纠缠熵，厘清 Ryu-Takayanagi 面与平直空间全息中摆动面之间的关系，并给出摆动面的信息论诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平直空间全息（flat-space holography）中提出的摆动面（swing surfaces）与 AdS/CFT 中成熟的 Ryu-Takayanagi 公式之间的关系尚不清晰；作者希望厘清 RT 面在平直极限下的行为，并为摆动面找到一个基于 CFT 的信息论解释。

Method: 作者首先证明普通 Ryu-Takayanagi 公式在 AdS₃/CFT₂ 的平直极限下不存在良定义，并将其与平直极限下算符-态对应（state-operator map）的微妙之处相联系；随后构造摆动方案的 AdS₃ 提升（uplift），并通过两条互补途径发展其信息论诠释：(1) 证明在一类纯 CFT 态中，摆动面长度等于适当选取的近零（nearly null）边界区间之间的互信息，其引力解释涉及发生在 AdS₃ 体内部的散射过程；(2) 将摆动面的信息论诠释问题约化为更一般的问题，即全息中黑洞内视界的信息论诠释问题。

Result: 普通 RT 公式在平直极限下不存在良定义的极限；摆动面长度可解释为纯 CFT 态中近零边界区间之间的互信息，且其引力图像涉及体内部的散射过程；摆动面的信息论诠释可归结为黑洞内视界的信息论诠释这一更一般的问题。

Conclusion: RT 面与摆动面的关系通过 AdS₃ 提升得以建立，摆动面在信息论上对应 CFT 互信息，其深层机制与全息中黑洞内视界的信息论诠释密切相关，为平直空间全息与 AdS/CFT 之间搭建了桥梁。

Abstract: We study holographic entanglement entropy in the flat limit of AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$, and clarify the relation between Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces and the swing surfaces proposed for flat-space holography. We show that the ordinary RT formula does not admit a well-defined flat-space limit and connect this to subtleties of the state-operator map in the flat limit. We then construct an AdS$_3$ uplift of the swing prescription and develop its corresponding information-theoretic interpretation through two complementary approaches. First, we show that the swing surface length computes the mutual information between suitably chosen nearly null boundary intervals in a special class of pure CFT states. The gravity interpretation of this result involves scattering processes which take place deep inside of the AdS$_3$ bulk. Second, we reduce the problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation for swing surfaces to the more general problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation of black hole inner horizons in holography.

</details>


### [170] [Finite cyclic monodromy in BMS3 gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16823)
*Evangelos Melas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维渐近平坦 BMS 引力在含绕数、无恒定超动量的双曲 Virasoro 轨道上的诱导表示，重推稳定子并证明其在自旋双覆盖中为一条直线与偶数阶循环群的直积，由此循环因子的特征成为与连续标号独立的离散诱导标号；文章进一步分析形式光滑等变截面的限制、有限循环等变条件、庞加莱螺旋度阶梯、局部平坦绕数 BMS 几何及轨道泛函的有限单值选择规则，但未构造全无穷维轨道上的酉实现，循环扇区的量子引力解释仍属猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三维渐近平坦 BMS 引力在双曲 Virasoro 轨道（带绕数、无恒定超动量）上的诱导数据与稳定子结构，厘清离散循环标号与连续标号的关系（此处二者独立，不同于有质量 BMS 粒子中离散标号由自旋固定），并为绕数轨道上的量子引力扇区提供数学基础。

Method: 利用相关 Hill 算子的 developing map 重新推导稳定子；在自旋双覆盖中分解稳定子（直线×偶阶循环群）；通过形式光滑等变截面到全局 Lorentz 轨道的限制以及独立定义的酉全局分支，分析有限循环等变条件与有限差分方程；对类时投影动量点推导庞加莱螺旋度阶梯，构造具有双曲 Lorentz holonomy 的局部平坦绕数 BMS 几何，并用光滑实周期近视界流模型排除绕数轨道，最后得到有限单值解析轨道泛函的选择规则与闭式表达式。

Result: 证明稳定子在自旋双覆盖中为一条直线与偶数阶循环群的直积，循环因子特征构成独立离散诱导标号；在相应有限旋转对称点，截面限制给出有限循环等变条件，酉全局分支实现有限差分方程；得到类时投影动量下的庞加莱螺旋度阶梯，证明投影静止能量不是轨道不变量；光滑实周期近视界流模型排除绕数轨道；给出有限单值解析轨道泛函的选择规则与闭式。

Conclusion: 绕数双曲 Virasoro 轨道上的诱导表示的离散循环标号与连续标号相互独立，并孕生有限循环等变条件与有限差分结构；但全无穷维轨道上的酉实现尚未构造，循环扇区在量子引力中的物理解释仍是猜想。

Abstract: We investigate inducing data for three dimensional asymptotically flat BMS gravity on hyperbolic Virasoro orbits with winding, which contain no constant supermomentum, and the restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to global Lorentz orbits. Using the developing map of the associated Hill operator we rederive their stabilizer and show that, in the spin double cover, it is the direct product of a line with a cyclic group of even order. The character of the cyclic factor is therefore a discrete inducing label independent of the continuous one, unlike for the massive BMS particles, where it is fixed by the spin. At points with the corresponding finite rotational symmetry, restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to a global Lorentz orbit yields the finite cyclic equivariance condition, while an independently defined unitary global branch realizes the associated finite difference equation. At points with timelike projected momentum we derive the resulting ladder of Poincare helicities, show that the projected rest energy is not an orbit invariant, construct locally flat winding BMS geometries with hyperbolic Lorentz holonomy, prove that the smooth real periodic near horizon current model excludes these winding orbits, and obtain selection rules and closed form expressions for finite monodromy resolved orbital functionals. A unitary realization on the full infinite dimensional orbit is not constructed, and the interpretation of the cyclic sectors in quantum gravity remains conjectural.

</details>


### [171] [$α'^2$-corrections to the bosonic string from an action-level double copy map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15862)
*Eric Lescano,Agustina Pereyra Grau,Jesús A. Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在作用量层面为玻色子弦的低能有效作用量（含 Riem²、Riem³ 高阶导数修正及相应 Kalb–Ramond 耦合）构建了一个双重拷贝（double copy）描述，需引入 F³ 规范理论耦合与对色-运动学替换中结构常数→三动量张量映射的形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论有效作用量含有超越爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量的高阶曲率修正（Riem²、Riem³）及 Kalb–Ramond 耦合，作者希望将这些修正纳入双重拷贝框架，从而在规范理论与引力之间建立作用量层面的系统对应，并检验该框架是否唯一地再现玻色子弦的低能结构。

Method: 通过构造高阶导数规范理论拉格朗日量（在场重定义意义下仅由单个 F³ 耦合控制），并对色-运动学替换规则作形变——将结构常数替换为对应的三动量张量。在横向无迹规范下、允许场重定义的前提下，将规范理论与引力作用量逐项匹配，求解耦合常数与映射形变所满足的匹配条件。

Result: 证明标准替换规则下任何高阶导数规范理论算子都无法产生 Riem³ 顶角；匹配条件存在唯一解（二重根），使三阶作用量呈左右对称形式 (YM+F³)⊗(YM+F³)。该映射准确再现了玻色子弦在 α' 阶的立方 Riem H² 耦合、α'² 阶无六导数立方 Kalb–Ramond 耦合，以及无 Kalb–Ramond 场奇次幂相互作用。

Conclusion: 通过引入 F³ 耦合与对色-运动学映射的形变，可唯一地在作用量层面实现玻色子弦低能三阶修正的双重拷贝描述，且所得结构具有左右对称性并完整匹配弦论预期，表明该形变框架是刻画高阶导数引力修正的有效途径。

Abstract: The low-energy effective action of the bosonic string contains higher-derivative corrections of the form Riem$^2$ and Riem$^3$ to the Einstein--Hilbert action, together with the associated Kalb--Ramond couplings. We construct an action-level double copy description of these corrections up to cubic order in the fields. Two ingredients are required: a higher-derivative gauge-theory Lagrangian, which up to field redefinitions is controlled by a single $F^3$ coupling, and a deformation of the color-to-kinematics replacement of the structure constants by the corresponding three-momentum tensor. We show that no higher-derivative gauge-theory operator can produce the Riem$^3$ vertex with the standard replacement rule, and that the matching conditions for the gauge-theory coupling and the deformation of the map admit a unique solution, a double root, which renders the cubic action left--right symmetric, of the form $(\mathrm{YM}+F^3)\otimes(\mathrm{YM}+F^3)$. Without further input, the resulting map reproduces the cubic $\mathrm{Riem}\,H^2$ coupling of the bosonic string at order $α'$, the absence of six-derivative cubic Kalb--Ramond couplings at order $α'^2$, and the absence of interactions odd in the Kalb--Ramond field. The comparison is performed in transverse-traceless gauge, up to field redefinitions.

</details>


### [172] [Infinite spin superparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17006)
*Eugenia Boffo,Martin Cederwall*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在3、4、6、10维的超闵可夫斯基时空上，通过高阶导数超粒子作用量构造了超庞加莱代数的无限自旋表示，并利用超扭子变换推导出首次量子化谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶导数粒子模型（如连续自旋粒子）通常用于实现无限自旋表示；本文旨在将此机制推广到超对称情形，在3、4、6、10维构造超庞加莱代数的无限自旋表示，并借助超扭子变换揭示其谱结构，从而获得更高的自旋乃至无限自旋的多重态。

Method: 作者从一个定义在超闵可夫斯基背景上的高阶导数超粒子作用量出发，在3、4、6、10维中构造模型；随后执行超扭子变换，将模型映射到（超）扭子空间中，并据此分析并推导对应模型的首次量子化谱（即由约束和哈密顿量子化得到的粒子谱）。

Result: 成功得到超庞加莱代数的无限自旋表示，并利用超扭子变换推导出相应的首次量子化谱，明确给出各维度（3、4、6、10维）下无限自旋超多重态的谱内容。

Conclusion: 研究表明，高阶导数超粒子作用量与超扭子变换相结合，能够系统性地构造并刻画3、4、6、10维超庞加莱代数的无限自旋表示及其首次量子化谱，为理解高维超对称理论中的无限自旋（超）多重态提供了新途径。

Abstract: We obtain infinite spin representations of the super-Poincaré algebra from a higher-derivative superparticle action on a super-Minkowski background in 3, 4, 6 and 10 dimensions. Super-twistor transforms are performed, and used to deduce first-quantised spectra.

</details>


### [173] [Cosmological correlators and kinematic flow on complex Hartle-Hawking saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15868)
*Vikramaditya Mondal,Shinji Mukohyama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究闭合宇宙中正宇宙学常数下洛伦兹引力路径积分由复鞍点（如 Hartle-Hawking 鞍点）主导时，如何在不显式追踪体时间演化的条件下计算宇宙学关联函数及其对运动学变量平移（运动学流）的响应。以三次相互作用的共形耦合标量检验场为例，发现波函数系数可写成离散谱求和，求和项对应爱因斯坦静态宇宙中的系数，并等同于 Srivastava-Daoust 多变量超几何函数；递推关系使求和坍缩为有理函数，并给出离散运动学流及二、三、四点关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹引力路径积分在闭合宇宙中的复鞍点（Hartle-Hawking 鞍点）背景下，需要理解如何计算宇宙学关联函数，以及关联函数在运动学变量平移（运动学流）下的行为，同时希望避免显式追踪体时间演化。

Method: 以正宇宙学常数的闭合宇宙、齐性各向同性度规的 Hartle-Hawking 复鞍点为背景，研究带三次相互作用的共形耦合标量检验场。将波函数系数表达为离散谱求和（不同于平坦 FLRW 情形下的积分），求和项由爱因斯坦静态宇宙中的相应系数给出（可类比平坦时空的多胞形程序），并识别为 Srivastava-Daoust 多变量超几何函数；利用适用于任意位点数的递推关系使求和坍缩为有理函数，对单/双位点 Feynman-Witten 树图导出在有限求和集上封闭的差分方程，构造运动学变量中的离散运动学流，其变换矩阵为上三角且元素为有理数，并满足路径无关的一致性条件。

Result: 波函数系数可由离散谱求和表示，求和项对应爱因斯坦静态宇宙系数并等同于 Srivastava-Daoust 多变量超几何函数；递推关系使求和坍缩为有理函数。对单、双位点树图导出离散运动学流的差分方程，变换矩阵为上三角、元素为有理数且满足一致性条件，保证流的路径无关。最终得到二、三、四点宇宙学关联函数，并在晚期共形边界上给出四点函数的显式差分方程。

Conclusion: 在 Hartle-Hawking 复鞍点背景下，波函数系数可用离散谱求和表示并等同于多变量超几何函数，递推关系将其化简为有理函数；离散运动学流的差分方程在有限求和集上封闭，变换矩阵具有上三角结构与路径无关的一致性，由此可系统得到二、三、四点宇宙学关联函数，并在晚期共形边界上写出四点函数的显式差分方程。

Abstract: The Lorentzian gravitational path integral over homogeneous and isotropic metrics in a closed universe with positive cosmological constant is dominated by complex saddle points, such as the Hartle-Hawking saddles. We ask how to compute cosmological correlators on such complex backgrounds, and how they respond to the shifts of the kinematic variables---the kinematic flow---without tracking the bulk time evolution explicitly. We, specifically, study a conformally coupled scalar test field with a cubic interaction around these saddles. We find that the wave function coefficients can be written as discrete spectral sums with the summands containing the corresponding coefficients in the Einstein static universe, which are obtainable in analogy with the polytopes program for flat spacetime. Whereas the wave function coefficients of the flat FLRW case are given by integrals over shifted kinematics, the discrete spectrum on the three-sphere for Hartle-Hawking saddles produces sums. We identify the coefficients, which depend on the final hypersurface through a phase fixed by its size, with Srivastava-Daoust multi-variable hypergeometric functions. They satisfy recursion relations for any number of sites, under which the sums collapse into rational functions. For the one- and two-site Feynman-Witten tree diagrams, we derive difference equations in the kinematic variables which close on a finite set of sums, giving a discrete kinematic flow. The transformation matrices are upper triangular with rational elements, and satisfy a consistency condition which makes the flow path independent. We then obtain the two-, three- and four-point cosmological correlators. At the late-time conformal boundary, we derive an explicit difference equation for the four-point function.

</details>


### [174] [Twisting IKKT with branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17270)
*Fabian Hahner,Natalie M. Paquette*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在BV形式下对IKKT矩阵模型的超对称twist进行分类，发现除有限N下的两种twist外，在无限秩极限下还存在五种需要非对易背景（对应于IIB超引力中的D-膜）的新twist。这些twist与平直空间IIB超引力的所有twisting超荷等价类相对应，但轨道分层结构不同，其根源在于IIB超代数的一个更高中心扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受IKKT矩阵模型与IIB型弦理论之间猜想性关系的驱动，作者希望对矩阵模型的超对称twist进行完整分类，并澄清其与平直空间IIB超引力中twist分类的对应关系及差异来源。

Method: 作者在BV（Batalin–Vilkovisky）形式中处理IKKT模型，允许动力学与运动学超荷同时参与twist，并对无限秩极限下依赖非对易背景的twist进行分类；随后将这些twist通过经典弦场论的开闭对偶与BCOV理论（拓扑B模型的闭弦场论）的形变相联系，并利用BV形式重建Moore–Nekrasov–Shatashvili的局域化证明。

Result: 在有限N下仅存在两类twist；在无限秩下新增五类要求非对易D-膜背景的twist，共计实现平直空间IIB超引力中所有twisting超荷的等价类。轨道分层与超引力情形不同，差异归因于IIB超代数在BV处理中出现的更高中心扩张。围绕非对易背景展开可得到A-膜与B-膜乘积上的worldvolume理论，包括在涌现D3-膜上的四维BF理论。所有twist均与BCOV理论的形变相关；对具有Spin(7)对称性的其中一个twist，作者用BV形式直接重建了MNS局域化，其中辅助场完备化由二次反场项编码。

Conclusion: IKKT矩阵模型在无限秩极限下拥有丰富的超对称twist结构，超出有限N情形的两类，并与IIB超引力的twist分类在等价类层面一致、但在轨道分层上因更高中心扩张而不同。这些twist与拓扑弦的BCOV理论形变及MNS局域化密切相关，揭示了矩阵模型、IIB超引力与拓扑弦论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Motivated by the conjectural relationship between the IKKT matrix model and type IIB string theory, we classify supersymmetric twists of the matrix model, allowing for both dynamical and kinematical supercharges. Beyond the two twists available at any finite $N$, which we studied in our previous work, we find five additional classes at infinite rank which require non-commuting backgrounds associated with D-branes in IIB supergravity. Together, these twists realize representatives of every equivalence class of twisting supercharges in flat-space type IIB supergravity recently classified by the first author; however, the precise form of the orbit stratification differs. This difference is related to a higher central extension of the IIB superalgebra that appears in our treatment of the IKKT model in the BV formalism. Expanding around the non-commutative backgrounds gives worldvolume theories on products of A- and B-branes, including four-dimensional BF theory on an emergent D3-brane. We relate all these twists to deformations of BCOV theory (the closed string field theory of the topological B-model) using the open-closed duality of classical string field theory. For one of the twists with Spin$(7)$ symmetry, we also show the localization of the IKKT partition function by Moore, Nekrasov, and Shatashvili can be directly reconstructed with the BV formalism, with their auxiliary field completion encoded by quadratic antifield terms. Throughout, we provide self-contained treatments of the formal techniques we employ.

</details>


### [175] [Cosmological Dressing Rules for Massive Scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15870)
*Jonathan Gräfe,Matthias Nowinski,Ivo Sachs,Siva Pawan Venna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出德西特关联函数的Schwinger-Keldysh表述（包括调和分析与dressed平直空间关联函数两种形式）本质上是同一Sturm-Liouville问题的等价谱表示，并由此导出任意质量标量场借助Euler积分形式辅助传播子的dressing规则，使其可用平直空间图形化工具处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德西特（de Sitter）空间关联函数的Schwinger-Keldysh表述存在多种不同形式，作者希望揭示这些形式之间的深层联系，并统一为可操作的计算框架。

Method: 将调和分析与dressed平直空间关联函数识别为同一Sturm-Liouville问题的等价谱表示，并以该Sturm-Liouville问题为出发点，推导适用于任意质量标量场的dressing规则；所得辅助传播子采用Euler积分形式，从而兼容平直空间的图形化（图论/费曼图）计算工具。

Result: 成功建立了统一谱表示框架，并为任意质量标量场导出了以Euler积分为形式的辅助传播子dressing规则，使得德西特关联函数可通过平直空间图形化工具计算。

Conclusion: 不同德西特关联函数表述本质同源，可统一于Sturm-Liouville谱问题；由此导出的dressing规则为利用平直空间工具处理德西特关联函数提供了系统化途径。

Abstract: The Schwinger-Keldysh formulation of de Sitter correlation functions comes in different guises including harmonic analysis and dressed flat space correlators. We observe that these are equivalent spectral representations of the same Sturm-Liouville problem. Starting from the latter we derive dressing rules for scalar fields of arbitrary mass with auxiliary propagators in the form of Euler integrals that are accessible to flat space diagrammatic tools.

</details>


### [176] [Subsystem Flow: Quantum Error Correction, Petz Recovery, and Ergodic Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15887)
*Yidong Chen,Nima Lashkari,Kwing Lam Leung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子纠错与冯·诺依曼代数中子系统包含（子代数 N⊂M 在参考态 Ω 下）之间的对应，引入“子系统流”等工具刻画模块自治的偏离，并研究其解析延拓给出的 Petz 恢复映射与包含父哈密顿量，证明遍历性、谱离散性等性质，并猜想父哈密顿量本征谱可定义量子混沌的局域谱测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错中希望编码逻辑信息免受物理子系统擦除；而在局域多体系统中，子系统包含 N⊂M 在固定参考态下的“模块自治”恰对应存在保参考的纠错码。本文希望系统刻画偏离这一纠错机制时的动力学与谱性质，从而将纠错条件、恢复映射与量子混沌联系起。

Method: 作者定义子系统流度量偏离模块自治的程度，分块小流对应相对熵损失；引入历史代数与持久核刻画模块轨道生成与保留的可观测量；通过对子系统流做解析延拓得到 Petz 恢复映射与一族正的包含父哈密顿量，并研究其演化、不动点代数、L^2-核性下谱的离散性与耗散半群及其谱隙性质。

Result: 在一般情形下，用父哈密顿量演化可使 N 离开 M，某些情况能生成整个 M∨M'；共享循环且分离向量的包含可生成一切；因子的标准包含（如 QFT 中嵌套子区域）是遍历的；L^2-核性保证父哈密顿量谱离散；以 Petz 恢复映射重复构造的耗散半群的不动点代数为模块持久核，且在 L^2-核性下谱有隙。

Conclusion: 子系统包含的模块动力学与量子纠错深度关联：模块自治对应可纠错，偏离自治由子系统流量化，解析延拓给出恢复映射与父哈密顿量；遍历性与谱性质在因子标准包含及 L^2-核性下成立，并猜想父哈密顿量的本征值与本征矢可用于定义量子混沌的局域谱测度。

Abstract: In quantum error correction (QEC), the goal is to engineer an encoding that protects logical information against the erasure of a physical subsystem. In local many-body quantum systems, by contrast, nature provides a subsystem inclusion $\mN\subset \mM$ in a fixed reference state $\ketΩ$. Asking whether the inclusion admits a reference-preserving code that protects subsystem $\mN$ against erasure of the complement is equivalent to asking whether $\mN$ is preserved under the modular dynamics of $\mM$ in this state (modular autonomy). We define the subsystem flow, which measures the departure from this error correction regime. Away from autonomy, the infinitesimal flow quantifies relative-entropy loss, while the history algebra and persistent core describe which observables the modular orbit generates and retains. We call an inclusion ergodic when modular orbits generate everything and retain nothing. Analytic continuation of subsystem flow yields Petz recovery maps and a family of positive inclusion parent Hamiltonians. We show that, in general, evolving with these parent Hamiltonians allows $\mN$ to leave $\mM$, and in some cases, it can generate the entire $\mM\vee\mM'$. Inclusions with a common cyclic and separating vector generate everything, and standard inclusions of factors, such as those of nested subregions in QFT, are ergodic. Moreover, the $L^2$-nuclearity property implies that the spectrum of parent Hamiltonians is discrete. We define a dissipative semigroup by repeating the Petz recovery map, whose fixed-point algebra is the modular persistent core and whose spectrum is gapped under the assumption of $L^2$-nuclearity. We conjecture that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of inclusion parent Hamiltonians can be used to define local spectral measures of quantum chaos.

</details>


### [177] [Exact Infrared Triangle in Gravity with Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15921)
*Sangmin Choi,Ameya Kadhe,Prahar Mitra,Andrea Puhm*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合无质量物质的Einstein引力在红外区的普适性，证明由长程相互作用产生的新型超旋转荷守恒等价于经典对数软引力子定理的无质量极限，并给出位移记忆尾部公式。结果精确到引力耦合各阶，揭示了长程引力"穿着"（dressing）可重新求和，同时给出超旋转荷的高阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Einstein引力耦合无质量物质在红外极限下的普适性与记忆效应，特别是在存在长程相互作用时，将超旋转荷守恒同软引力子定理（对数软定理）联系起来，并推广此前针对大质量物质的工作。

Method: 在长程相互作用存在的前提下，构造并分析新型超旋转荷（superrotation charge）守恒律；将其与经典对数软引力子定理的无质量极限建立等价关系；提出位移记忆（displacement memory）尾部（tail）的计算公式；并计算超旋转荷的精确高阶修正（对引力耦合常数精确，各阶不截断）。

Result: 证明新型超旋转荷守恒等价于经典对数软引力子定理的无质量极限；给出位移记忆尾部的公式；发现长程引力穿着（dressings）可重新求和；结果对引力耦合常数精确，且得出对应Cachazo-Strominger次领头阶软引力子定理的1圈精确高阶修正。

Conclusion: 质量物质情形下已有的工作在此被以质上不同的方式推广至无质量物质：长程相互作用导致的新超旋转荷守恒、对数软定理的无质量极限、位移记忆尾部及可重新求和的长程引力穿着相互印证，且所有结果在引力耦合常数上都是精确的。

Abstract: Einstein gravity coupled to massless matter exhibits universality in the infrared. In the presence of long-range interactions we show that a novel superrotation charge conservation is equivalent to the massless limit of the classical logarithmic soft graviton theorem, and we propose a formula for the corresponding tail to the displacement memory. These results, which extend earlier work on gravity coupled to massive matter in a qualitatively different way, are exact in the gravitational coupling and reveal that long-range gravitational dressings resum. We also compute higher-order corrections to the known superrotation charge which are exact in the gravitational coupling and would correspond to a 1-loop exact correction to the Cachazo-Strominger subleading soft graviton theorem.

</details>


### [178] [Magnetic-slope crossovers in the QCD bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15929)
*Aalok Misra,Shivam S. Kushwah,Keshav Dasgupta,Charles Gale,Sangyong Jeon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文结合自顶向下的 MQGP 型 M/弦理论构造与动力学理论恒等式，提出纵向体粘滞/剪切粘滞比 ζ_∥/η_∥ 随磁场（斜率）在温度轴上两次改变符号，反转点分别位于解禁闭赝临界温度 T_0 之下和之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场如何改变夸克-胶子物质中的耗散，几乎无法从第一性原理直接约束，因为实时输运无法被格点模拟直接获取；因此需要借助弦论/动力学理论等非格点手段给出可检验的符号预言。

Method: 采用自顶向下的 MQGP 启发的 M/弦理论构造类 QCD 热等离子体；在指定单模准粒子截断下利用动力学理论恒等式，并结合现有格点状态方程数据做相容性构造；辅以自上而下的 VQCD 与手征 π 介子气体（T<T_0）及夸克-胶子等离子体（T>T_0）的相适载流子分析，以及格点声速数据的一致性检验。

Result: 提出一种对归一化不敏感的符号预言：ζ_∥/η_∥ 随磁场的斜率在 T_1<T_0<T_2 两点两次变号（较低反转点 T_1 以一个明确的 π 介子气体速率不等式为条件）；动力学理论恒等式与格点声速数据均复现同一模式；VQCD 在退禁闭相支持大温度下负斜率，但斜率交叉无法从现有 ζ_∥/s、η_∥/s 图中分辨。

Conclusion: 该工作给出关于磁场下 QCD 物质纵向耗散比斜率符号随温度两次反转的可检验预言，并展示了弦论构造、动力学理论与格点数据在定性行为上的一致性；VQCD 对高温负斜率的支持与交叉点的不可分辨性提示需要更精细的数据或计算。

Abstract: How a magnetic field alters dissipation in quark--gluon matter is almost unconstrained from first principles, since real-time transport is not directly accessible to lattice simulation. Using a top-down MQGP-inspired M/string-theory construction of a QCD-like thermal plasma, together with a kinetic-theory identity within a specified single-mode quasiparticle truncation and a compatibility construction using available lattice equation-of-state data, we propose a normalization-insensitive sign prediction that the field slope of the longitudinal bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio ζ_\parallel/η_\parallel reverses sign twice with temperature---below and above the deconfinement pseudo-critical temperature T_0, at T_1<T_0<T_2 (the lower reversal T_1 conditional on a stated pion-gas rate inequality). A kinetic-theory identity with phase-appropriate carriers, a quark--gluon plasma above T_0 and a chiral pion gas below, reproduces the same pattern, and lattice speed-of-sound data can be shown to be consistent as well. The bottom-up VQCD in the deconfined phase supports a negative slope for large temperatures; the slope crossover is not resolvable from the available ζ_\parallel/s, η_\parallel/s plots.

</details>


### [179] [Imaging the two-sided black hole interior with four-point correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15933)
*Joydeep Chakravarty*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用波包涂抹的边界四点函数，用以探测双边AdS黑洞视界后的局域物理。高频下波包沿测地线传播并在体点附近局域散射，使关联函数因子化为平坦时空类散射振幅；通过特殊的算符时间排序和一个跨热圆半周的状态依赖算符插入实现该构型，该可观测量还能探测零激波与物质壳等非平凡态，通过信号跳变"成像"黑洞内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞视界内部的物理难以从边界直接探测，作者希望构造一种能够探测双边AdS黑洞视界后局域物理、并能对内部非平凡态（如零激波、物质壳）成像的边界可观测量。

Method: 提出用波包涂抹的边界四点函数；利用非平凡的时间排序，并将一个状态依赖的算符插入沿热圆移动半周；在高频极限下波包沿测地线传播并在体点附近局域散射，使关联函数因子化为平坦时空类散射振幅。

Result: 高频边界波包瞄准内部体点时沿测地线传播并局域散射，关联函数因子化为平坦时空类散射振幅；该可观测量能探测非平凡态特征，视界后的零激波与物质壳在体点跨越它们时表现为观测信号的跳变，从而实现对黑洞内部的成像。

Conclusion: 所构造的波包涂抹四点函数是一种有效的黑洞内部探针，既能局域化提取平坦时空类散射信息，又能通过信号跳变揭示视界后零激波与物质壳等非平凡态的空间结构。

Abstract: We propose a boundary four-point function smeared with wavepackets, which probes local physics behind the horizon of a two-sided AdS black hole. At high frequencies, boundary wavepackets aimed at an interior bulk point travel along geodesics and scatter locally about the bulk point, factorizing the correlator into a flat-space like scattering amplitude. To obtain this configuration, we utilize a non-trivial time ordering of operators, and also use one state-dependent operator insertion moved halfway across the thermal circle. An interesting property of this observable is that it also probes features of nontrivial states: null shocks and matter shells behind the horizon show up as jumps in the observed signal as the bulk point crosses them, thereby imaging the black hole interior.

</details>


### [180] [Self-Force and Bremsstrahlung in Finite Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15993)
*Kyungsun Lee,Piljin Yi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性电动力学（以 Born–Infeld 理论为原型）中运动点电荷的自力与伴随的 Bremsstrahlung 辐射，其中自场与自能均为有限值。作者分别独立计算局域自力与全局辐射耗散，并证明二者相互一致；同时指出虽然非线性在渐近辐射场中趋于关闭，但其残余效应会通过运动电荷的有效多极矩表现出来，从而偏离 Maxwell 理论的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maxwell 理论中点电荷的自力已被包括多位著名物理学家在内的前人广泛研究。本文希望在 Coleman 对 Maxwell 情形清晰论述的基础上，进一步探索在自场与自能有限的非线性电动力学中同一问题如何呈现，并以 Born–Infeld 理论作为原型，考察非线性对自力与辐射的修正。

Method: 作者首先回顾 Coleman 关于 Maxwell 理论中自力的论述，然后以 Born–Infeld 理论为原型，独立计算局域自力和全局辐射耗散，比较两者以检验它们的一致性。计算中利用运动电荷的有效多极矩刻画非线性效应，并讨论文献中自力两种不同表现形式如何进入计算；附录进一步将结果推广到 Born–Infeld ModMax 情形。

Result: 结果显示，局域自力与全局辐射耗散两项独立计算相互一致。虽然非线性在渐近辐射场中会关闭（即远区辐射场还原为 Maxwell 形式），但其残余效应仍通过运动电荷的有效多极矩保留下来，从而使得 Born–Infeld 理论的结果偏离 Maxwell 理论。

Conclusion: 本文阐明了有限自场、自能的 Born–Infeld 型非线性电动力学中，点电荷自力与 Bremsstrahlung 的相互一致性，并揭示非线性残留效应经由有效多极矩影响辐射。作者还详细讨论了文献中自力的两种不同表现及其在本文计算中的体现，并以 Born–Infeld ModMax 作为推广。

Abstract: We study the self-force and the accompanying Bremsstrahlung for a moving point charge in nonlinear electrodynamics with finite self-field and self-energy. The self-force in the Maxwell theory has been the subject of many studies, often by renowned physicists. After reviewing Coleman's lucid treatise for Maxwell among them, we wish to explore the same question in the Born--Infeld theory, our prototype. We independently compute the local self-force and the global radiative dissipation, and show how the two are mutually consistent. Despite the nonlinearity turning off for the asymptotic radiative field, its residual effect survives via the effective multipole moments of a moving charge, allowing deviation from Maxwell. We close with a detailed discussion about two different manifestations of the self-force in the literature and how these enter our computation. One of the appendices explores Born--Infeld ModMax as a generalization.

</details>


### [181] [Silent Heavy Fields: An Exclusion Rule for the Cosmological Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16029)
*Shuntaro Aoki,Zhehan Qin,Masahide Yamaguchi,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在宇宙学对撞机框架下推导了一个“排除规则”，指出当 de Sitter 不变的重场扇区在单个顶点处与单个外场协变耦合时，两类费曼图的振荡信号会消失；作者用谱分解加场重定义证到所有圈阶的两点函数交换类，并通过匹配将结论推广到单圈多边形，同时首次给出化学势玩具模型中三角圈图非零的主导信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学对撞机利用原初关联函数中的振荡信号探测暴胀期间的重场，但存在“盲区”——重场可以在时间上持续演化却不在关联函数中留下信号。本文旨在弄清在何种条件下这些信号会消失，以便修正先前被认为存在的近似信号，并明确真正能观测到信号的图类。

Method: 作者首先针对重场扇区与单个外场在单顶点协变耦合的情况，利用谱分解（spectral decomposition）将重场两点函数的交换图展开，再通过场重定义（field redefinition）证明其振荡信号对任意圈阶都消失；随后将单圈多边形图的振荡贡献与第一类图进行匹配，从而把排除规则推广到这类图；最后在一个由化学势启发的玩具模型中显式计算三角圈图（triangle loop）的主导信号作为非零例子。

Result: 得到了一条普遍适用的排除规则，覆盖两类图：(1) 重场两点函数介导、与单个外场在单顶点协变耦合的交换图（对所有圈阶成立）；(2) 可通过匹配将其振荡贡献归结为第一类的单圈多边形图。据此，先前研究中落入这些类别的“近似信号”需要被重新审视；同时，作者首次给出了化学势玩具模型中三角圈图非零的主导信号。

Conclusion: 宇宙学对撞机探测存在系统性的盲区，本文的排除规则精确界定了哪些图类的振荡信号会消失，提示此前若干过程的近似信号需重新检验；同时通过给出一个非零的三角圈图信号，说明仍存在可观测的圈级振荡信号，为后续宇宙学对撞机的圈图研究与模型选择提供了明确判据。

Abstract: The cosmological collider uses oscillatory signatures in primordial correlators to probe heavy fields during inflation. This probe has blind spots, as heavy fields can keep ticking in time while remaining silent in the correlators. In this letter, we derive an exclusion rule under which cosmological collider signals vanish. The rule covers two classes of diagrams where a de Sitter invariant heavy sector couples covariantly to a single external field at one vertex. We first prove it for exchanges mediated by the two-point function of the heavy sector to all loop orders, using a spectral decomposition followed by a field redefinition. The result then extends to one-loop polygons by matching their oscillatory contributions onto those of the first class. The approximate signals of previously studied processes that fall into these classes must therefore be revisited. As a nonvanishing example, we derive the leading signal of a triangle loop for the first time in a toy model motivated by chemical potentials.

</details>


### [182] [Anti-periodic Gravitino and Dirac Effective Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16047)
*Karim Benakli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在保留U(1)_R对称性（禁止引力微子Majorana质量、允许Dirac质量）的框架下，如何用N=1超空间中的有效算符描述引力微子的Dirac质量，并通过五维最小超引力在S^1/Z_2紧化上的具体构造加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保留的U(1)_R对称性禁止引力微子获取Majorana质量，但允许Dirac质量。作者希望类比已知的Dirac规范微子质量机制，在N=1超空间中构造描述引力微子Dirac质量的有效算符，以理解这类质量的超对称结构与来源。

Method: 作者将自旋3/2双线性算符表示为规范不变的引力微子曲率超场，并通过含逆d'Alembertian的非局域核耦合；在最小五维超引力紧化于S^1/Z_2的模型（Scherk-Schwarz扭曲导致反周期边界条件）中，把物理Kaluza-Klein Dirac质量视作端点处局域源对应的曲率超场两点函数的极点；通过分离物理模与规范模，将这些传播子与一个16×16矩阵表示的二次费米子核关联起来，并在具有非零radion辅助场的固定背景上与两个N=1超空间不变量的费米子分量（含旋量收缩与归一化）进行匹配。

Result: 成功构造了描述引力微子Dirac质量的有效N=1超空间算符；在五维S^1/Z_2紧化模型中，物理KK Dirac质量表现为曲率超场两点函数的极点；16×16矩阵的各元素与两个N=1超空间不变量的费米子分量完全匹配，包括旋量收缩与归一化，且该匹配在非零radion辅助场的固定背景上完成。

Conclusion: 引力微子的Dirac质量可以通过N=1超空间中的非局域有效算符描述，该构造在五维S^1/Z_2紧化的最小超引力中得到了完整验证，费米子核与超空间不变量的对应关系精确成立，为理解超引力中Dirac质量生成机制提供了新框架。

Abstract: Introducing a preserved $U(1)_R$ symmetry forbids gravitini Majorana masses while allowing a Dirac mass. We investigate how a Dirac mass for gravitini can be described by an effective operator in $N=1$ superspace, in analogy with the well-known Dirac gaugino mass. We find that the corresponding spin-$3/2$ bilinear operator can be expressed in terms of gauge-invariant gravitino curvature superfields coupled through a nonlocal kernel involving an inverse d'Alembertian. We study this construction in minimal five-dimensional supergravity compactified on $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$. The gravitino satisfies anti-periodic boundary conditions induced by a Scherk-Schwarz twist. The physical Kaluza-Klein Dirac masses then appear as poles in the two-point functions of the curvature superfields associated with sources localized at the endpoints. Separation of physical and gauge modes relates these propagators to a quadratic fermionic kernel represented by a $16\times16$ matrix. Its elements match the fermionic components of two invariants written in $N=1$ superspace, including their spinor contractions and normalization. This matching is carried out on a fixed background with a nonzero radion auxiliary field.

</details>


### [183] [Holographic Counterterms from Restricted Weyl Symmetry in Even-Dimensional Einstein-AdS Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16187)
*Giorgos Anastasiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对称性的重整化方法，用于偶数维爱因斯坦-AdS引力：利用保持场方程渐近形式的无穷小体积Weyl变换，构造Einstein-Hilbert密度的体积协变完备化，得到任意偶数维的闭式表述，并验证其在八、十维黑洞上给出与全息重整化一致的有限壳上作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶数维AdS爱因斯坦引力的壳上作用量在径向截断趋于边界时发散，需要一套系统的重整化方案；传统全息重整化依赖具体维度的边界反项，作者希望借助一类保持场方程渐近不变的Weyl对称性，给出任意偶数维的统一、协变的构造，并与环境构造及已知结果对照。

Method: 核心方法是所谓“受限Weyl变换”：要求Einstein-Hilbert密度在参数为Σ(X)的无穷小体积Weyl变换下的变分，在去掉径向截断后，仅留下积分有限（即发散）的项以外为零。该要求诱导两类固定耦合的补偿项——Euler密度与一列协变全导数，形成递推关系；递推关系可因子化解为“平移Laplacian”的形式，从而在任意偶数维得到闭式完备化。

Result: 构造在Einstein背景上经归一化匹配后，与环境构造得到的共形不变量一致；重整化体积恒等式保证了任意偶数维欧氏AlAdS爱因斯坦几何的壳上有限性。该方法重现了4、6、8维已知的共形重整化作用量，并给出显式的10维完备化（含至□³的Laplacian幂）。对具有非共形平坦爱因斯坦视界的8维与10维AdS黑洞，所得的有限壳上作用量与全息重整化一致。

Conclusion: 基于渐近保持场方程的Weyl对称性，可以在任意偶数维对爱因斯坦-AdS引力给出闭式、协变的重整化作用量，统一了低维已知结果并推广到10维，且与全息重整化数值一致，表明该方法是一种系统且普适的重整化框架。

Abstract: We develop a symmetry-based approach to renormalization for even-dimensional Einstein-AdS gravity based on infinitesimal bulk Weyl transformations that preserve its field equations asymptotically. We determine a bulk-covariant completion of the Einstein-Hilbert density by requiring its variation under restricted Weyl transformations with parameter $Σ(X)$ to vanish modulo terms whose integrals remain finite as the radial cutoff is removed. This requirement induces two types of compensating terms with fixed couplings: the Euler density and a hierarchy of covariant total derivatives. The resulting recursive relations admit a factorized solution in terms of shifted Laplacians, yielding a closed-form completion in arbitrary even bulk dimensions. On Einstein backgrounds, these expressions agree, after matching normalizations, with those obtained through the ambient construction of conformal invariants. The associated renormalized-volume identity establishes on-shell finiteness for Euclidean AlAdS Einstein geometries in arbitrary even dimensions. The prescription reproduces the known conformally renormalized actions in four, six and eight dimensions and provides an explicit ten-dimensional completion involving powers of the Laplacian up to $\Box^3$. Applications to eight- and ten-dimensional AdS black holes with non-conformally-flat Einstein horizons yield finite on-shell actions in agreement with Holographic Renormalization.

</details>


### [184] [Discrete anomaly inflow from SymTFT of $U(1)$ global symmetry: the modified Villain resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16696)
*Napat Poovuttikul,K. Sapmee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在改进的 Villain 格点公式下，为带有反常系数 k 的 U(1) 全局对称性构造了格点 SymTFT，其中奇数 k 所需的自旋结构由格点 Majorana 费米子显式实现，并由此推导出 R/F 符号、Gu-Wen 关系及子群可规范化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从连续对称性的反常流入出发，推导超出群上同调范畴的全局反常结点数据。对于奇数 k 的 U(1) 对称性，其自旋结构（spin structure）通常需借助"玻色影子"等间接方法处理，作者希望直接用格点 Majorana 费米子显式实现这一结构，从而自然获得 R- 与 F-符号及 Gu-Wen 关系，避免经过玻色影子。

Method: 采用改进的 Villain 格点公式构造带反常系数 k 的 U(1) 对称性的格点 SymTFT；用格点 Majorana 费米子显式实现奇数 k 情形所需的自旋结构，并论证这些 Majorana 费米子恰好位于对称线的结点（即两个 U(1) 元素融合绕圈的位置）。从线上与结点的格点代数推导体（bulk）与带隙边界上的 R- 和 F-符号；限制到 Z₂ ⊂ U(1) 子群，通过映射环面（mapping torus）再现 mod-4 反常相位与费米子叠加规则。

Result: 证明了对于奇数 k，边界 F-符号无需经过玻色影子即满足 U(1) 对称缺陷的 Gu-Wen 关系；对 Z₂ 子群成功再现 mod-4 反常相位与费米子叠加规则；并作为应用确定了哪些子群 Z_d ⊂ U(1) 可以被规范，以及所得理论具有的对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向"从连续对称性的反常流入推导群上同调之外的全局反常结点数据"的第一步，展示了格点 SymTFT 框架在刻画含自旋结构的连续全局对称性及其反常结构方面的有效性。

Abstract: We construct a lattice SymTFT for a $U(1)$ global symmetry with anomaly coefficient $k$ using the modified Villain formulation, in which the spin structure required for odd $k$ is realised explicitly by lattice Majorana fermions. We demonstrate that the same Majorana fermions sit at the junctions of the symmetry lines, exactly where the fusion of two $U(1)$ elements wraps around the circle. From the lattice algebra of lines and junctions we derive the $R$- and $F$-symbols in the bulk and on the gapped boundary, and show that for odd $k$ the boundary $F$-symbol obeys the Gu-Wen relation for the $U(1)$ symmetry defect, without passing through a bosonic shadow. Restricting to $\mathbb{Z}_2\subset U(1)$, we reproduce the mod-4 anomalous phase from a mapping torus and the fermionic stacking rule. As a simple application of this SymTFT, we also determine which subgroups $\mathbb{Z}_d\subset U(1)$ can be gauged and the symmetry of the resulting theories. This is a first step towards deriving, from the anomaly inflow of a continuous symmetry, the junction data of global anomalies beyond group cohomology.

</details>


### [185] [Multiparticle residues of open-string tachyon amplitudes from DDF sewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16280)
*Dripto Biswas,Qu Cao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了临界开玻色弦中色序快子盘振幅的最大联立留数，通过求和所有物理中间态；利用DDF顶点缝合将求和化为高斯收缩，并以行列式与源二次型的系数提取求得留数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高效计算高阶弦振幅的极点留数，避免直接逐项求和物理中间态，需要把复杂的态求和转化为可系统化处理的代数/组合结构。

Method: 使用三次Del Giudice–Di Vecchia–Fubini（DDF）顶点的缝合，将物理中间态求和化为任意平面三次树的高斯收缩；每个内部能级固定一个精确的有限模截断。留数由行列式前因子乘以源中二次型指数后，在缝合变量中做系数提取得到。一致缝合约定下局部坐标标度在顶点与缝合度量间相消。四点情形通过三次局部映射与单玻色子应力张量Ward恒等式做全阶重求和，恢复Veneziano极点系数；并构造了五点双留数与六点梳状、雪花图的显式矩阵。

Result: 四点全阶重求和精确恢复Veneziano极点系数；给出五点双留数、六点comb与snowflake图的显式矩阵，并通过与独立的Koba–Nielsen展开的低阶核对验证。行列式为横玻色子的真空缝合因子。

Conclusion: 该构造将留数计算归结为行列式与高斯二次型，建立了与Grunsky算子及完整高阶Koba–Nielsen顶形式相联系的算子、坐标映射与测度恒等式，为系统计算任意高阶弦振幅极点留数提供了框架。

Abstract: We compute the maximal simultaneous residues of color-ordered tachyon disk amplitudes in the critical open bosonic string by summing all physical intermediate states. Sewing cubic Del Giudice--Di Vecchia--Fubini (DDF) vertices reduces this sum to Gaussian contractions for any planar cubic tree, with the internal levels specified independently. Each internal level fixes an exact finite mode cutoff. The residue then follows by coefficient extraction in the sewing variables from a determinant prefactor times the exponential of a quadratic form in the sources. With consistent sewing conventions, the local-coordinate scales cancel between the vertices and the sewing metric. At four points, cubic local maps and a one-boson stress-tensor Ward identity give an all-order resummation that recovers the Veneziano pole coefficients. The same construction gives explicit matrices for the five-point double residue and the six-point comb and snowflake, with low-level checks against independent Koba--Nielsen expansions. The determinant is the vacuum sewing factor of the transverse bosons. We formulate the operator, coordinate-map and measure identities needed to relate this construction to Grunsky operators and the full higher-point Koba--Nielsen top forms.

</details>


### [186] [De Sitter Gravity and Emergent Time from Fusion Matrix Product States and SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17045)
*Shinsei Ryu,Yu-ki Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个与二维德西特(dS)引力对偶的量子力学模型，用一个每步融合一个自旋的矩阵乘积态定义量子信道，并与无弦交叉的 Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev 模型的某个极限相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二维德西特引力提供一个具体的 dS/CFT 对应模型，并理解其未来边界的对称性与动力学。

Method: 构建由矩阵乘积态定义的量子信道，其最简部分是一个具有涌现 SL(2,R) 对称性的随机游走；该对称性选定一个时间变量，使游走满足 Schwarzian 作用量，从而给出 dS Jackiw-Teitelboim 引力的圆盘振幅。作者进一步在固定 dS$_2$ 背景下考虑自由无质量标量场，检验两点函数。

Result: 在 dS$_2$ 固定背景中，自由无质量标量场的 Bunch-Davies 真空两点函数与模型结果一致，且对称性匹配，从而支持该模型作为二维德西特引力的对偶描述。

Conclusion: 该构造为二维德西特引力的 dS/CFT 对应提供了一个具体模型。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-mechanical model dual to two-dimensional de Sitter (dS) gravity. It is a quantum channel, defined by a matrix product state that fuses one spin at each step, and is related to a limit of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model without chord crossings. Its simplest part is a random walk with an emergent $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ symmetry, which we identify with the symmetry of the future boundary of dS. It selects a time variable in which the walk obeys the Schwarzian action and yields the disk amplitude of dS Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity. As the simplest case, we consider free massless scalar fields in the fixed background of dS$_2$. We find the agreement of the two-point function in the Bunch-Davies vacuum and the matching of symmetry. Our construction provides a concrete model for the dS/CFT correspondence for two-dimensional de Sitter gravity.

</details>


### [187] [Thermodynamics within and across AdS Gauss--Bonnet holographic CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16364)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维 AdS 高斯-邦尼特引力中研究全息共形场论内部及不同理论（不同反常系数）之间的热竞争，利用边界第一定律导出与无量纲耦合共轭的功项，并给出跨理论共存线、克莱佩龙关系、临界指数等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究全息 CFT 在不同反常系数（高斯-邦尼特耦合）下热力学的全局竞争与相变行为，尤其是跨越不同理论（耦合区间端点）的一阶切换及其与引力相变（Hawking-Page、小-大黑洞共存）的相互作用。

Method: 在五维 AdS 高斯-邦尼特引力中，从边界第一定律推导与无量纲耦合共轭的功项；采用共同的边界时钟比较固定外源下的带电球对称鞍点；利用单位流逝圆柱时钟与零型-D 真空能，通过固定半径处的严格凹性将全局比较约化为耦合区间两端点的比较，并用推广的克莱佩龙关系联系共存线斜率与熵差。

Result: 改变外源会驱动端点 CFT 之间的一阶切换；优选耦合在共存线上跳跃，其斜率由广义克莱佩龙关系与熵差相联系；当爱因斯坦端点自身经历小-大共存时，第三个鞍点参与竞争并在跨理论边界产生拐点；外控制量的补偿变化可在有限能量移动下保持鞍点序不变。在固定 CFT 内确定了中性 Hawking-Page 线及带电小-大黑洞共存面，临界电荷在有限耦合处趋于零，熵隙以指数 1/2 关闭，临界电荷处温度扫描下热容以指数 2/3 发散。

Conclusion: 通过共同的边界热力学框架，可以统一刻画高斯-邦尼特引力中固定 CFT 内与跨 CFT 之间的热竞争；耦合作为热力学变量受外源驱动发生一阶切换，并与黑洞相变结构（临界点、临界指数）耦合，揭示了反常系数对全息 CFT 相图与临界行为的深刻影响。

Abstract: We study thermal competition within individual holographic conformal field theories (CFTs) and across theories with different anomaly coefficients in five-dimensional AdS Gauss--Bonnet gravity. We derive the work term conjugate to the dimensionless coupling from the boundary first law and use a common boundary clock to compare charged spherical saddles at fixed external source. Varying this source drives a first-order switch between the CFTs at the endpoints of the chosen coupling interval. In the unit-lapse cylinder clock with the zero-type-D vacuum energy, strict concavity at fixed radius reduces the global comparison to these two endpoints. The preferred coupling jumps across the coexistence line, whose slope is related to the entropy difference by a generalized Clapeyron relation. Where the Einstein endpoint itself undergoes small--large coexistence, a third saddle joins the competition and produces a kink in the cross-theory boundary. A compensating change of the external control preserves saddle ordering under finite energy shifts. Within fixed CFTs, we determine the neutral Hawking--Page line and the charged small--large black hole coexistence surface. The critical charge tends to zero at a finite coupling. The entropy gap closes with exponent one half, and the heat capacity diverges with exponent two thirds along a temperature sweep at the critical charge.

</details>


### [188] [Matching lattice and continuum chiral determinants at finite volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16544)
*Zhengwen Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了对平坦四环上的单代 SU(5) 格点手征规范理论，Craig 的规范不变 overlap 测度与 Hsu-Witten 的重构联络 Weyl 行列式（相位由五维 η-不变量固定）这两种复费米子权重在连续极限下一致，并给出了归一化、局部 Yang-Mills 抵消项的系数及收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点手征规范理论的两种主流费米子权重构造（Craig 规范不变 overlap 测度 vs. Hsu-Witten 重构联络 Weyl 行列式）各自的相位与模长行为迄今缺乏严格对比，需要证明二者在连续极限下的一致性，明确所需归一化与抵消项，并刻画真空展开不收敛情形下的行为。

Method: 对平坦四环上的单代 SU(5)，沿光滑有隙路径比较两种权重；扣除归一化并减掉系数可确定的局部 Yang-Mills 抵消项；在有界光滑族上假设均匀 Dirac 隙，使用 Hsu 的重构与小场比较并推广至有限振幅，得到对数变分的显式均匀收敛速率；在反射正、平领圈的重构下分析相位变分匹配及二次抵消项对数系数的轮廓依赖增强；对真空展开发散的 hypercharge 背景做数值比较并解析导出自由 overlap 真空极化系数。

Result: 沿光滑有隙路径，两种权重在连续极限下一致（在归一化与局部 Yang-Mills 抵消项处理后）；真空展开无需收敛，均匀 Dirac 隙即可；Craig 插值的重构界成立；Hsu 反射正重构下相位变分仍匹配，二次抵消项对数系数被可计算的轮廓依赖因子增强；hypercharge 背景下数值支持相位二次收敛，而模量获得对数修正项，其系数可由自由 overlap 真空极化解析给出。

Conclusion: Craig 的 overlap 权重与 Hsu-Witten 的 Weyl 行列式权重在一个 SU(5) 代的平坦四环上经归一化并减去局部 Yang-Mills 抵消项后在连续极限下一致，且各类边界条件（有界光滑族、反射正重构、发散真空展开）均给出明确的收敛速率与系数刻画。

Abstract: Recent formulations of lattice chiral gauge theories define a complex fermion weight either through Craig's gauge-invariant overlap measure or, following Hsu and Witten, through a continuum Weyl determinant on a reconstructed gauge connection, with its phase fixed by the five-dimensional $η$-invariant. For one $SU(5)$ generation on a flat four-torus, we prove that their relative weights agree in the continuum limit along smooth gapped paths, after fixing a normalization and subtracting a local Yang-Mills counterterm, whose coefficient we determine. On bounded smooth families a uniform Dirac gap suffices: the vacuum expansion need not converge. We obtain explicit uniform convergence rates for the logarithmic variations and, for Craig's interpolation, prove the reconstruction bounds that the matching requires, extending Hsu's small-field comparison to finite amplitude. For Hsu's reflection-positive reconstruction with flat collars, the phase variations still match, while the logarithmic coefficient of the quadratic counterterm is enhanced by a calculable, profile-dependent factor. In a hypercharge background on which the vacuum expansion diverges, numerical comparisons at the input gauge field support quadratic convergence of the phase, whereas the modulus acquires a logarithmic correction to quadratic scaling, whose coefficient follows analytically from the free overlap vacuum polarization.

</details>


### [189] [Accelerating solitons in Rindler space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16568)
*Huiquan Li,Jianyong Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维闵可夫斯基时空中可积的相对论非线性场论（以 sine-Gordon 模型为例）在 Rindler 空间中的行为，发现经典孤子在加速观者视角下表现为携带部分拓扑荷的孤子，并伴随与视界的能量交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积的相对论非线性场论在二维闵可夫斯基时空中具有良好性质，但加速观者（Rindler 观察者）只能看到时空的一部分，因此作者希望探究这些可积理论及其经典孤子解在 Rindler 空间中的表现与物理含义。

Method: 以经典 sine-Gordon 模型为具体对象，考察其在 Rindler 楔形区域内的孤子解：加速观者只能观测到闵可夫斯基时空中完整孤子的一部分，该部分本身构成携带部分拓扑荷的孤子。通过分析孤子运动轨迹与视界附近的能量流，研究其动力学行为。

Result: 发现 Rindler 空间中孤子解具有如下运动图像：孤子从视界入射后减速并在某点停下，随后反向加速，以趋近光速落回视界。能量可在视界处流入或流出：较高能量的孤子从视界获得能量，而较低能量的孤子则损失能量。

Conclusion: 可积性在 Rindler 空间中得以保持，且经典 sine-Gordon 孤子在加速观者视角下呈现为携带部分拓扑荷的孤子，其运动表现为从视界减速、反转并加速回视界，同时伴随与视界之间的能量交换，揭示了加速参考系下孤子与视界的非平凡相互作用。

Abstract: Relativistic nonlinear field theories that are integrable in two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime remain integrable in Rindler space. We investigate the properties of classical sine-Gordon (SG) solitons in Rindler space. Accelerating observers in each of the two Rindler wedges can see only a portion of the full soliton in Minkowski spacetime; this portion itself forms a soliton carrying a partial topological charge. The soliton solution admits the following interpretation: a soliton incoming from the horizon decelerates to stop at some point, then falls back and accelerates to the speed of light toward the horizon. Energy can flow into and out of the Rindler horizon. The higher energy soliton gains energy from the horizon while the lower one loses energy.

</details>


### [190] [Is Spin Slow in Hot QCD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16684)
*Zi-Hao Liu,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明轻夸克自旋在夸克-胶子等离子体中并不像此前预期的那样慢化，而是以输运速率 g^4T ln(1/g) 快速阻尼，几乎与矢量流一样快，从而否定了自旋可作为流体力学慢变量的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前普遍认为，在热QCD中由于螺旋度翻转被质量压低，夸克自旋弛豫十分缓慢，因此可将其提升为一个流体力学（慢）变量。作者旨在检验这一论断，探讨轻夸克自旋在高温夸克-胶子等离子体中的真实阻尼速率。

Method: 作者使用完整的领头阶碰撞算符（full leading-order collision operator）进行分析。由于空间自旋与动量锁定，守恒螺旋度的软碰撞和小角度散射能够轻易旋转自旋。此外，自旋阻尼不只由单一弛豫时间描述，而是涉及多个动理学模式的叠加，并考察了全局转动（仅贡献可逆进动）和更强耦合的影响。

Result: 结果表明轻夸克自旋以输运速率 g^4T ln(1/g) 阻尼，几乎与矢量流一样快；阻尼过程涉及多个动理学模式而非单一弛豫时间；全局转动只添加可逆的进动项；更强的耦合进一步不利于自旋慢模式的存在。

Conclusion: 在夸克-胶子等离子体中不存在缓慢的自旋流体力学模式，自旋不宜被视为独立的慢变量；先前基于质量压低螺旋度翻转的直觉并不成立。

Abstract: Promoting spin to a hydrodynamic variable requires slow relaxation, as mass-suppressed helicity flips in hot QCD suggest. Using the full leading-order collision operator, we show instead that light-quark spin damps at the transport rate $g^4T\ln(1/g)$, nearly as fast as vector currents. Since spatial spin locks to momentum, helicity-conserving soft, small-angle scatterings readily rotate it. Damping involves multiple kinetic modes beyond a single relaxation time, global rotation adds only reversible precession, and stronger coupling further disfavors a slow spin mode in quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [191] [Gauge Theory Bootstrap with varying $N_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16824)
*Rafael Cordoba,Yifei He,Martin Kruczenski,Derek Ping,Jignesh Mohanty,Emil Alegria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用规范理论 Bootstrap 方法研究颜色数 N_c 可变的渐近自由规范理论，对 3≤N_c≤12 提取散射长度、有效力程、低能常数及 σ、ρ、f_2 共振，发现 ρ 和 f_2 质量对 N_c 弱依赖且宽度随 N_c 减小（符合 1/N_c 标度），而 σ 介子质量和宽度均随 N_c 增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过将颜色数 N_c 直接作为可调微观参数引入求和规则与渐近形状因子，检验规范理论 Bootstrap 方法对渐近自由规范理论低能强耦合动力学的适用性，并探究共振态（尤其是 σ 介子）的 N_c 依赖行为。

Method: 采用规范理论 Bootstrap（Gauge Theory Bootstrap），令 N_c 进入求和规则和渐近形状因子，以直接调节该微观参数；在 3≤N_c≤12 范围内提取散射长度、有效力程、低能常数 \bar{l}_i，并获取 σ、ρ、f_2 共振的质量与宽度。

Result: 在 3≤N_c≤12 范围内得到散射长度、有效力程和低能常数；ρ 与 f_2 质量仅弱依赖 N_c，宽度随 N_c 增大而减小，符合常规 q\bar{q} 介子的 1/N_c 标度；而 σ 介子质量与宽度均随 N_c 增大而增大，与先前 unitarized χPT 分析一致。

Conclusion: 该研究为规范理论 Bootstrap 用于研究渐近自由规范理论提供了进一步检验，成功刻画了共振态对不同 N_c 的响应，并区分了常规 q\bar{q} 介子与 σ 介子的不同大 N_c 行为。

Abstract: We use the Gauge Theory Bootstrap to study asymptotically free gauge theories with a varying number of colors $N_c$. The parameter $N_c$ enters the computation through the sum rules and asymptotic form factors, allowing us to tune this microscopic parameter directly and examine the corresponding low-energy strongly coupled dynamics. For $3\le N_c\le 12$, we extract the scattering lengths, effective ranges, and low-energy constants $\bar{l}_i$, and study their $N_c$ dependence. We also obtain the $σ$, $ρ$, and $f_2$ resonances for the same range of $N_c$. The $ρ$ and $f_2$ masses depend only weakly on $N_c$, and their widths decrease as $N_c$ increases, consistent with the $1/N_c$ scaling expected for conventional $q\bar{q}$ mesons. In contrast, the $σ$ meson mass and width both increase with $N_c$, in agreement with a previous unitarized $χ$PT analysis. These results provide a further test of the Gauge Theory Bootstrap for studying asymptotically free gauge theories.

</details>


### [192] [Holographic viscous transport in scalar-coupled Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16936)
*Tao Ma,Song He,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet全息模型中，用边界推迟应力-能量张量关联函数（Kubo公式）直接提取的黏度，检验了剪切和体黏度的视界公式，结果支持未修正的Eling-Oz公式在标量依赖耦合下依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验全息等离子体剪切和体黏度的视界公式是否与直接从边界推迟关联函数（Kubo公式）得到的黏度一致，尤其在高斯-邦内（Gauss-Bonnet）耦合依赖标量场、偏离常数耦合情形下，视界保守流/熵产生公式和Eling-Oz公式是否仍有效。

Method: 采用五维Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet模型，参数对标零重子密度下的格点QCD状态方程。通过加入曲率项和标量抵消项获得UV有限的重整化关联函数，并保留对高斯-邦内耦合α的完整依赖（不使用视界守恒律或熵产生公式）求解涨落方程。分别用边界Kubo方法和视界保守流方法计算剪切与体黏度，并与未修正的Eling-Oz公式比较。

Result: 常数耦合下，视界保守流计算与边界Kubo结果在两个通道均一致。对于标量依赖耦合，两种方法都发现η/s存在极小值。体通道中，一阶α截断的保守流计算给出的ζ/s峰值高度偏低，峰温几乎不变。对两种耦合，未修正的EO公式ζ/s=(4π)^{-1}(∂φ_h/∂ln s)^2均复现边界的ζ/s结果，即即使高斯-邦内相互作用使η/s偏离1/(4π)，EO公式的前置因子1/(4π)依然保留。

Conclusion: 这些结果为视界处方提供了直接的边界基准，并表明未修正的EO公式在超越常数耦合（标量依赖耦合）情形下仍然成立，其1/(4π)前置因子不受高斯-邦内相互作用对η/s偏移的影响。

Abstract: We test horizon formulas for the shear and bulk viscosities of a holographic plasma against viscosities extracted directly from retarded stress-tensor correlators. We work in a five-dimensional Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet model calibrated to the lattice QCD equation of state at zero baryon density. UV-finite renormalized correlators are obtained by including the required curvature and scalar counterterms, and the fluctuation equations are solved while retaining the full dependence on the Gauss-Bonnet coupling $α$, without using a horizon conservation law or an entropy-production formula. For constant coupling, the horizon conserved-current calculation reproduces the boundary Kubo results in both channels. For the scalar-dependent coupling studied here, both methods find a minimum in the shear-viscosity-to-entropy-density ratio $η/s$. In the bulk channel, the conserved-current calculation truncated at first order in $α$ yields a lower peak height in $ζ/s$, with the peak temperature nearly unchanged relative to the boundary result. For both couplings, the unmodified Eling-Oz (EO) formula, $ζ/s=(4π)^{-1}(\partialφ_h/\partial\ln s)^2$, reproduces the boundary result for $ζ/s$, where $φ_h$ is the horizon value of the scalar. The prefactor $1/(4π)$ is thus retained even when the Gauss-Bonnet interaction shifts $η/s$ from this value. These results provide a direct boundary benchmark for horizon prescriptions and support the unmodified EO formula beyond constant coupling.

</details>


### [193] [Covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus, non-dissipative transport and the chiral anomaly in inhomogeneous systems with nontrivial momentum-space topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17178)
*M. A. Zubkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理、统一并拓展了非阿贝尔规范场中费米子的协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分及其在坐标空间非均匀、动量空间拓扑非平凡系统中的应用,涵盖手征分离效应、量子霍尔效应、费米面不变量、手征反常分解及非对易几何形式,并给出多项新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对非阿贝尔规范场中费米子的协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分及其相关拓扑不变量(如手征分离效应、量子霍尔效应、动量空间不变量 N3、非椭圆模型费米面不变量等)进行系统化回顾与统一,规范既有结果的符号约定、明确假设与定义,并填补若干理论空白(如反常分解的无同伦假设、重整化速度流的 Luttinger–Ward 恒等式、非椭圆模型费米面不变量的留数计算等)。

Method: 采用协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分框架,将非阿贝尔规范场中费米子的量子力学写成相空间符号(符号)与 Moyal 乘积形式;借助指标配对(index-pairing)、非对易几何、留数计算、主象征的纤维度(fibre degree)及 Luttinger–Ward 恒等式等手段,研究动量空间拓扑、费米面不变量与反常之间的关系,并在闭合四流形上分析手征反常对瞬子数 N3 与动量空间不变量 N3 的分解。

Result: 新结果包括:协变符号不依赖规范场的算符的刻画及积分反常的排序无关性;在闭合四流形上无同伦假设下,反常分解为瞬子数与 N3 的乘积在"未扭转指标"为零时精确成立;重整化速度流背后的 Luttinger–Ward 恒等式及非重整化定理中替换布里渊区紧性的连续条件;手征分离效应的 Moyal 型 N3 与主象征的纤维度相等;非椭圆模型费米面不变量的留数求值(连续与格点情形)及其蕴含的守恒荷沿磁场的平衡流(非哈密顿形式允许的手征磁效应);球形费米面模型以手征分离效应和单外尔反常换取该效应,经修改后可在付出反常代价下恢复该效应;以及手征分离不变量的指标配对形式。

Conclusion: 协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分为理解非均匀系统与动量空间拓扑提供了统一而有力的框架;反常、瞬子数与动量空间不变量 N3 之间在适当拓扑条件下存在精确的分解关系;非椭圆模型的非哈密顿形式可支持手征磁效应等非平凡输运现象,其行为通过费米面不变量与手征分离不变量的指标配对形式得到系统刻画。

Abstract: We review, systematize and extend the covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus for fermions in non-Abelian gauge fields and its applications to systems inhomogeneous in coordinate space with nontrivial momentum-space topology: the non-Abelian chiral separation and quantum Hall effects, their stability under interactions, the Fermi-surface invariant of inhomogeneous systems and the non-elliptic models that carry it, with their equilibrium chiral magnetic effect, the factorization of the chiral anomaly into the instanton number and the momentum-space invariant $N_3$, and the noncommutative-geometric form of the invariants. Previous results are brought to common conventions, with hypotheses stated and notions defined. New results: the characterization of the operators whose covariant symbol is gauge-field independent and the ordering independence of the integrated anomaly; the anomaly factorization on a closed four-manifold without the homotopy hypothesis, the factorization into instanton number and $N_3$ holding exactly when the untwisted index vanishes; the Luttinger--Ward identity behind the renormalized-velocity current and the continuum conditions replacing the compactness of the Brillouin zone in the non-renormalization theorems; the identification of the Moyal $N_3$ of the chiral separation effect with the fibre degree of the principal symbol; the residue evaluation of the Fermi-surface invariant of the non-elliptic models, in the continuum and on a lattice, and the equilibrium current of the conserved charge along the magnetic field that it implies -- a chiral magnetic effect permitted by the non-Hamiltonian form of these models -- which the model with spherical Fermi surfaces trades for a chiral separation effect and a single-Weyl anomaly, and a modification of it restores at the price of the anomaly; and the index-pairing form of the chiral separation invariant.

</details>


### [194] [Love numbers for the Myers-Perry geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17261)
*Rhucha Deshpande,Oleg Lunin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在任意维度的Myers-Perry几何中分析标量场的Love数，指出潮汐响应由两个常数刻画：一个描述潜在对数跑动，另一个为Love数；并论证跑动参数对黑洞视界结构不敏感，同时用多种近似方法计算了依赖视界边界条件的Love数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞潮汐响应（Love数）可揭示黑洞内部结构与引力的本质，但标量场在Myers-Perry黑洞（高维旋转黑洞）中的Love数及对数跑动行为的普适性尚未充分厘清。作者希望区分并理解两类参数——对视界结构不敏感的跑动参数与依赖边界条件的Love数。

Method: 在任意维度Myers-Perry几何中研究标量场的波动方程：利用波方程的渐近展开显式计算描述对数跑动的参数；Love数因依赖视界处边界条件，采用若干种不同的近似方法分别计算。

Result: 标量场的潮汐响应由两个常数完全指定：一个刻画潜在的对数跑动，另一个即Love数。跑动参数对视界结构不敏感，并已在任意维度的Myers-Perry黑洞中显式算出；Love数则依赖视界边界条件的具体细节，并通过多种近似方法获得。

Conclusion: 该工作区分了标量场潮汐响应中对数跑动参数与Love数两种独立量：前者具有普适性、可解析显式计算；后者与视界边界条件密切相关，需借助不同近似处理，为理解高维旋转黑洞的潮汐响应提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: We analyze Love numbers for scalar fields in the Myers-Perry geometry in arbitrary dimensions. The tidal responses of black holes are specified by two constants which encode a potential logarithmic running and the Love number. We argue that the parameter specifying the running is not sensitive to the structure of the horizon, and we explicitly compute this parameter for Myers-Perry black holes in arbitrary dimensions by utilizing the asymptotic expansion of the wave equation. The Love numbers rely on the details of the boundary conditions at the horizon, and we compute them using several distinct approximations.

</details>


### [195] [A precision bound for gravitational transseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17274)
*Ji Hoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出全息可观测量的透明级数存在由D-膜瞬子数据设定的精度界，并发展大N resurgence方法以捕捉引力鞍点的 $e^{-N^2}$ 渐近；通过研究 $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS 指标验证了热AdS与黑洞上D-膜瞬子数据之间的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞阈值以上的全息可观测量，其透明级数在有限N下能多大程度确定可观测量的极限，并揭示传统 $e^{-N}$ 瞬子之外的 $e^{-N^2}$ 渐近结构。

Method: 基于Écalle的工作发展大N resurgence方法，捕捉引力鞍点超越 $e^{-N}$ 瞬子的 $e^{-N^2}$ 渐近；在主例 $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS 指标中，推导热AdS与黑洞背景上D-膜瞬子数据之间的对偶关系（交换两种背景上的D-膜瞬子数目）。

Result: 在黑洞相中预期的D-膜瞬子和增长假设下，发现透明级数重求和存在解析障碍，该障碍设定了精度界；在指标中，对偶关系再现了缠绕黑洞的D3-膜作用量。

Conclusion: 大N透明级数受到D-膜瞬子数据设定的精度界约束，这一机制有望推广到一般情形，并与双锥虫洞相关。

Abstract: We propose that the large $N$ transseries of a holographic observable above the black hole threshold admits a precision bound, a limit on how precisely the transseries can determine the observable at finite $N$, set by the data of its D-brane instanton sectors. In doing so, we develop methods in large $N$ resurgence, based on the work of Écalle, that capture the $e^{-N^2}$ asymptotics of gravitational saddles beyond the $e^{-N}$ instantons. Under an assumption on the growth of the D-brane instanton sums that we expect in the black hole phase, we find an analytic obstruction to the resummation of the large $N$ transseries. This obstruction sets the precision bound. As our main example and consistency check, we study the $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills and its black hole. We derive relations between the D-brane instanton data on thermal AdS and on the black hole, in the form of a duality that exchanges the numbers of D-brane instantons on the two backgrounds. In the index, these relations reproduce the actions of D3-branes wrapped on the black hole. We conclude with our expectations for generic situations and their relation to the double-cone wormhole.

</details>


### [196] [The $(5,5)$ String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17275)
*Justin Kaidi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究一种 N=(2,1) 异质弦，其右移子在 R^{2,2} 上、左移子具有 (5,5) 符号差，并伴随格 II_{3,3}。该弦无需零流，目标空间保持四维，所有态（含十六个超荷的无穷塔）均无质量；作者提出其经典目标理论是该超代数上的自对偶规范理论，是一个纯三次的闭弦全模场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 N=(2,1) 弦需要引入零流（null current），且其物理态结构与目标空间解释不够自然。本文旨在构造一个无需零流、目标空间仍为四维的变体，并探究其全部无质量态能否统一地由某个规范代数（含引力）描述，从而走向“纯三次”的闭弦场论。

Method: 通过将左移子格取为 II_{3,3}（符号差 (5,5)）构造 N=(2,1) 异质弦；对中性玻色子，将 Kutasov-Martinec 行列式作用量对偶化为自对偶度规与自对偶 Maxwell 场的三次作用量，并用单个超场补全费米子；对带电态，提出其耦合常数由左移子物理态构成的李超代数的结构常数给出。

Result: 该弦所有态构成具有十六个超荷的无穷塔且全部无质量；中性玻色子部分可对偶为自对偶度规耦合自对偶 Maxwell 场的三次作用量，并可经单个超场包含费米子；带电态耦合与左移子物理态李超代数的结构常数一致的证据成立，从而支持目标理论为自对偶规范理论。

Conclusion: 作者提出该弦的经典目标理论是左移子物理态所成李超代数上的自对偶规范理论：弦的每个态都是一个规范场，引力也属于该规范代数。这将是一个仅含三次项、涵盖闭弦全部模式的场论，统一了引力、规范场与物质场。

Abstract: We study an $N=(2,1)$ heterotic string with right-movers on $\mathbb R^{2,2}$ and left-movers of signature $(5,5)$, obtained by adjoining the lattice $\mathrm{II}_{3,3}$. Unlike the standard $N=(2,1)$ strings it requires no null current, and its target space remains four-dimensional. All of its states, an infinite tower with sixteen supercharges, are massless. For the neutral bosons, the Kutasov-Martinec determinant action is dual to a cubic action for a self-dual metric coupled to self-dual Maxwell fields, and we complete it with fermions using a single superfield. For the charged states we give evidence that the couplings are the structure constants of a Lie superalgebra formed by the physical states of the left-movers. We are thus led to propose that the classical target theory is a self-dual gauge theory for this superalgebra: every state of the string is a gauge field, and gravity is part of the gauge algebra. This would be a purely cubic field theory of all the modes of a closed string.

</details>


### [197] [Bound States of Two D0-Branes and the Three-Graviton Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17325)
*Sergio Sanjurjo,Themistocles Zikopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 作者数值求解了M理论的SU(2) BFSS矩阵模型基态，对应两个D0膜（或沿M理论圆带两单位动量的11维引力子）的零能束缚态，数值再现了三引力子耦合，并论证其受超对称保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证BFSS猜想中双D0膜零能束缚态的存在性及其波函数结构，并通过数值解精确验证此前解析计算的三引力子耦合，同时阐明该耦合的超对称保护机制。

Method: 对SU(2) BFSS矩阵模型进行数值求解基态波函数；分析波函数的强耦合核心与渐近幂律尾部；从矩阵模型中未重整化的一圈双D0膜相互作用推导三引力子耦合值；并利用表示论论证新提出的激发束缚态。

Result: 数值解表明波函数具有强耦合核心及D0膜远离时的渐近幂律尾部；幂律衰减系数与Herderschee和Maldacena此前计算的三引力子耦合高度吻合；进一步证明该耦合在矩阵模型中受超对称保护。

Conclusion: BFSS矩阵模型的SU(2)基态即双D0膜零能束缚态，其渐近行为编码三引力子耦合，该耦合受超对称保护且数值结果与解析值一致；并对新提出的激发束缚态给出表示论层面的简明论证。

Abstract: We numerically solve for the ground state of the $SU(2)$ BFSS matrix model for M-theory. This state corresponds to the zero-energy bound state of two D0-branes or, by the BFSS conjecture, to the eleven-dimensional graviton with two units of momentum along the M-theory circle. The wavefunction exhibits a strongly coupled core, as well as an asymptotic power-law tail where the D0-branes are well separated. The coefficient of this power-law decay is the three-graviton coupling, previously computed by Herderschee and Maldacena, which our numerical solution reproduces with high accuracy. We also show that this coupling is protected by supersymmetry in the matrix model, and derive its value from the non-renormalized one-loop interaction of the two D0-branes. Lastly, we provide a simple representation-theoretic argument regarding the recently proposed excited bound states of the model.

</details>


### [198] [Decoupling Dynamics: A Tale of Two Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17327)
*Andrea Antinucci,Oscar Lewis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文证明了对连续阿贝尔对称性做动力学规范（dynamical gauging）前后的两种 SymTFT，是同一个 (d+1) 维动力学全息规范理论的不同拓扑极限；规范操作改变了该理论的边界条件，从而选出不同的拓扑极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SymTFT 已知能刻画 d 维 QFT 的对称性，并在有限对称性规范下保持不变，但在连续对称性动力学规范下会改变。此前缺乏对这一现象的全量子、逐 Hilbert 空间层面的解释：为什么规范后 SymTFT 会变化、以及拓扑缺陷、荷与反常如何在丢弃电流动力学信息后保留下来。

Method: 作者将一个 (d+1) 维的动力学全息规范理论置于不同的拓扑极限下，用规范操作切换其边界条件来选取不同极限；结合体全息理论极限与"光子截断"（photon truncation）给出 SymTFT 在 Hilbert 空间与算子层面的全量子定义，并对 (2+1) 维体理论做了完整量子分析，涵盖非反常与反常情形。

Result: 揭示了规范前后 SymTFT 的差异源于同一个体动力学规范理论的不同拓扑极限；光子截断过程解释了拓扑对称缺陷、荷与反常得以保留，而电流所载的动力学信息被丢弃。对四维边界带混合轴矢量-矢量反常的理论，成功恢复了规范矢量对称后剩余的非可逆手征对称性所对应的 SymTFT。

Conclusion: 连续阿贝尔对称性的动力学规范改变边界 SymTFT，可统一理解为同一个高维动力学全息规范理论在规范操作切换边界条件下的不同拓扑极限；该构造为 SymTFT 的量子定义及规范前后对称结构如何变化提供了清晰、自洽的框架。

Abstract: The symmetries of a $d$-dimensional QFT are known to be captured by a topological theory in one dimension higher, the Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT), which is invariant under gauging finite symmetries of the QFT, but not under dynamical gauging of continuous symmetries. We show that the distinct SymTFTs of a $d$-dimensional QFT before and after dynamical gauging of a continuous Abelian symmetry arise as different topological limits of the same dynamical holographic gauge theory in $(d+1)$-dimensions. Gauging changes the boundary condition of this dynamical theory and thereby selects a different topological limit, explaining why the associated SymTFT changes. We give a fully quantum definition of these SymTFTs at the level of Hilbert spaces and operators by combining suitable limits of the bulk holographic theory with photon truncation. This construction clarifies how topological symmetry defects, charges, and anomalies survive while the dynamical information carried by currents is discarded. We study non-anomalous and anomalous examples, providing a complete quantum analysis for $(2+1)$-dimensional bulks. For a four-dimensional boundary theory with a mixed axial-vector anomaly, we recover the SymTFT of the non-invertible chiral symmetry that remains after gauging the vector symmetry.

</details>
